9 月 27 日你自己说的重点 —— 直接从这三个开始：
脂肪酸合成 · 甘油三酯与磷脂 · 胆固醇（你说「脂质合成有点模糊」）
维生素与辅酶 · 谁搬什么（你说「完全没碰」）
磷酸戊糖 · 糖异生 · 糖原 · 酮体 · 尿素循环（除三大以外的代谢）

同物异名 · 考官换个名字问，你要认得出是同一个

代谢

  • Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions = reductive pentose phosphate cycle（卡尔文循环 = 暗反应）· "dark" 是误导：白天照跑，只是不直接用光。carbon fixation 是它干的事（第一步 rubisco），不算同义词
  • citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle（柠檬酸循环 = 三羧酸循环）
  • glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof(–Parnas) pathway（糖酵解）
  • pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway（磷酸戊糖途径）
  • respiratory chain = electron transport chain（呼吸链 = 电子传递链）
  • β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation（β-氧化）· lipid metabolism 是总称，β-氧化只是其中一条分解路
  • urea cycle = ornithine cycle = Krebs–Henseleit cycle（尿素循环 = 鸟氨酸循环）
  • Cori cycle = lactic acid cycle · glucose–alanine cycle = Cahill cycle
  • Complex I = NADH dehydrogenase · II = succinate dehydrogenase · III = cytochrome bc₁ complex · IV = cytochrome c oxidase · V = ATP synthase = F₀F₁-ATPase
  • ubiquinone = coenzyme Q = CoQ（泛醌）
  • carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1
  • triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat（甘油三酯）· phosphatidylcholine = lecithin · phosphatidylethanolamine = cephalin
  • rubisco = ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate carboxylase/oxygenase
  • GAP = glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate = G3P · fructose-1,6-bisphosphate（旧称 diphosphate）· 2,3-BPG = 2,3-DPG
  • -ate = -ic acid：pyruvate = pyruvic acid · lactate = lactic acid · malate = malic acid（生理 pH 下存在的是 -ate 那个）
  • TPP = thiamine pyrophosphate = thiamine diphosphate
  • 维生素：B₁ thiamine · B₂ riboflavin · B₃ niacin（nicotinic acid / nicotinamide）· B₅ pantothenic acid · B₆ pyridoxine · B₇ biotin = vitamin H · B₉ folate = folic acid · B₁₂ cobalamin · C ascorbic acid · A retinol · D calciferol（D₃ cholecalciferol）· E tocopherol · K phylloquinone

酶与蛋白

  • Km = Michaelis constant · kcat = turnover number · kcat/Km = specificity constant = catalytic efficiency
  • isoenzyme = isozyme · zymogen = proenzyme · propeptide = prosequence
  • apoenzyme ＋ cofactor = holoenzyme · prosthetic group = 结合得很紧的 cofactor
  • allosteric enzyme = regulatory enzyme
  • peptide bond = amide bond（肽键）
  • β-sheet = β-pleated sheet · β-turn = reverse turn = β-bend
  • 血红蛋白 T state = tense = deoxy · R state = relaxed = oxy
  • chaperone：很多就是 heat shock proteins（Hsp）
  • signal peptide = signal sequence ≠ trp 操纵子的 leader sequence（同名不同物）

核酸与中心法则

  • coding strand = sense strand = non-template strand · template strand = antisense strand = non-coding strand
  • −10 box = Pribnow box（TATAAT）· TATA box = Goldberg–Hogness box
  • Shine–Dalgarno sequence = ribosome binding site（RBS）
  • start codon = initiation codon（AUG）· stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon（UAA ochre · UAG amber · UGA opal）
  • intron = intervening sequence（内含子）
  • CAP = CRP（catabolite activator protein = cAMP receptor protein）
  • bacterial topoisomerase II = DNA gyrase · primase = DnaG · helicase = DnaB
  • EF-Tu ↔ 真核 eEF1A · EF-G ↔ 真核 eEF2
  • Tm = melting temperature · inverted repeat = palindrome

实验与 PESB

  • Western blot = immunoblot · SDS-PAGE = denaturing PAGE
  • size-exclusion chromatography = gel filtration = gel permeation chromatography（分子筛）
  • IMAC ⊃ Ni-NTA（Ni-NTA 是 IMAC 的一种，His-tag 用的就是它）
  • Beer–Lambert law = Beer's law · OD₆₀₀ = optical density at 600 nm（菌液的浑浊，测的是散射不是吸收）
  • qPCR = real-time PCR ≠ RT-PCR（reverse transcription PCR）—— 最常见的陷阱
  • CFPS = cell-free protein synthesis = in vitro transcription–translation（TX-TL）
  • directed evolution = in vitro evolution · folding landscape = energy landscape = folding funnel

病毒

  • virion = virus particle · bacteriophage = phage · capsid = protein coat · nucleocapsid = capsid ＋ 核酸 · prophage = 整合进宿主基因组的噬菌体（lysogenic）

Structure & properties

Protein structure

卡 01 · 四层结构，每层靠什么键

Must say

Four levels. Primary is the covalent level, the order of residues. Secondary is held by main-chain hydrogen bonds only, independent of the side chains. Tertiary is held mainly by the hydrophobic effect. Quaternary is several chains, all non-covalent.

二级只靠主链自己的氢键，跟侧链无关——这一句是分水岭。

Numbers

α-helix: 3.6 residues per turn, rise 0.54 nm, hydrogen bond from C=O to the N–H of i+4. Trp and Tyr absorb at 280 nm.

反平行 β 折叠（= β-sheet = β-pleated sheet）比平行的稳（氢键正对着）。

Hook

Denaturation destroys the higher structure but not the primary structure. Sickle-cell is one residue: Glu → Val at position 6 of the β chain.

Say this

A protein has four levels of structure, and the useful way to remember them is by what holds each one together. The primary structure is the covalent level: the order of residues joined by peptide bonds (also called amide bond), including the disulfide positions. The secondary structure is local, and it is held by hydrogen bonds of the main chain only — the side chains take no part. The tertiary structure is the fold of one whole chain, held mainly by the hydrophobic effect, with ionic bonds, hydrogen bonds and disulfides as well. The quaternary structure is several chains together, and it is entirely non-covalent. ⚠ Denaturation destroys everything above the primary level, so the protein loses its activity, but the peptide bonds are not broken.

约 40 秒 · 「按什么键把它们绑住」是这一段的骨架

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hudeček、Martínek

① 肽键有部分双键性质，所以是平面的、不能旋转，几乎都是反式 partial double bond · planar · trans

The peptide bond has partial double-bond character, so it is planar and cannot rotate, and it is almost always trans, with the two α-carbons on opposite sides; the cis form occurs practically only before proline.

一级结构里的键就是它。

② 二硫键是两个半胱氨酸之间的共价键，在内质网里由氧化形成 disulfide bond

The disulfide bond, the second covalent bond in proteins, is an S–S bond between two cysteines, formed by oxidation in the endoplasmic reticulum; it joins the A and B chains of insulin, and it is not formed in the cytosol, which is reducing.

三级结构里唯一的共价键。

③ 氢键是 N–H 或 O–H 的氢和另一个 N 或 O 的孤对电子之间的吸引，有方向性 hydrogen bond

The hydrogen bond, the first of the non-covalent forces, is an attraction between a hydrogen on nitrogen or oxygen and a lone pair on another nitrogen or oxygen; it has a fixed direction, which is why it can hold the α-helix and β-sheet (also called β-pleated sheet) in shape.

C–H 不能形成氢键。

④ 盐桥是带负电和带正电的侧链之间的吸引，pH 改变电荷时就消失 salt bridge · ionic bond

The salt bridge, the second non-covalent force, is the attraction between a negative side chain, aspartate or glutamate, and a positive one, lysine or arginine; it disappears when the pH removes either charge.

所以强酸强碱能使蛋白变性。

⑤ 范德华力存在于任何相互靠近的原子之间，单个很弱，但数量很多 van der Waals

The van der Waals attraction, the third, acts between any atoms in close contact; each one is very weak, but the tightly packed interior of a protein contains a very large number of them.

没有方向性，决定内部堆积得多紧。

⑥ 疏水效应不是键，而是非极性基团聚在一起时水的熵增加 hydrophobic effect

The hydrophobic effect, by contrast, is not a bond: water molecules are ordered around nonpolar side chains, and when those side chains come together the water is released and the entropy of the water increases; this is the main force that folds the chain.

说出「不是键」是加分。

⑦ 加热、尿素、强酸强碱、去污剂破坏二、三、四级结构，但一级结构不变 denaturation

All these non-covalent forces are broken by heat, urea, strong acid or base, and detergents, and β-mercaptoethanol reduces the disulfides; this is denaturation, and the primary structure stays because no peptide bond is broken.

煮鸡蛋不是水解。

⑧ 书外：Anfinsen 让变性的核糖核酸酶自己重新折叠并恢复活性 Anfinsen · ribonuclease

Because the sequence survives, Christian Anfinsen could denature ribonuclease with urea and β-mercaptoethanol, remove them slowly, and the enzyme refolded and became active again, which showed that the sequence determines the structure. The name Anfinsen and the ribonuclease experiment are general knowledge rather than from my textbook.

「序列决定结构」的实验证据。

⑨ 在细胞里，很多蛋白需要 GroEL 或 Hsp70 这类分子伴侣帮助折叠 chaperones

In the cell, however, many proteins fold with the help of chaperones (also called heat shock protein (many of them)) such as Hsp70 and the GroEL–GroES complex, which bind partly folded chains and stop them from aggregating.

试管里能自己折，细胞里常需要分子伴侣（= chaperone = heat shock protein (many of them)）。

⑩ 书外：折叠错误的蛋白聚成淀粉样纤维，见于阿尔茨海默病和朊病毒病 amyloid · prion

When folding fails, proteins can aggregate into amyloid fibres, as amyloid-β does in Alzheimer's disease, and in prion diseases such as Creutzfeldt–Jakob disease a misfolded prion protein converts normal copies into the misfolded form. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑪ 圆二色谱测二级结构含量，色氨酸荧光测是否解折叠 ⭐ Hudeček CD · tryptophan fluorescence

To check whether a protein is folded, circular dichroism measures the difference in absorption of left and right circularly polarised light, which reports the α-helix and β-sheet content, and tryptophan fluorescence shifts to a longer wavelength when a buried tryptophan becomes exposed to water on unfolding.

先说测的物理量，再说推出了什么。

⑫ 分子动力学模拟用的就是这些相互作用 ⭐ Martínek molecular dynamics

A molecular dynamics simulation calculates the movement of a protein in water using exactly these bonds and forces; it can propose a hypothesis, but an experiment has to test it.

「模拟提出假设，实验证明它」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the hydrophobic effect a bond?" — No, it is an increase in the entropy of water. · 不是键。
  • "Does denaturation break peptide bonds?" — No; only hydrolysis by acid or a protease does. · 不断肽键（= peptide bond = amide bond）。
  • "Do salt bridges hold the α-helix?" — No, main-chain hydrogen bonds do. · 螺旋靠主链氢键。
  • "Is tertiary structure entirely non-covalent?" — No, disulfide bonds are covalent. · 别忘了二硫键。
  • "Can a C–H group form a hydrogen bond?" — No, carbon is not electronegative enough. · C–H 不行。

定义

  • partial double bond — a bond between a single and a double bond, which cannot rotate · 部分双键
  • trans / cis — the two α-carbons on opposite / the same side of the peptide bond · 反式 / 顺式
  • reducing — keeping –SH groups as –SH, so disulfides do not form · 还原性
  • entropy — a measure of disorder · 熵
  • urea — a small molecule that unfolds proteins at high concentration · 尿素
  • β-mercaptoethanol — a reducing agent that breaks disulfide bonds · β-巯基乙醇
  • denaturation — loss of secondary, tertiary and quaternary structure · 变性
  • chaperone — a protein that helps other proteins fold · 分子伴侣
  • amyloid — insoluble fibres of misfolded protein · 淀粉样纤维
  • prion — a misfolded protein that converts normal copies of itself · 朊病毒
  • circular dichroism — difference in absorption of left and right circularly polarised light · 圆二色谱
  • molecular dynamics — computer simulation of atomic movement over time · 分子动力学

Proline & glycine — the two odd ones

卡 01 · 几乎必被追问的一对

Must say

Proline is the helix breaker, for two reasons: its nitrogen has no N–H left to donate the hydrogen bond, and its five-membered ring locks one backbone angle. The same two reasons put it in β-turns (also called reverse turn, β-bend). Glycine has only a hydrogen as its side chain, so it is the most flexible and the only one with no chiral carbon.

Numbers

On a Ramachandran plot glycine has the largest allowed region and proline the smallest.

拉氏图画的是 φ 和 ψ 两个二面角的允许区。

Hook

Collagen needs a glycine every third residue — only glycine fits the crowded centre of the triple helix.

Say this

Two residues behave differently from the other eighteen. Proline is the helix breaker, for two reasons: its side chain closes back onto its own nitrogen, so there is no N–H left to donate a hydrogen bond, and the five-membered ring locks one of the backbone dihedral angles. Those same two reasons make it common in beta turns, where the chain has to change direction. Glycine is the opposite: its side chain is a single hydrogen, so it is the most flexible residue and the only one without a chiral carbon. On a Ramachandran plot glycine has the largest allowed region and proline the smallest. ⚠ And that flexibility is why collagen needs a glycine at every third position — nothing else fits the centre of the triple helix.

约 40 秒 · 脯氨酸那两个理由要一口气说完

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Lipovová

① 二十种氨基酸按侧链分为非极性、极性不带电、带负电、带正电四组，甘氨酸单列 grouped by side chain

Glycine and proline are two of the twenty amino acids, which are grouped by their side chain into nonpolar, polar uncharged, negatively charged and positively charged, with glycine often listed on its own.

非极性 8 · 极性不带电 6 · 负 2 · 正 3 · 甘氨酸单列。

② 丙氨酸、缬氨酸、亮氨酸、异亮氨酸是脂肪族的，在蛋白质内部 Ala · Val · Leu · Ile

Next to proline in the nonpolar group, alanine has a methyl side chain; valine replaces glutamate in sickle-cell haemoglobin; leucine is purely ketogenic, so it never becomes glucose; and isoleucine has two chiral carbons.

Ala 甲基 · Val 镰状细胞 · Leu 纯生酮 · Ile 两个手性碳。

③ 苯丙氨酸、酪氨酸、色氨酸有芳香环，吸收紫外光，也是激素的原料 Phe · Tyr · Trp

Phenylalanine, an essential amino acid, is converted into tyrosine; tyrosine is both aromatic and polar and is the precursor of adrenaline and thyroxine; and tryptophan absorbs most strongly at 280 nm and is the precursor of serotonin.

Phe → Tyr · Tyr 做肾上腺素、甲状腺素 · Trp 做血清素。

④ 半胱氨酸形成二硫键，甲硫氨酸是每条肽链的第一个氨基酸 Cys · Met

The two sulfur amino acids differ: cysteine has a thiol group that forms disulfide bonds, and methionine is the first amino acid of every new chain and, as S-adenosylmethionine, the main methyl donor of the cell.

Cys 二硫键 · Met 起始 ＋ SAM 供甲基。

⑤ 丝氨酸和苏氨酸有羟基，是磷酸化和 O-糖基化的位点 Ser · Thr

Serine and threonine carry a hydroxyl group, so together with tyrosine they are the sites of phosphorylation by kinases; serine and threonine also carry O-linked sugars, and serine is the catalytic residue of chymotrypsin and trypsin.

Ser、Thr、Tyr = 磷酸化位点。

⑥ 天冬氨酸和谷氨酸带负电，天冬酰胺和谷氨酰胺是它们的酰胺 Asp · Glu · Asn · Gln

Aspartate and glutamate have a second carboxyl group and are negatively charged, and glutamate is the centre of amino-group transfer; asparagine carries the N-linked sugars, and glutamine carries ammonia in the blood.

Glu 转氨中心 · Asn 挂 N-糖 · Gln 运氨。

⑦ 赖氨酸、精氨酸、组氨酸带正电，三者的碱性基团不同 Lys · Arg · His

Lysine has an amino group at the end of its side chain, where biotin is attached to its enzymes; arginine has a guanidino group, is the most basic, and releases urea in the urea cycle (also called ornithine cycle, Krebs–Henseleit cycle); histidine has an imidazole ring that takes or gives a proton near pH 7, and it binds nickel in a His-tag column.

Lys 挂生物素 · Arg 最碱、出尿素 · His 搬质子、His-tag。

⑧ 甘氨酸是血红素和嘌呤的原料，精氨酸产生一氧化氮 beyond proteins

Amino acids are also raw material: glycine with succinyl-CoA makes haem, and glycine also enters the purine ring; arginine gives nitric oxide; and glutamate gives the neurotransmitter GABA.

接血红蛋白、核苷酸两个主题。

⑨ 胶原里的脯氨酸被羟化成羟脯氨酸，这一步需要维生素 C，缺乏会得坏血病 hydroxyproline · vitamin C · scurvy

Back to proline: in collagen many prolines are converted into hydroxyproline, a step that needs vitamin C, so a lack of vitamin C causes scurvy, with weak collagen and bleeding gums.

接维生素主题。

⑩ 九种必需氨基酸人体不能合成，必须从食物中获得 essential amino acids

Essential amino acids, nine in humans, cannot be made by the body: threonine, tryptophan, phenylalanine, leucine, isoleucine, lysine, methionine, valine and histidine.

苏 色 苯 · 亮 异 赖 · 甲 缬 组。

⑪ 定点突变把一个残基换成丙氨酸，就能看出这个侧链的作用 ⭐ Lipovová site-directed mutagenesis

In site-directed mutagenesis, replacing a residue with alanine removes its side chain without changing the backbone, and swapping lysine for glutamate tests a salt bridge.

从氨基酸表回到实验。

⑫ 书外：硒代半胱氨酸和吡咯赖氨酸是第 21、22 种，由终止密码子编码 selenocysteine · pyrrolysine

Selenocysteine, found in glutathione peroxidase, and pyrrolysine, found in some archaea, are inserted at a UGA or UAG stop codon (also called termination codon, nonsense codon). That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is glycine L or D?" — Neither; it has no chiral carbon. · 都不是。
  • "Is tyrosine aromatic or polar?" — Both; different books put it in different groups. · 两者都是。
  • "Cysteine or cystine?" — Cystine is two cysteines joined by a disulfide bond. · 胱氨酸是两个半胱氨酸。
  • "Which side chain is the most basic?" — Arginine. · 精氨酸。
  • "Does a His-tag bind through lysine?" — No, through the imidazole of histidine. · 是组氨酸的咪唑。

定义

  • side chain — the R group that differs between amino acids · 侧链
  • chiral carbon — a carbon carrying four different groups · 手性碳
  • ketogenic — degraded to acetyl-CoA or acetoacetate, not to glucose · 生酮
  • S-adenosylmethionine — activated methionine that donates methyl groups · S-腺苷甲硫氨酸
  • phosphorylation — adding a phosphate group, done by a kinase · 磷酸化
  • imidazole — the five-membered ring with two nitrogens in histidine · 咪唑
  • guanidino group — the strongly basic group of arginine · 胍基
  • GABA — gamma-aminobutyric acid, an inhibitory neurotransmitter · γ-氨基丁酸
  • scurvy — disease caused by lack of vitamin C · 坏血病
  • essential amino acid — one the body cannot synthesise · 必需氨基酸
  • site-directed mutagenesis — changing one chosen codon in a gene · 定点突变
  • stop codon — UAA, UAG or UGA, which normally ends translation · 终止密码子（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon）

Myoglobin & haemoglobin

卡 01 · 协同效应的教科书例子

Must say

Myoglobin is one subunit and gives a hyperbolic curve — it stores oxygen. Haemoglobin is four subunits and gives a sigmoid curve — it transports it. The sigmoid shape is positive cooperativity: binding the first oxygen pulls the subunits from the T state (also called tense state, deoxy form) to the R state (also called relaxed state, oxy form).

Numbers

Four subunits, α₂β₂, four oxygens.

Hook

Three things stabilise the T state and make it release oxygen in the tissues: H⁺ and CO₂ (the Bohr effect) and 2,3-BPG (also called 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate, 2,3-DPG), which sits in the positively charged central cavity. Without 2,3-BPG haemoglobin barely releases any oxygen at all.

Say this

Myoglobin has one subunit and a hyperbolic binding curve, and it stores oxygen in muscle. Haemoglobin has four subunits, α₂β₂, and a sigmoid curve, and it transports oxygen. The sigmoid shape means positive cooperativity: binding the first oxygen shifts the subunits from the T state, which binds weakly, to the R state, which binds strongly. Three things stabilise the T state and therefore make haemoglobin release oxygen where it is needed: protons and carbon dioxide, which together are the Bohr effect, and 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate, which sits in the positively charged central cavity. ⚠ Without 2,3-BPG, haemoglobin would hold onto its oxygen and hardly release any in the tissues.

约 40 秒 · 「双曲线储氧 / S 形运输」是开口第一句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Martínková、Hodek、Hudeček

① 氧结合在血红素的二价铁上，铁被氧化成三价就成了高铁血红蛋白，不能带氧 haem · Fe²⁺ · methaemoglobin

Oxygen binds to the ferrous iron, Fe²⁺, of haem, an iron atom held in a porphyrin ring; methaemoglobin, haemoglobin with oxidised Fe³⁺, cannot bind oxygen.

铁必须是二价。

② 肌红蛋白使肌肉呈红色，鲸和海豹的肌肉里特别多 myoglobin

Myoglobin, the oxygen store of muscle, gives muscle its red colour, and whales and seals have very high amounts of it for long dives.

储氧的蛋白，潜水动物最多。

③ 二氧化碳在血液里主要以碳酸氢根的形式运输 CO₂ as bicarbonate

The other gas, carbon dioxide, travels in blood mainly as bicarbonate, made in red cells by carbonic anhydrase, and a smaller part binds to haemoglobin itself.

接 Bohr 效应和血液 pH。

④ 胎儿血红蛋白与 2,3-BPG 结合弱，所以对氧亲和力更高 foetal haemoglobin

Foetal haemoglobin, made of two α and two γ chains, binds 2,3-BPG weakly, so it has a higher oxygen affinity and takes oxygen from the mother's blood across the placenta.

同一个调节分子，结合弱就抓氧更牢。

⑤ 在高原上红细胞里的 2,3-BPG 增加，组织里放出更多氧 high altitude

In adults the same molecule is used for adaptation: at high altitude and in anaemia red cells make more 2,3-BPG, so haemoglobin releases more oxygen in the tissues.

2,3-BPG（= 2,3-BPG = 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate = 2,3-DPG） 来自糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）的一条支路。

⑥ 一氧化碳和铁结合得比氧牢得多，还让其余亚基不放氧 carbon monoxide

Carbon monoxide, a colourless poisonous gas, binds the haem iron far more tightly than oxygen, and it also keeps the other subunits in the R state, so the remaining oxygen is not released; it also inhibits Complex IV (also called cytochrome c oxidase) of the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain).

带得少，带着的也不放。

⑦ 血红素由甘氨酸和琥珀酰辅酶 A 合成，降解成胆红素 haem synthesis · bilirubin

Haem is made from glycine and succinyl-CoA, and when old red cells are broken down it is converted to biliverdin and then bilirubin, the yellow bile pigment that colours stool and urine.

接氨基酸和柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）两个主题。

⑧ Kendrew 解出肌红蛋白结构，Perutz 解出血红蛋白结构，1962 年共获诺贝尔奖 Kendrew · Perutz

John Kendrew solved myoglobin by X-ray crystallography in 1957, Max Perutz solved haemoglobin, and they shared the 1962 Nobel Prize in Chemistry. The Perutz part and the Nobel Prize are general knowledge rather than from my textbook.

最早解出的两个蛋白结构。

⑨ 血红素传感蛋白结合 O₂、NO 或 CO 后构象改变，激活另一端的结构域 ⭐ Martínková haem-based gas sensors

The same principle as the T-to-R change is used by haem-based gas sensors: O₂, NO or CO binds the haem iron, the protein changes conformation, and a histidine kinase or other domain at the other end is switched on; soluble guanylate cyclase, the NO receptor, works this way.

「一个位点的结合改变构象，构象本身就是信号」。

⑩ 细胞色素 P450 也含血红素，但它用氧去氧化药物 ⭐ Hodek cytochrome P450

Cytochrome P450, a family of liver enzymes, contains the same haem, but instead of carrying oxygen it uses oxygen to oxidise drugs and foreign compounds.

同一个血红素，两种功能。

⑪ 氧合和脱氧血红蛋白的吸收光谱不同，可以用分光光度计测 ⭐ Hudeček UV-Vis absorption spectrum

Haem is coloured, so oxyhaemoglobin and deoxyhaemoglobin have different absorption spectra, and a spectrophotometer or a pulse oximeter can measure oxygen saturation directly.

动脉血鲜红、静脉血暗红就是这个差别。

⑫ 书外：章鱼和螃蟹用含铜的血蓝蛋白，豆科根瘤里有豆血红蛋白 haemocyanin · leghaemoglobin · thalassaemia

Haemocyanin, a copper protein, carries oxygen in octopus and crabs, leghaemoglobin in legume root nodules keeps oxygen away from nitrogenase, and in thalassaemia too little α or β chain is made. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。镰状细胞是链错了，地贫是链少了。

陷阱问法

  • "Why not use myoglobin for transport?" — Its hyperbolic curve would release little oxygen in the tissues. · 双曲线在组织里放不出氧。
  • "Does 2,3-BPG bind oxyhaemoglobin?" — No, it binds the deoxy T state（= T state = tense state = deoxy form）. · 只结合脱氧态。
  • "Is the Hill coefficient the number of sites?" — No; for haemoglobin it is about 2.8 with four sites. · 不是位点数。
  • "Can Fe³⁺ haem carry oxygen?" — No, that is methaemoglobin. · 三价铁不行。
  • "Is haemoglobin an example of tertiary structure?" — No, of quaternary; myoglobin is the tertiary example. · 它是四级结构的例子。

定义

  • haem — an iron atom held in a porphyrin ring · 血红素
  • porphyrin — a flat ring of four pyrroles that binds a metal · 卟啉
  • methaemoglobin — haemoglobin with Fe³⁺, unable to bind oxygen · 高铁血红蛋白
  • bicarbonate — HCO₃⁻, the main form of CO₂ in blood · 碳酸氢根
  • carbonic anhydrase — the enzyme that converts CO₂ and water to bicarbonate · 碳酸酐酶
  • R state — the high-affinity conformation of haemoglobin · R 态（= R state = relaxed state = oxy form）
  • bilirubin — yellow pigment from haem breakdown · 胆红素
  • histidine kinase — an enzyme that phosphorylates its own histidine in bacterial signalling · 组氨酸激酶
  • soluble guanylate cyclase — the haem protein that senses NO and makes cGMP · 可溶性鸟苷酸环化酶
  • cytochrome P450 — haem enzymes that oxidise drugs and foreign compounds · 细胞色素 P450
  • pulse oximeter — a clip that measures oxygen saturation by light absorption · 脉搏血氧仪
  • haemocyanin — copper-containing oxygen carrier of molluscs and arthropods · 血蓝蛋白
  • leghaemoglobin — haemoglobin of legume root nodules · 豆血红蛋白
  • thalassaemia — inherited reduced production of α or β globin chains · 地中海贫血

Enzymes & kinetics

卡 02 · 17 · 19

Must say

An enzyme only lowers the activation energy. It does not change the equilibrium constant and it does not change ΔG. The active site has the highest affinity for the transition state, not for the substrate.

「对过渡态亲和力最高」这一句是理解的分水岭，不是背的。

Numbers

Km (also called Michaelis constant) is the substrate concentration at half Vmax. It is a property of the enzyme, independent of enzyme concentration, and a larger Km means weaker binding. kcat/Km is the specificity constant (also called kcat/Km, catalytic efficiency), limited by diffusion at 10⁸–10⁹ M⁻¹s⁻¹.

Hook

Competitive: Km up, Vmax unchanged, lines meet on the y-axis. Non-competitive: Km unchanged, Vmax down, lines meet on the x-axis. Uncompetitive: both down, lines are parallel. Classic examples: sulfonamides, and malonate on succinate dehydrogenase.

Say this

An enzyme lowers the activation energy and nothing else — it does not change the equilibrium constant and it does not change ΔG, so it only changes how fast equilibrium is reached, not where it lies. ⚠ The key idea is that the active site has its highest affinity for the transition state, not for the substrate, and the binding energy released is what pays for lowering the barrier. Km is the substrate concentration at half Vmax; it is characteristic of the enzyme, independent of enzyme concentration, and a larger Km means weaker binding. For inhibition: competitive raises Km and leaves Vmax, non-competitive lowers Vmax and leaves Km, and uncompetitive lowers both.

约 45 秒 · 「对过渡态亲和力最高」是区分「懂」和「背」的那句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① kcat/Km 是专一性常数，表示底物浓度很低时酶的效率 specificity constant

Beyond Km, the specificity constant, kcat/Km, is the rate constant for free enzyme and free substrate going to product, so it gives the efficiency of the enzyme at low substrate concentration, which is the normal situation in the cell.

把结合和催化两步合成一个数。

② kcat/Km 的上限是扩散极限，约 10⁸–10⁹ M⁻¹s⁻¹ diffusion limit

The diffusion limit, about 10⁸ to 10⁹ per molar per second, is the highest possible value, because an enzyme cannot react faster than it meets its substrate; triose phosphate isomerase and acetylcholinesterase come close to it and are called catalytically perfect.

到了上限，再改只能改稳定性和专一性。

③ 两个底物竞争同一个酶时，比较的是 kcat/Km，不是 Km comparing substrates

This is also why kcat/Km is the right number for comparing substrates: when two substrates compete for one enzyme, their rates are in the ratio of their kcat/Km values.

只看 Km（= Km = Michaelis constant） 等于只看结合，不看反应。

④ 活性中心用酸碱催化、共价催化、金属离子，以及把底物靠近并定向 catalytic strategies

To reach such rates, active sites use acid–base catalysis, as by histidine; covalent catalysis, as by the serine of chymotrypsin; metal ions, as the zinc of carbonic anhydrase; and holding the substrates close together in the right orientation.

接氨基酸主题：His 传质子，Ser 进攻底物。

⑤ 很多酶需要辅酶，大多数辅酶来自维生素 coenzymes

Many enzymes also need a coenzyme: NAD⁺ comes from niacin, FAD from riboflavin, coenzyme A from pantothenic acid, and pyridoxal phosphate from vitamin B6.

酶蛋白 ＋ 辅因子 = 全酶。接维生素主题。

⑥ 细胞用别构调节、磷酸化、反馈抑制和酶原激活来调节酶 regulation

Cells regulate enzymes by allosteric effectors, as ATP on phosphofructokinase; by phosphorylation, as in glycogen phosphorylase; by feedback inhibition from the end product; and by zymogen (also called proenzyme) activation, as trypsinogen is cut into trypsin.

别构 · 磷酸化 · 反馈 · 酶原（= zymogen = proenzyme）。

⑦ 同工酶催化同一反应，结构不同，血中的同工酶谱可以用于诊断 isoenzymes

Isoenzymes (also called isozyme) catalyse the same reaction but differ in structure; lactate dehydrogenase has different forms in heart and in skeletal muscle, and their pattern in blood helps to diagnose which organ is damaged.

LDH 是标准例子。

⑧ 酶活性用产物生成速率来测，单位是 U 或 katal enzyme activity

Activity is measured as the rate of product formation, often by absorbance, for example the absorbance of NADH at 340 nm, and it is given in units or katals.

U = 每分钟 1 μmol；katal = 每秒 1 mol。

⑨ 阿司匹林、青霉素、他汀和神经毒剂都是酶抑制剂 drugs as inhibitors

Aspirin irreversibly inhibits cyclooxygenase, penicillin inhibits the bacterial enzyme that cross-links the cell wall, statins inhibit HMG-CoA reductase in cholesterol synthesis, and sarin irreversibly inhibits acetylcholinesterase.

不可逆 vs 可逆，接抑制那一段。

⑩ 细胞色素 P450 被抑制或被诱导，会改变另一种药物的清除速度 ⭐ Hodek P450 · drug interactions

Drugs also act on the enzymes that remove them: cytochrome P450 enzymes clear many drugs, and grapefruit juice inhibits them while rifampicin induces them, so the level of another drug rises or falls.

浓度升到中毒，或降到无效。

⑪ 定点突变可以改变酶的专一性和稳定性，改造后的结果用 kcat/Km 报告 ⭐ Lipovová enzyme engineering

In enzyme engineering, site-directed mutagenesis changes the specificity or thermostability of an enzyme, the result is reported as kcat/Km, and engineered proteases and amylases are used in washing powders.

流水线：构建 → 表达 → 纯化 → 测活。

⑫ 书外：核酶是有催化活性的 RNA，催化抗体是针对过渡态类似物制备的抗体 ribozymes · catalytic antibodies

Ribozymes, catalytic RNAs, include the peptidyl transferase of the ribosome, and catalytic antibodies, raised against a transition-state analogue, can catalyse the matching reaction. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

后者直接证明「结合过渡态 = 催化」。

陷阱问法

  • "Does a high kcat/Km mean tight binding?" — No, it combines binding and turnover. · 不等于结合牢。
  • "Is Km the affinity?" — Only when the chemical step is slow. · 只在催化步很慢时。
  • "Can an enzyme exceed the diffusion limit?" — Only when substrate is passed directly between enzymes in a complex, called substrate channelling. · 单个酶不能。
  • "Does an enzyme shift the equilibrium?" — No, it only changes how fast equilibrium is reached. · 不改平衡。

定义

  • kcat — turnover number: substrate molecules converted per enzyme molecule per second · 转换数（= kcat = turnover number）
  • specificity constant — kcat/Km, the efficiency at low substrate · 专一性常数（= specificity constant = kcat/Km = catalytic efficiency）
  • diffusion limit — the rate at which two molecules meet in water · 扩散极限
  • acid–base catalysis — speeding a reaction by giving or taking a proton · 酸碱催化
  • covalent catalysis — the enzyme forms a temporary covalent bond with the substrate · 共价催化
  • coenzyme — a small organic molecule an enzyme needs · 辅酶
  • allosteric effector — a molecule that binds outside the active site and changes activity · 别构效应物
  • feedback inhibition — the end product inhibits the first enzyme of its pathway · 反馈抑制
  • zymogen — an inactive enzyme precursor activated by cleavage · 酶原
  • isoenzymes — different proteins catalysing the same reaction · 同工酶（= isoenzyme = isozyme）
  • katal — the amount of enzyme converting one mole per second · 开特
  • induction — the cell making more of an enzyme · 诱导
  • thermostability — resistance of a protein to heat · 热稳定性
  • transition-state analogue — a stable molecule shaped like the transition state · 过渡态类似物
  • substrate channelling — passing an intermediate directly from one active site to the next · 底物通道

Carbohydrates

卡 06

Must say

A monosaccharide is an aldehyde or a ketone with two or more hydroxyls, general formula (CH₂O)ₙ, that cannot be hydrolysed to a smaller sugar.

Numbers

Maltose α-1,4 · lactose β-1,4 · sucrose α-1,β-2 · cellulose β-1,4. Glycogen and starch: main chain α-1,4, branches α-1,6.

Hook

Sucrose is the only non-reducing one of those, because both anomeric carbons are used in the bond. Humans cannot digest cellulose because we have no enzyme for β-1,4.

Say this

A monosaccharide is an aldehyde or a ketone carrying two or more hydroxyl groups, with the general formula (CH₂O)ₙ, and it cannot be hydrolysed to anything smaller. In water it interconverts between the open chain and the ring, which is mutarotation, giving the alpha and beta anomers. The linkages are worth naming precisely: maltose is α-1,4, lactose is β-1,4, sucrose is α-1,β-2, and cellulose is β-1,4. ⚠ Sucrose is the only non-reducing one of those, because both anomeric carbons are used in the bond. Glycogen and starch are both α-1,4 chains with α-1,6 branches, and humans cannot digest cellulose because we have no enzyme for a β-1,4 bond.

约 40 秒 · 蔗糖为什么非还原，理由要说出口

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hodek、Hudeček、Lipovová

① 糖有四个功能：储能、遗传信息、结构支撑、细胞识别 four functions

Carbohydrates have four functions: energy storage as starch and glycogen, information as the ribose of RNA and the deoxyribose of DNA, support as cellulose and chitin, and recognition as the sugar chains on cell surfaces.

开口先给这四个词。

② 葡萄糖以糖原形式储存，因为一个大分子几乎不增加渗透压 glycogen · osmotic pressure

Glycogen, the storage form in liver and muscle, joins many thousands of glucose units into one molecule, so the cell stores a lot of glucose without raising the osmotic pressure.

十万个葡萄糖在渗透压上只算一个粒子。

③ 糖原分支很多，因为糖原磷酸化酶只能从非还原端切下葡萄糖 branching

Glycogen is highly branched because glycogen phosphorylase releases glucose only from the non-reducing ends, and every branch adds another end, so glucose can be released quickly.

分支越多，同时开工的末端越多。

④ α 键的链卷成螺旋，用于储能；β 键的链是直的，形成纤维 α storage · β structure

The type of bond decides the use: α-1,4 chains coil into a helix that amylase digests easily, while β-1,4 chains are straight and hydrogen-bond into fibres, as in cellulose and in chitin, the polymer of N-acetylglucosamine in insect cuticle and fungal walls.

同是葡萄糖，键的构型决定用途。

⑤ 牛和白蚁能消化纤维素，是靠肠道微生物的纤维素酶 cellulase · gut microbes

Because of the β bond, cows and termites digest cellulose only through cellulase made by microbes in their gut, and in humans cellulose passes through as dietary fibre.

动物自己都没有纤维素酶。

⑥ 细胞里的糖几乎都以磷酸酯形式代谢，另有糖醛酸、氨基糖、脱氧糖、糖醇 derivatives

Inside the cell sugars are metabolised almost only as phosphate esters, such as glucose 6-phosphate, and other derivatives are glucuronic acid, glucosamine, 2-deoxyribose, sorbitol and ascorbic acid, which is vitamin C.

每个衍生物都是改了一个基团。

⑦ 糖链通过天冬酰胺（N-连接）或丝氨酸、苏氨酸（O-连接）接到蛋白上，ABO 血型只差末端一个糖 glycoproteins · ABO blood groups

Sugar chains are attached to proteins through asparagine, N-linked, or serine and threonine, O-linked, and the A, B and O blood groups differ only in the terminal sugar: N-acetylgalactosamine for A, galactose for B, and none for O.

接「蛋白质翻译后修饰」主题。

⑧ 糖胺聚糖带大量负电，存在于结缔组织，肝素是抗凝剂 glycosaminoglycans · heparin

Glycosaminoglycans, long negatively charged chains of repeating disaccharides, fill connective tissue: hyaluronic acid in joint fluid, chondroitin sulfate in cartilage, and heparin, which prevents blood clotting.

关节液 · 软骨 · 抗凝。

⑨ 书外：血糖高时葡萄糖不经酶就连到血红蛋白上，测 HbA1c 看几个月的平均血糖 glycation · HbA1c

Glycation, glucose attaching to proteins without an enzyme, also happens to haemoglobin, and doctors measure HbA1c to see a diabetic patient's average blood glucose over the past two to three months. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

接血红蛋白主题。主动标「书外」。

⑩ 肝脏把葡萄糖醛酸连到药物上，使它溶于水而被排出 ⭐ Hodek glucuronidation

Glucuronic acid has a second use: in phase II of drug metabolism the liver attaches it to drugs, bilirubin and toxins, which makes them water-soluble so they can be excreted in bile or urine.

第一相 P450 氧化，第二相挂上葡萄糖醛酸。

⑪ 糖是手性的，会旋转偏振光，变旋现象就是从旋光度的变化发现的 ⭐ Hudeček optical rotation · polarimetry

Sugars are chiral, so a solution rotates plane-polarised light, measured with a polarimeter; mutarotation was discovered as a slow change of that rotation after dissolving glucose, and iodine stains amylose blue.

先说测的物理量：旋光角。

⑫ 糖苷酶水解糖苷键，能区分 α 和 β 以及不同的糖，也能用来合成糖链 ⭐ Lipovová glycosidases · transglycosylation

Glycosidases, the enzymes that hydrolyse glycosidic bonds, distinguish α from β and one sugar from another, as α-L-fucosidase removes only terminal fucose; under suitable conditions they also transfer sugars to build new chains, which is transglycosylation.

回到她的岩藻糖苷酶。

陷阱问法

  • "Starch and cellulose are both glucose; why can we digest only starch?" — Starch has α bonds; we have no enzyme for β-1,4. · α 和 β 的区别。
  • "How many reducing ends does glycogen have?" — One, but very many non-reducing ends. · 还原端只有一个。
  • "Is fructose always a five-membered ring?" — When bound, as in sucrose, yes; free fructose prefers the six-membered ring. · 游离时偏好六元环。
  • "Why does muscle store glycogen rather than fat for sprinting?" — Glycogen can be used quickly and without oxygen. · 快，而且不需要氧。

定义

  • osmotic pressure — pressure that draws water towards more dissolved particles · 渗透压
  • non-reducing end — a chain end whose anomeric carbon is in a bond · 非还原端
  • chitin — β-1,4 polymer of N-acetylglucosamine · 几丁质
  • cellulase — enzyme that hydrolyses β-1,4 bonds of cellulose · 纤维素酶
  • dietary fibre — plant polysaccharides that humans cannot digest · 膳食纤维
  • phosphate ester — a sugar hydroxyl joined to phosphate · 磷酸酯
  • glucuronic acid — glucose with carbon 6 oxidised to a carboxyl · 葡萄糖醛酸
  • glycosaminoglycan — repeating disaccharide chain of uronic acid and amino sugar · 糖胺聚糖
  • glycation — non-enzymatic attachment of a sugar to a protein · 糖化
  • phase II — the conjugation step of drug metabolism · 第二相反应
  • polarimeter — instrument measuring the rotation of polarised light · 旋光仪
  • glycosidase — enzyme that hydrolyses a glycosidic bond · 糖苷酶
  • transglycosylation — transfer of a sugar to an acceptor other than water · 转糖基

Nucleic acid structure

卡 03

  1. ⚠ 这道题问的是「区别」，所以第一句先说有几条轴 四条轴：糖、碱基、链数、功能。前三条是化学，第四条不一样——第四条是「各自干什么」， 而前三条的化学就是在为它付账。⚠ 这一句是这道题的全部结构，先给轴，再往轴上填。 （卷子上这道题出现过两次，是重复最多的几道之一。）
  2. 轴一 · 糖 —— 差别只有一个 2′-OH DNA 是 2-脱氧-D-核糖，RNA 是 D-核糖，差别就是 2′ 碳上那个羟基。 ⚠ 「2′」的撇是为了跟碱基上的编号分开。这一个羟基决定了后面所有事： 它让 RNA 在碱里容易水解，所以 RNA 不稳定、DNA 稳定——存长期信息的那个必须稳。
  3. 轴二 · 碱基 —— 三个共用，一个各有 A、G、C 两边都有；尿嘧啶只在 RNA，胸腺嘧啶主要在 DNA。 ⚠ 为什么 DNA 要多花一个甲基做胸腺嘧啶：胞嘧啶会自发脱氨变成尿嘧啶， DNA 里如果本来就有尿嘧啶，修复系统就分不清哪个是损伤。所以 DNA 里出现 U 一定是错误。
  4. 轴三 · 链数 —— 双链对单链，但别说死 DNA 通常是右手双螺旋，mRNA 和 rRNA 是单链。 ⚠ 别说「RNA 是单链」就完：rRNA 和 tRNA 会自己折回来配对，形成局部的螺旋， tRNA 的三叶草就是这么来的。准确的说法是「RNA 是单链，但可以自己配对」。
  5. 轴四 · 功能 —— 这条跟前三条不是一类 DNA 存全部遗传信息；RNA 是其中一条指令的临时拷贝。 ⚠ 而「拷贝」这个角色只属于 mRNA——tRNA 和 rRNA 不是拷贝，它们本身就是干活的分子。 落点：脱氧核糖是从核糖还原来的，所以 RNA 的化学出现在 DNA 之前（接 RNA world）。
Must say

B-DNA is a right-handed double helix with antiparallel strands. A=T is two hydrogen bonds, G≡C is three, so more GC means a more stable helix. Denaturation gives the hyperchromic effect: absorbance at 260 nm rises as the strands separate.

Numbers

10.5 base pairs per turn, 0.34 nm per base. Tm (also called melting temperature) is the temperature at which half is denatured, and it rises with GC content and ionic strength. Pure DNA A260/A280 = 1.8, pure RNA = 2.0.

Hook

Most transcription factors read the sequence through the major groove. The peptidyl transferase activity is in the rRNA — the ribosome is a ribozyme.

Say this

There are four differences between DNA and RNA. Three are chemical — which sugar, which bases, and how many chains — and the fourth is what each one is for, which is what the chemistry pays for. The sugar in DNA is 2-deoxy-D-ribose and in RNA D-ribose, so the difference is a single 2′ hydroxyl, and that hydroxyl is why RNA is hydrolysed in alkali and DNA is stable. Both use A, G and C, but uracil is in RNA and thymine in DNA; cytosine deaminates to uracil, so a uracil found in DNA is always damage. DNA is usually double-stranded and RNA single-stranded, although transfer RNA and ribosomal RNA fold back and pair with themselves. And the fourth difference: DNA stores all the information, while RNA is a working copy of one instruction — though only messenger RNA is a copy, because transfer RNA and ribosomal RNA are working molecules in their own right. B-DNA is a right-handed double helix with two antiparallel strands, about 10.5 base pairs per turn and 0.34 nanometres per base. A pairs with T through two hydrogen bonds and G with C through three, so a higher GC content gives a more stable helix and a higher melting temperature. When it denatures the strands separate and the absorbance at 260 nanometres rises — that is the hyperchromic effect — and Tm is the temperature at which half of it has melted. ⚠ For purity, pure DNA has an A260 over A280 of 1.8 and pure RNA 2.0. And most transcription factors read the sequence through the major groove, because that is where the bases are accessible.

约 75 秒 · 增色效应和 Tm 的定义常被连着问

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hudeček、Lipovová、Hodek

① 磷酸二酯键把一个糖的 3′ 碳连到下一个糖的 5′ 碳，每个核苷酸带一个负电荷 phosphodiester bond

The phosphodiester bond, the bond of the backbone, is one phosphate joining the 3′ carbon of one sugar to the 5′ carbon of the next; it keeps one negative charge, so DNA is negative along its whole length and agarose gel electrophoresis separates it by size.

一个核苷酸一个负电 → 电泳按长度分。

② DNA 聚合酶只能把核苷酸加到游离的 3′-OH 上，所以需要引物 free 3′-OH · primer

Because of this bond, DNA polymerase can only add a nucleotide to a free 3′-OH, so the chain grows 5′ to 3′, and it cannot start a chain, so primase first makes a short RNA primer.

RNA 聚合酶不需要引物。接复制主题。

③ 书外：没有 3′-OH 的双脱氧核苷酸使链终止，这是 Sanger 测序和 AZT 的原理 chain termination

A dideoxynucleotide, which has no 3′-OH, stops the chain, and this is the basis of Sanger sequencing and of the HIV drug AZT. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

同一条规则，一个用于测序，一个用于抗病毒。

④ 碱基通过 β-N-糖苷键连在糖的 1′ 碳上，这个键会自发断裂，嘌呤最常见 N-glycosidic bond · depurination

The N-glycosidic bond joins the base to the 1′ carbon of the sugar in the β configuration, and it can break spontaneously, most often for purines, which is depurination; the empty site is repaired by base excision repair.

不是磷酸二酯键，别混。接 DNA 修复。

⑤ RNA 的 2′-OH 使它在碱中容易水解；DNA 用胸腺嘧啶，因为胞嘧啶脱氨会变成尿嘧啶 2′-OH · thymine vs uracil

The two nucleic acids differ in two ways: the 2′-OH of ribose makes RNA easy to hydrolyse in alkali, and DNA uses thymine because cytosine deamination produces uracil, which the repair system recognises as damage only if uracil is not a normal DNA base.

DNA 用来长期保存，RNA 用来临时工作。

⑥ mRNA 带遗传信息，tRNA 运氨基酸，rRNA 构成核糖体，snRNA 构成剪接体 mRNA · tRNA · rRNA · snRNA

RNA comes in several kinds: mRNA carries the coding sequence to the ribosome; tRNA carries an amino acid and reads the codon with its anticodon; rRNA forms the ribosome and catalyses the peptide bond (also called amide bond); and snRNA forms the spliceosome, which removes introns (also called intervening sequence).

只有 mRNA 是临时拷贝，其余都是结构和功能成分。

⑦ miRNA 和 siRNA 使互补的 mRNA 沉默，lncRNA 调控基因，核酶是有催化活性的 RNA miRNA · siRNA · lncRNA · ribozyme

miRNA and siRNA, about 21 nucleotides long, silence a complementary mRNA; lncRNAs, longer than 200 nucleotides, regulate genes, as Xist inactivates one X chromosome; and a ribozyme is an RNA with catalytic activity, which supports the RNA world hypothesis.

小 RNA 沉默 · 长 RNA 调控 · 核酶催化。

⑧ 脱水的 DNA 变成 A 型，某些序列形成左手 Z 型，环状 DNA 有超螺旋，真核 DNA 缠在组蛋白上 A-DNA · Z-DNA · supercoiling · histones

Besides B-DNA, dehydrated DNA takes the A form, some GC-rich sequences form left-handed Z-DNA, circular bacterial DNA is supercoiled by DNA gyrase (also called DNA gyrase, topoisomerase II (bacteria)), and eukaryotic DNA is wound around positively charged histones in nucleosomes.

只有 Z 是左手。接染色质主题。

⑨ 书外：Griffith 1928 年发现，加热杀死的 S 型肺炎球菌能把活的 R 型变成致死的 S 型 Griffith · transformation

How we know DNA is the genetic material begins with Frederick Griffith in 1928: live S strain pneumococcus, smooth with a capsule, killed mice, while live R strain, rough without a capsule, and heat-killed S were each harmless. But live R plus heat-killed S killed the mouse and live S was recovered, so something from the dead cells had changed R into S, which is where the word transformation comes from; that is general knowledge, not from my textbook.

S 有荚膜致死，R 无荚膜不致死；死 S ＋ 活 R → 小鼠死，还分离出活 S。

⑩ Avery、MacLeod 和 McCarty 1944 年证明转化因子是 DNA Avery · MacLeod · McCarty

Oswald Avery, Colin MacLeod and Maclyn McCarty purified this transforming principle in 1944: protease and RNase did not stop the transformation, but DNase did, so the transforming principle is DNA.

书 p009 年表只有这一行：1944，Avery，遗传信息由 DNA 传递。

⑪ 书外：Hershey 和 Chase 1952 年发现，进入细菌的是噬菌体的 ³²P 标记的 DNA，不是 ³⁵S 标记的蛋白 Hershey–Chase

Alfred Hershey and Martha Chase in 1952 labelled a bacteriophage either with ³⁵S in its protein or with ³²P in its DNA, and only the ³²P entered the bacteria. That is general knowledge, not from my textbook.

蛋白含硫不含磷，DNA 含磷不含硫，所以两种同位素能分开标记。

⑫ Chargaff 发现 A=T、G=C，Franklin 拍了 X 射线衍射图，Watson 和 Crick 1953 年提出双螺旋 discovery

Erwin Chargaff showed that A equals T and G equals C, Rosalind Franklin obtained the X-ray diffraction pictures of a helix, and James Watson and Francis Crick proposed the double helix in 1953.

A=T、G=C 的比例 → 碱基配对的线索。

⑬ 堆积的碱基吸光较少，所以解链时 260 nm 吸光度升高；圆二色谱能区分 B 型和 Z 型 ⭐ Hudeček base stacking · CD

The 260 nm absorbance comes from the bases, and stacked bases absorb less than free ones, which is why melting increases it; circular dichroism distinguishes right-handed B-DNA from left-handed Z-DNA.

增色效应的物理原因。

⑭ 核酸酶水解磷酸二酯键，S1 核酸酶只切单链 DNA 和 RNA ⭐ Lipovová nucleases · S1 nuclease

Nucleases hydrolyse phosphodiester bonds, and S1 nuclease from Aspergillus oryzae cuts only single-stranded DNA and RNA, so it is used to remove single-stranded ends and to map unpaired regions.

回到她的 S1 核酸酶。

⑮ 苯并芘被细胞色素 P450 活化后与鸟嘌呤共价结合，形成 DNA 加合物 ⭐ Hodek DNA adducts

Benzo[a]pyrene, a carcinogen in tobacco smoke, is activated by cytochrome P450 into an epoxide that binds covalently to guanine, forming a DNA adduct that can cause mutations.

解毒反而活化 → Hodek 的研究方向。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the base joined to the sugar by a phosphodiester bond?" — No, by an N-glycosidic bond. · 是 N-糖苷键。
  • "Why can't DNA polymerase start a chain?" — It needs a free 3′-OH to add to. · 需要 3′-OH。
  • "Is RNA always single-stranded?" — No, it folds back and base-pairs with itself, as in tRNA. · 会自身配对。
  • "Are A-DNA and Z-DNA both left-handed?" — No, only Z-DNA. · 只有 Z。
  • "Is GC more stable only because of three hydrogen bonds?" — No, base stacking also contributes. · 还有碱基堆积。

定义

  • phosphodiester bond — one phosphate esterified to two sugar hydroxyls · 磷酸二酯键
  • agarose gel electrophoresis — separating DNA by size in an electric field · 琼脂糖凝胶电泳
  • primase — the RNA polymerase that makes primers · 引物酶
  • dideoxynucleotide — a nucleotide lacking the 3′-OH · 双脱氧核苷酸
  • AZT — azidothymidine, an HIV drug that terminates reverse transcription · 齐多夫定
  • depurination — loss of a purine base by breaking the N-glycosidic bond · 脱嘌呤
  • base excision repair — repair that removes a damaged or missing base and refills it · 碱基切除修复
  • deamination — loss of an amino group · 脱氨
  • spliceosome — the RNA–protein complex that removes introns（= intron = intervening sequence） · 剪接体
  • Xist — the lncRNA that inactivates one X chromosome in female cells · Xist
  • RNA world — the hypothesis that early life used RNA for both information and catalysis · RNA 世界
  • DNA gyrase — bacterial topoisomerase that introduces supercoils · DNA 旋转酶（= DNA gyrase = topoisomerase II (bacteria)）
  • pneumococcus — Streptococcus pneumoniae, the bacterium that causes pneumonia · 肺炎链球菌
  • capsule — a polysaccharide coat around a bacterium that protects it from the immune system · 荚膜
  • transformation — uptake of DNA from outside that changes a cell's heritable traits · 转化
  • transforming principle — the substance from dead S cells that caused transformation · 转化因子
  • bacteriophage — a virus that infects bacteria · 噬菌体
  • histones — basic proteins around which DNA is wound · 组蛋白
  • base stacking — flat bases lying on top of each other in the helix · 碱基堆积
  • S1 nuclease — a fungal nuclease specific for single-stranded nucleic acid · S1 核酸酶
  • DNA adduct — a foreign molecule covalently bound to DNA · DNA 加合物

Metabolism — the ledger

Where it all joins up

⚠ 脑子糊的时候先看这一节 —— 一条缝能同时固定两块。 每条都压成一个抓手：一个缩写、一句对仗，或者一串多米诺

  1. ABC — 生物素羧化酶要的三样 ATP · Biotin · CO₂。三个酶正好各属一条路： 丙酮酸羧化酶（糖异生）· 乙酰-CoA 羧化酶（脂肪合成）· 丙酰-CoA 羧化酶（奇数碳脂肪酸）。 ⚠ 说「biotin-dependent carboxylases」才准：rubisco 和 PEP 羧化酶都不用生物素、也不花 ATP， γ-谷氨酰羧化酶用的是维生素 K。
  2. 磷酸化 ＝ 饥饿模式 — 拆的开，造的关 这一条能一口气解释五个酶：糖原磷酸化酶开 · 糖原合酶关 · HSL 开（拆脂肪）· ACC 关（别造脂肪）· PDH 关（省着葡萄糖）。 ⚠ 方向不用一个个背 —— 问「饿的时候身体想干嘛」就行。
  3. 柠檬酸 ＝ 货 ＋ 令 它是 TCA 的中间物，是把 acetyl-CoA 运出线粒体的车， 同时还是信号：关 PFK-1、开 ACC。 ⚠ 一个分子既是货又是命令：「碳多了，别烧糖，去存脂肪」。
  4. 丙二酰-CoA ＝ 造出来的东西，反手当门闩 它是脂肪合成的第一个产物，而它关掉 CPT-1，也就是氧化的门。 ⚠ 产物本身就是对向通路的开关 —— 跟上一条合起来，脂代谢互控就全了。
  5. No NAD⁺, no glycolysis（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway） — 一条线串四块 GAPDH 那一步必须有 NAD⁺ → 缺氧时靠乳酸或乙醇再生 → 有氧时靠呼吸链再生 → 所以柠檬酸循环自己不用氧，却离了氧就停。 ⚠ 糖酵解 · 发酵 · 呼吸链 · TCA 四块，是这一条线缝起来的。
  6. 红细胞的多米诺 — 一个事实倒出四个答案 没有线粒体 → 只能糖酵解 → 所以靠 PPP 拿 NADPH 抗氧化 → 所以 G6PD 缺乏会溶血 → 而 2,3-BPG 正好是糖酵解的旁路产物。 ⚠ 推第一句，后面四句自己出来。
  7. 维生素 ＝ 全书的索引（一个动词一个 B） B₁ 去羧（TPP：PDH · α-KG · 转酮酶）· B₇ 加羧（所有生物素羧化酶）· B₆ 转氨（PLP：所有转氨酶）· B₁₂ 重排（甲基丙二酰-CoA 变位酶）· B₉ 搬一碳（THF）· B₅ 搬酰基（CoA）· B₂ / B₃ 搬氢。 ⚠ B₁ 把碳拿下来，B₇ 把碳装上去 —— 这一对最好记。 背维生素的时候你顺带复习了六条路。
  8. 花一个 CO₂ 买「不可逆」— 同一招用了两次 ACC 加上 CO₂ → FAS 缩合时又放掉；丙酮酸羧化酶加上 → PEPCK 又放掉。 ⚠ 两处都不是为了把碳留下，是为了让下一步不可逆。
  9. 不可逆 → 控制点 → 有调节物 → 对向通路换酶 ⚠ 这不是四个知识点，是一条推理链。 碰到任何一条陌生的路，找那个不可逆的酶，其余三句能推出来。 调节物按四类猜：自己的产物 · 能量信号 · 激素磷酸化 · 别条路的中间物。
  10. β-氧化（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation）前三步 ＝ 柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）最后三步 脱氢 → 加水 → 脱氢，一模一样的化学（succinate → fumarate → malate → OAA）。 ⚠ 同一套化学在两条路上复用 —— 白拿的加分。
  11. PDH 和 α-KG 脱氢酶是同一台机器 三个酶、五个辅因子、同样的氧化脱羧（支链氨基酸那个是第三台）。 ⚠ 背一次会三个。
  12. 光合和呼吸互为逆过程 — 同一个数，两个方向 光合把水拆开放氧，ΔG°′ ＝ +2867 kJ/mol（吸能，所以要光）； 葡萄糖完全氧化把氧变回水，−2870。 ⚠ 两个数几乎相等，正负号相反 —— 说出这一点比背两个数强。
  13. 从原理走到药 — 过渡态那一条 酶对过渡态的亲和力最高 → 所以长得像过渡态的分子是最强的抑制剂 → 他汀就是 HMG-CoA 还原酶的竞争性抑制剂。 ⚠ 一句原理接一个药名，是很漂亮的收尾。

Say this

A few rules tie most of metabolism together. The biotin-dependent carboxylases all need the same three things — ATP, biotin and CO₂ — and there are three of them: pyruvate carboxylase, acetyl-CoA carboxylase and propionyl-CoA carboxylase. Phosphorylation generally means the fasting state: it switches breakdown on and synthesis off, which is why it activates glycogen phosphorylase but inactivates glycogen synthase, acetyl-CoA carboxylase and pyruvate dehydrogenase. Citrate is both cargo and signal: it carries acetyl units out of the mitochondrion, and at the same time it inhibits phosphofructokinase and activates acetyl-CoA carboxylase. Malonyl-CoA does the mirror image: it is the first product of synthesis and it blocks carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1), so synthesis and oxidation can never run together. And underneath all of it: control always sits at the irreversible step, because that is the only place where the enzyme, rather than the concentrations, decides the flux.

约 55 秒 · 这一段可以当「代谢调控」那道题的开场，也可以当任何代谢题的收尾

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Ryšlavá

① 各条代谢途径在葡萄糖-6-磷酸、丙酮酸、乙酰 CoA、草酰乙酸这四个分子上相连 four junction molecules

The pathways are connected through four molecules: glucose-6-phosphate, pyruvate, acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate, and each of them can go into more than one pathway.

四个连接分子。

② 糖、脂肪和大多数氨基酸都分解成乙酰 CoA，再由柠檬酸循环和呼吸链氧化 convergence on acetyl-CoA

Of these four, acetyl-CoA is where sugar, fat and most amino acids all end up, so a single citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) and respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) can oxidise all of them.

三类燃料共用一套氧化酶。

③ 乙酰 CoA 不能变回丙酮酸，所以糖能变成脂肪，脂肪酸的碳不能变成糖 pyruvate dehydrogenase is irreversible

Because pyruvate dehydrogenase cannot run backwards, acetyl-CoA never becomes pyruvate again, so sugar can become fat, but fatty-acid carbons cannot become glucose.

原因在 PDH 不可逆。

④ 饭后胰岛素让细胞分解葡萄糖、合成糖原，多余的转成脂肪 fed state · insulin

That is why a meal ends up partly as fat: after eating, insulin makes cells take up and break down glucose, build glycogen, and convert the surplus into fatty acids.

胰岛素促进储存。

⑤ 空腹时胰高血糖素让肝先分解糖原，再做糖异生，同时分解脂肪，时间长了产生酮体 fasting · glucagon

In fasting, glucagon reverses this: the liver first breaks down glycogen, then makes glucose by gluconeogenesis, fat tissue releases fatty acids, and in a long fast the liver makes ketone bodies for the brain.

顺序：糖原 → 糖异生 → 脂肪酸 → 酮体。

⑥ Cori 循环：剧烈运动缺氧时肌肉产生乳酸，乳酸到肝里重新变成葡萄糖 Cori cycle

The Cori cycle (also called lactic acid cycle) belongs to a third state, hard exercise: muscle runs short of oxygen and makes lactate, which travels to the liver and is turned back into glucose.

名字「Cori cycle（= Cori cycle = lactic acid cycle）」书上没有。

⑦ 肝能向血液释放葡萄糖，肌肉不能；脑主要用葡萄糖；脂肪组织储存和释放脂肪 organ specialisation

These states look different in each organ because organs have different enzymes: the liver can release glucose into the blood, muscle cannot, the brain uses mainly glucose, and adipose tissue stores and releases fat.

器官的差别来自酶的差别。

⑧ 合成与分解不同时进行：同一个信号开一个、关另一个，而且常在不同区室 reciprocal regulation

Inside one cell, synthesis and breakdown of the same molecule are never run at the same time: one signal switches one on and the other off, and often they are in different compartments, such as fatty-acid oxidation in the mitochondria and fatty-acid synthesis in the cytosol.

反向调控 ＋ 不同区室。

⑨ 草酰乙酸被拿走后要补充，植物里 PEP 羧化酶和苹果酸酶也做这件事 ⭐ Ryšlavá anaplerosis

Returning to oxaloacetate: when it is withdrawn for gluconeogenesis it must be replaced by anaplerotic reactions, and in plants PEP carboxylase and malic enzyme, known from C4 photosynthesis, also do this in non-photosynthetic cells.

Ryšlavá 研究的就是这几个酶。

⑩ 书外：葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环把肌肉的氮以丙氨酸形式运到肝 glucose–alanine cycle

The glucose–alanine cycle (also called Cahill cycle) runs next to the Cori cycle: muscle sends amino groups to the liver as alanine, and the liver sends glucose back. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "So no part of a fat can become glucose?" — The glycerol can; only the fatty-acid carbons cannot. · 甘油能，脂肪酸不能。
  • "Why can muscle glycogen not raise blood glucose?" — Muscle lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, so it cannot release free glucose. · 肌肉没有葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。
  • "Why do you get fat from eating too much bread?" — Extra glucose becomes acetyl-CoA, and acetyl-CoA becomes fatty acids. · 多余的糖变成脂肪酸。

定义

  • insulin — hormone released after a meal that promotes uptake and storage · 胰岛素
  • glucagon — hormone released in fasting that makes the liver release glucose · 胰高血糖素
  • glycogen — branched glucose polymer stored in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • compartment — a separate space in the cell, such as the cytosol or the mitochondrial matrix · 区室
  • anaplerotic reaction — a reaction that replaces citric acid cycle intermediates · 回补反应
  • glucose-6-phosphatase — enzyme that removes the phosphate so glucose can leave the cell · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶

The ledger: what each pathway makes, and where it goes

卡 gly · 24 · 25 · 23 · 32 · 一张表管到底：在哪儿 · 要不要氧 · 产什么 · 产物去哪儿

  1. ⚠ 先记住一句话：整条主干上，真正用到氧的只有一步 氧只在呼吸链的最后一步被用掉，它在那里接走电子、变成水。 除此之外，糖酵解、柠檬酸循环、β-氧化本身都不用氧。 那为什么没氧它们全停？因为它们都要 NAD⁺，而把 NADH 变回 NAD⁺ 的只有呼吸链，呼吸链要氧。 所以「需不需要氧」要分两种答法：直接用氧的（只有呼吸链），和离了氧就停的（几乎所有其他的）。 ⚠ 这个区分本身就是考点，被问「柠檬酸循环需要氧吗」时，答「不直接用，但没氧就停」。
  2. CO₂ 和 H₂O 各自从哪儿来 你呼出的 CO₂ 全部来自脱羧反应：丙酮酸脱氢酶一个、柠檬酸循环两个、磷酸戊糖途径一个。 代谢水来自呼吸链——氧接走电子和质子变成水。 ⚠ 常见误答：以为 CO₂ 是在呼吸链产生的。呼吸链不产 CO₂，只产水。
  3. 产物去哪儿，看它是「能量」「碳架」还是「氮」 能量类（NADH、FADH₂ → 呼吸链 → ATP；NADPH 不去呼吸链，留给合成和抗氧化）。 碳架类（乙酰 CoA → 柠檬酸循环烧掉，或出线粒体去做脂肪酸和胆固醇，或在肝里做酮体； 循环的中间物 → 氨基酸、血红素、葡萄糖）。 氮类（氨基酸的氮 → 谷氨酸/谷氨酰胺 → 尿素 → 肾 → 尿）。
  4. 同一个产物，在不同器官去向不同 葡萄糖-6-磷酸：肝里能脱磷酸变成葡萄糖放进血液，肌肉没有那个酶，所以肌肉的糖原只供自己用。 乳酸：肌肉产生，进血液到肝，再变回葡萄糖（Cori 循环）。 酮体：肝里造，但肝自己用不了，送去脑、心和肌肉。 ⚠ 「谁造、谁用」经常是同一道题的第二问。
通路在哪儿要氧吗产什么产物去哪儿
Glycolysis 糖酵解cytosol 胞质不用pyruvate · ATP · NADH丙酮酸 → 线粒体；NADH → 穿梭 → 呼吸链
Fermentation 发酵cytosol无氧时才走lactate 或 ethanol ＋ CO₂目的是把 NAD⁺ 还回去；乳酸 → 肝（Cori）
Pentose phosphate 磷酸戊糖cytosol不用NADPH · ribose-5-P · CO₂NADPH → 合成与抗氧化；核糖 → 核苷酸
PDH 丙酮酸脱氢酶matrix 线粒体基质间接acetyl-CoA · CO₂ · NADH乙酰 CoA → 柠檬酸循环 / 脂肪酸 / 酮体
Citric acid cycle 柠檬酸循环matrix间接CO₂ · NADH · FADH₂ · GTP还原型 → 呼吸链；中间物 → 氨基酸、血红素、糖
β-oxidation β-氧化matrix间接acetyl-CoA · NADH · FADH₂同上；肝里过剩时 → 酮体
Ketogenesis 生酮liver matrix 肝间接acetoacetate · β-hydroxybutyrate进血液 → 脑、心、肌肉（肝自己用不了）
Respiratory chain 呼吸链inner membrane 内膜⚠ 直接用H₂O · 质子梯度 → ATPATP → 全身；这里不产 CO₂
Gluconeogenesis 糖异生liver 胞质＋线粒体耗 ATPglucose进血液 → 脑、红细胞
Glycogen 糖原cytosol（肝、肌）不用glucose-1-P → glucose-6-P肝 → 血糖；肌肉 → 只供自己
Fatty acid synthesis 脂肪酸合成cytosol不用palmitate→ 甘油三酯、磷脂、膜
Amino acid breakdown 氨基酸分解liver 肝间接NH₃ → urea · 碳架尿素 → 肾 → 尿；碳架 → 循环 / 糖 / 酮体
Light reactions 光反应thylakoid 类囊体膜⚠ 产氧O₂ · ATP · NADPH氧放掉；ATP 和 NADPH → 卡尔文循环
Calvin cycle 卡尔文循环stroma 基质不用triose phosphate 三碳糖→ 蔗糖、淀粉

Say this

Across the whole of metabolism, oxygen is used directly at only one point: at the end of the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain), where it accepts the electrons and becomes water. Glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway), the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) and fatty acid oxidation (also called β-oxidation) do not use oxygen themselves, but they all stop without it, because they all need NAD⁺, and only the respiratory chain turns NADH back into NAD⁺. The carbon dioxide we breathe out comes from decarboxylations — one in pyruvate dehydrogenase, two in each turn of the citric acid cycle — not from the respiratory chain. As for where the products go: NADH and FADH₂ go to the chain to make ATP, while NADPH does not — it is kept for biosynthesis and antioxidant defence. Acetyl-CoA is either oxidised in the cycle, or carried out of the mitochondrion as citrate to build fatty acids and cholesterol, or turned into ketone bodies in the liver. And the nitrogen of amino acids ends up as urea, which the kidney excretes.

约 60 秒 · 「只有一步直接用氧」和「CO₂ 不是呼吸链产的」是两个区分度最高的点

Glycolysis

卡 gly · 十步分两相：前五步花钱，后五步赚钱

  1. 什么时候跑 — 一直在跑 饭后血糖高的时候最快；缺氧的时候它是唯一的能量来源； 红细胞一辈子只能靠它（没有线粒体）。在胞质，不需要氧。
  2. 投入相（第 1–5 步）— 先花 2 个 ATP 磷酸化 → 异构 → 再磷酸化 → 劈开 → 互换 glucose →[hexokinase|ATP] glucose-6-P →[phosphoglucose isomerase] fructose-6-P →[PFK-1|ATP · 限速] fructose-1,6-bisP →[aldolase] DHAP ＋ GAP DHAP 再由 triose phosphate isomerase 变成 GAP，所以一个葡萄糖给出两个 GAP。 ⚠ 为什么一上来要花钱：磷酸基带负电，把葡萄糖锁在细胞里出不去； 而且拉低胞内的游离葡萄糖浓度，进来的梯度才维持得住。
  3. 回收相（第 6–10 步）— 每个三碳赚 2 个 ATP 氧化装磷 → 给 ATP → 挪磷 → 脱水 → 再给 ATP GAP →[GAPDH|NAD⁺ ＋ Pᵢ] 1,3-bisphosphoglycerate →[phosphoglycerate kinase|→ATP] 3-phosphoglycerate →[phosphoglycerate mutase] 2-phosphoglycerate →[enolase|−H₂O] PEP →[pyruvate kinase|→ATP] pyruvate ⚠ GAPDH 是全程唯一的氧化步，它把无机磷装上去做成高能的酰基磷酸， 能量是从这里进来的，后面两次给 ATP 都是靠它。
  4. 三个关卡 — 全是 kinase，全不可逆 hexokinase（肝里是 glucokinase：Km 高、不被 G6P 抑制，饭后才大量吃糖）· PFK-1（最核心：ATP 和柠檬酸关它，AMP、ADP 和果糖-2,6-二磷酸开它）· pyruvate kinase（被果糖-1,6-二磷酸前馈激活；肝型被磷酸化关掉）。
  5. 分叉 — 丙酮酸三条去路，取决于有没有氧 有氧 → 进线粒体，PDH 做成 acetyl-CoA。 缺氧（动物） → 乳酸。缺氧（酵母） → 乙醇 ＋ CO₂。 ⚠ 后两条的目的不是产能，是再生 NAD⁺ —— 没有 NAD⁺，GAPDH 那一步就停， 整条路卡死。这一句被问到的频率很高。
  6. 别的糖从哪儿插进来 果糖（肝里经果糖激酶和醛缩酶 B 进到三碳那一层）· 半乳糖（经三个酶变成 G1P 再进）· 糖原（磷酸解直接给 G1P → G6P，省掉第一个激酶）。
  7. 总账 无氧：净 2 ATP ＋ 2 乳酸（毛 4 净 2）。 有氧：你的书说 38，现代教材 30–32。 ⚠ 差在两处：每个 NADH 折算 3 还是 2.5；以及胞质的两个 NADH 靠哪条穿梭进线粒体 （见呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）那一段）—— 这就是 30 和 32 的区别。

Say this

Glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) runs in the cytosol and needs no oxygen. It has ten steps in two phases. The first five spend two ATP — phosphorylation, isomerisation, a second phosphorylation, cleavage, and interconversion of the two trioses. The phosphate keeps the sugar inside the cell and keeps the gradient for uptake. The second five recover four, so the net is two ATP and two NADH per glucose. The only oxidation is at glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate (also called GAP, G3P) dehydrogenase, and that is where the energy enters. Three steps are irreversible and all three are kinases: hexokinase, phosphofructokinase-1, which is the main control point, and pyruvate kinase. Pyruvate then has three fates: with oxygen it goes to acetyl-CoA; without it, to lactate in animals or ethanol in yeast — and the point of that is to regenerate NAD-plus, without which the pathway would stop.

约 55 秒 · 「再生 NAD⁺」那一句别漏

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Martínek

① 从细菌到人几乎所有细胞都有糖酵解，它不需要氧，也不需要线粒体 universal pathway

Almost every cell, from bacteria to humans, has glycolysis, and it needs neither oxygen nor mitochondria, so it is thought to be one of the oldest pathways.

不用氧、不用线粒体。

② 底物水平磷酸化：糖酵解直接把高能中间物上的磷酸转给 ADP substrate-level phosphorylation

Because it works without oxygen, glycolysis makes ATP by substrate-level phosphorylation, the direct transfer of a phosphate onto ADP: phosphoglycerate kinase and pyruvate kinase move a phosphate from a high-energy intermediate directly onto ADP.

与氧化磷酸化对比。

③ Buchner 证明无细胞的酵母提取液也能发酵，Harden 和 Young 在里面发现了第一个辅酶 NAD discovery

This pathway is also where biochemistry started: Buchner showed that a cell-free (also called cell-free protein synthesis, CFPS, in vitro transcription–translation) yeast extract ferments sugar, and Harden and Young then found the first coenzyme, NAD, in that extract.

两件都在书 p009 的表里。

④ 酵母把丙酮酸变成乙醇和 CO₂，所以面包发起来、酒里有酒精；肌肉则产生乳酸 fermentation

In yeast, pyruvate is turned into ethanol and CO₂, which is why bread rises and wine contains alcohol, while working muscle turns pyruvate into lactate.

三个生活问题同一个答案。

⑤ 乳酸脱氢酶有多种同工酶，心肌和骨骼肌的不一样 LDH isoenzymes

Lactate dehydrogenase, the enzyme that makes lactate, exists as isoenzymes (also called isozyme) built from heart and muscle subunits, so heart and skeletal muscle handle lactate differently.

书上同工酶（= isoenzyme = isozyme）的标准例子（p064）。

⑥ 巴斯德效应：有氧时细胞消耗的葡萄糖少得多 Pasteur effect

The Pasteur effect is that yeast or muscle uses much less glucose when oxygen is present, because each glucose then gives far more ATP.

名字是通用知识。

⑦ 糖酵解的中间物通向糖原、磷酸戊糖途径、甘油和氨基酸，在肝里还能反向做糖异生 links to other pathways

Glycolysis is also a source of building blocks: its intermediates lead to glycogen, the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway), glycerol for fats and alanine and serine, and in the liver the pathway runs in reverse as gluconeogenesis.

说到哪就接到哪个主题。

⑧ 氰化物不停糖酵解；血糖采血管里加氟化物，是为了抑制烯醇化酶 cyanide · fluoride

Cyanide, a poison, blocks the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain), not glycolysis, but glycolysis alone cannot supply the brain and heart; and fluoride, added to blood-glucose tubes, inhibits enolase, so red cells stop consuming the glucose before it is measured.

两个都是通用知识。

⑨ 己糖激酶结合葡萄糖后才闭合，这是诱导契合的经典例子 ⭐ Martínek induced fit

Hexokinase, the first enzyme, closes around glucose only after glucose binds, which is the classic example of induced fit, and this closure stops it from simply hydrolysing ATP.

Martínek 做结合口袋和构象变化。

⑩ 书外：Warburg 效应是很多癌细胞在有氧时仍把葡萄糖发酵成乳酸 Warburg effect

The Warburg effect is the exception to the Pasteur effect: many cancer cells ferment glucose to lactate even with oxygen, and PET scans detect tumours by their high glucose uptake. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Glycolysis（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway） is anaerobic — so it runs only without oxygen?" — No, it runs all the time; it simply does not need oxygen. · 一直在跑，只是不需要氧。
  • "Is glucose oxidised to CO₂ in glycolysis?" — No, only to pyruvate; the CO₂ is released later. · 只到丙酮酸。
  • "Is lactate a waste product?" — No, the liver converts it back to glucose. · 肝会把它变回葡萄糖。

定义

  • substrate-level phosphorylation — making ATP by transferring a phosphate from a metabolite directly to ADP · 底物水平磷酸化
  • coenzyme — a small organic molecule an enzyme needs to work · 辅酶
  • isoenzymes — different forms of an enzyme that catalyse the same reaction · 同工酶
  • Pasteur effect — lower glucose consumption in the presence of oxygen · 巴斯德效应
  • enolase — glycolytic enzyme that removes water from 2-phosphoglycerate · 烯醇化酶
  • induced fit — the enzyme changes shape when the substrate binds · 诱导契合
  • PET scan — imaging that detects a radioactively labelled glucose analogue · 正电子发射断层扫描

Pentose phosphate pathway

⚠ 她说没碰过的那一批 · 两段，第一段不可逆、第二段可逆

  1. 氧化段 — 两次脱氢，掉一个碳（不可逆）glucose-6-P →[G6PD|NADPH ①] 6-phosphogluconolactone →[lactonase|＋H₂O] 6-phosphogluconate →[6PGDH|NADPH ② · −CO₂] ribulose-5-PG6PD 是限速酶。⚠ 它的信号不是 ATP，是 NADP⁺ 够不够——这条最容易答错。
  2. 非氧化段 — 把糖架重排（可逆）核酮糖-5-磷酸可以异构成核糖-5-磷酸（给核酸），也可以经转酮酶（搬 2 碳）和转醛酶（搬 3 碳）重排回果糖-6-磷酸和甘油醛-3-磷酸，接回糖酵解。⚠ 这一段是可逆的，所以细胞可以按需要决定要核糖还是要 NADPH。

Say this

The pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway) runs in the cytosol and has two parts. The oxidative part is irreversible: two dehydrogenations and one decarboxylation give two NADPH, one CO₂ and ribulose-5-phosphate. The control enzyme is glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase, and what controls it is the supply of NADP-plus, not ATP. The non-oxidative part is reversible: transketolase moves two carbons and transaldolase moves three, so the sugars can be rearranged back into fructose-6-phosphate and glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate (also called GAP, G3P). The whole pathway neither makes nor spends ATP. Its two products are NADPH, for reductive synthesis and antioxidant defence, and ribose-5-phosphate, for nucleotides.

约 40 秒 · 「0 ATP」和「看 NADP⁺ 不看 ATP」是两个必说

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Ryšlavá

① 磷酸戊糖途径不产能，它为合成提供 NADPH 和核糖-5-磷酸 a pathway for biosynthesis

The pentose phosphate pathway does not oxidise glucose for energy; it produces NADPH and ribose-5-phosphate for biosynthesis.

两个产物，都用于合成。

② 细胞需要核糖多就只走可逆段，需要 NADPH 多就把多余的糖送回糖酵解 adjustable output

Because the second part is reversible, a cell that needs mainly ribose can make it without the oxidative part, and a cell that needs mainly NADPH sends the surplus sugars back into glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway).

两个产物的比例可以调。

③ 核糖-5-磷酸是核苷酸的糖，分裂的细胞需要大量 nucleotide synthesis

The ribose-5-phosphate becomes the sugar of every nucleotide, so rapidly dividing cells depend on it.

接核酸主题。

④ NADPH 用于合成脂肪酸和胆固醇，所以肝、脂肪组织和肾上腺皮质里这条途径最活跃 active tissues

The NADPH is used to make fatty acids and cholesterol, so the pathway is most active in the liver, adipose tissue and adrenal cortex.

接脂肪合成、胆固醇两个主题。

⑤ NADPH 让谷胱甘肽保持还原态，保护红细胞不被活性氧损伤 glutathione

NADPH also keeps glutathione in its reduced form, and reduced glutathione removes hydrogen peroxide, which is why red blood cells depend on this pathway.

接呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）里的活性氧。

⑥ 书外：G6PD 缺乏症患者吃蚕豆或服用伯氨喹后会溶血，这种缺陷在疟疾流行区常见 G6PD deficiency

This is why glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase deficiency causes haemolysis after eating fava beans or taking primaquine, an antimalarial drug, and the deficiency is common where malaria was, because it gives some protection. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑦ 转酮酶需要硫胺素（维生素 B₁） thiamine

In the reversible part, transketolase needs thiamine diphosphate, made from vitamin B₁, the same cofactor as in pyruvate dehydrogenase.

接维生素、PDH 两个主题。

⑧ 光合作用的 Calvin 循环用同样的转酮酶和醛缩酶反应 Calvin cycle

The same transketolase and aldolase reactions appear in the Calvin cycle (also called Calvin–Benson cycle, dark reactions, light-independent reactions), where plants use them to regenerate ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate, the CO₂ acceptor.

接光合作用主题。

⑨ 细胞色素 P450 氧化药物时，电子来自 NADPH ⭐ Hodek cytochrome P450

NADPH is also the electron source for cytochrome P450, which oxidises drugs and other foreign compounds in the liver, so drug metabolism also depends on this pathway.

Hodek 做的就是 P450。

⑩ 植物受胁迫时活性氧增多，谷胱甘肽和抗坏血酸的再生也靠 NADPH ⭐ Ryšlavá plant oxidative stress

In plants under stress, reactive oxygen species increase, and regenerating reduced glutathione and ascorbate also uses NADPH.

Ryšlavá 做植物抗氧化酶。

陷阱问法

  • "Does its NADPH go into the respiratory chain?" — No, it is used for synthesis and antioxidant defence. · 不进呼吸链。
  • "Is it only for making pentoses?" — No, it can run just to make NADPH and return the sugars to glycolysis（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）. · 也可以只为 NADPH。
  • "Is it the same as the Calvin cycle?" — They share transketolase and aldolase reactions, but the Calvin cycle fixes CO₂. · 共用反应，目的不同。

定义

  • nucleotide — the building block of DNA and RNA · 核苷酸
  • glutathione — a small thiol-containing peptide that removes peroxides · 谷胱甘肽
  • reactive oxygen species — superoxide, hydrogen peroxide and related reactive forms of oxygen · 活性氧
  • haemolysis — breakdown of red blood cells · 溶血
  • transketolase — enzyme that transfers a two-carbon unit between sugars · 转酮酶
  • Calvin cycle — the pathway in which plants fix CO₂ into sugar · 卡尔文循环（= Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions）
  • cytochrome P450 — haem enzymes that oxidise drugs and foreign compounds · 细胞色素 P450

Pyruvate dehydrogenase

糖和脂之间的单行道 · 三个酶、五个辅因子的接力

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有氧、而且细胞要烧糖的时候 它是糖酵解和柠檬酸循环之间的闸门。能量够了就关，不够就开。 在线粒体基质，丙酮酸得先被转运进去。
  2. 一句话总反应 pyruvate ＋ CoA-SH ＋ NAD⁺ →[pyruvate dehydrogenase complex] acetyl-CoA ＋ CO₂ ＋ NADH 氧化脱羧：掉一个碳变 CO₂，剩两个碳挂到 CoA 的硫上，脱下的氢给 NAD⁺。
  3. 三个酶的接力 E1 脱羧（TPP 上去接住，CO₂ 跑掉）→ E2 把那两个碳经硫辛酰胺的摆臂交给 CoA → E3 收拾残局（把硫辛酰胺重新氧化，氢经 FAD 交给 NAD⁺）。
  4. 五个辅因子 — 四个是 B 族维生素 TPP（B₁）· 硫辛酰胺 · CoA（B₅）· FAD（B₂）· NAD⁺（B₃） 口诀 Tender Loving Care For Nancy。 ⚠ α-酮戊二酸脱氢酶用的是同一套，背一次会两个。
  5. 调控 — 两层，方向一致 产物抑制：acetyl-CoA、NADH、ATP 多了就关。 共价修饰：PDH kinase 磷酸化 → 关；磷酸酶去磷酸化 ＋ Ca²⁺ 和 ADP → 开。 ⚠ 磷酸化＝关，跟糖原磷酸化酶正好相反，这个反向爱考。
  6. 为什么它这么要紧 ⚠ 这一步不可逆，所以 acetyl-CoA 回不到丙酮酸。 糖能变脂肪，脂肪变不回糖，断点就在这儿。 ⚠ 缺硫胺素这一步就瘫，丙酮酸和乳酸堆起来，脑子最先垮（脑只烧糖）。

Say this

Pyruvate dehydrogenase is the gate between glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) and the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle), in the mitochondrial matrix, and the reaction is an oxidative decarboxylation: pyruvate gives acetyl-CoA, CO₂ and NADH. It is a complex of three enzymes using five cofactors — TPP, lipoamide, coenzyme A, FAD and NAD-plus — and four of those are B vitamins. It is switched off by its own products, acetyl-CoA and NADH, and also by phosphorylation, which is the opposite direction from glycogen phosphorylase. ⚠ And because this step is irreversible, acetyl-CoA can never go back to pyruvate — that is why fat cannot be converted to glucose.

约 40 秒 · 结尾那句「所以脂肪变不成糖」是整段的落点

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hudeček

① 三个酶组成一个复合体，中间物直接从一个活性中心传到下一个 substrate channelling

Substrate channelling is the reason for the complex: because the three enzymes sit together, the intermediate is passed directly from one active site to the next without being released into solution.

中间物不释放。

② α-酮戊二酸脱氢酶和支链酮酸脱氢酶结构相同 a family of three complexes

The same structure is used by α-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase in the citric acid cycle and by branched-chain α-keto acid dehydrogenase in the breakdown of valine, leucine and isoleucine.

三个复合体，同一种结构。

③ 它产生的乙酰 CoA 进柠檬酸循环，或用于合成脂肪酸、胆固醇、酮体 fates of acetyl-CoA

The acetyl-CoA it produces enters the citric acid cycle, or is used to make fatty acids, cholesterol or ketone bodies.

接四个主题。

④ 丙酮酸在线粒体里还能被丙酮酸羧化酶变成草酰乙酸，用于糖异生 pyruvate carboxylase

Pyruvate in the mitochondrion has a second fate: pyruvate carboxylase turns it into oxaloacetate for gluconeogenesis, and acetyl-CoA activates that enzyme while it inhibits pyruvate dehydrogenase.

乙酰 CoA 让丙酮酸去做糖，而不是去烧。

⑤ 饭后胰岛素使它活化，空腹时它被关掉以节省葡萄糖给脑 fed vs fasted

This choice follows the fed and fasted states: after a meal insulin promotes the active form, and in fasting the complex is switched off so that glucose is kept for the brain.

接代谢整合。

⑥ 酵母发酵时由丙酮酸脱羧酶把丙酮酸变成乙醛，再还原成乙醇 pyruvate decarboxylase

In fermenting yeast, pyruvate goes a different way: pyruvate decarboxylase removes CO₂ to give acetaldehyde, which is reduced to ethanol, using the same thiamine diphosphate but without oxidation.

接糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）和发酵。

⑦ 脚气病是缺硫胺素引起的：这一步停下，乳酸堆积，神经和心脏先受损 beriberi

Because thiamine is essential here, beriberi, the disease of thiamine deficiency, stops this step: lactate accumulates, and the nervous system and heart are damaged first. The name beriberi is general knowledge.

接维生素主题。

⑧ NADH 在 340 nm 有吸收，NAD⁺ 没有，所以用分光光度计测它的活性 ⭐ Hudeček absorbance of NADH

Its activity is measured with a spectrophotometer, because NADH absorbs light at 340 nm and NAD⁺ does not.

Hudeček 做光谱学。

⑨ 书外：二氯乙酸抑制 PDH 激酶，使复合体保持活性 dichloroacetate

Dichloroacetate, a small drug, inhibits pyruvate dehydrogenase kinase, so the complex stays active, and it has been tested for lactic acidosis. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is it part of the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）?" — No, it links glycolysis to the cycle. · 不是循环的一步。
  • "Does phosphorylation switch it on?" — No, phosphorylation switches it off. · 磷酸化是关。
  • "Can acetyl-CoA be made into glucose?" — Not in animals, because this step is irreversible. · 不能，这一步不可逆。

定义

  • substrate channelling — passing an intermediate between active sites without releasing it · 底物通道
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • pyruvate carboxylase — enzyme that adds CO₂ to pyruvate to give oxaloacetate · 丙酮酸羧化酶
  • acetaldehyde — two-carbon aldehyde that yeast reduces to ethanol · 乙醛
  • beriberi — disease caused by thiamine deficiency · 脚气病
  • spectrophotometer — instrument that measures how much light a solution absorbs · 分光光度计
  • lactic acidosis — too much lactate and acid in the blood · 乳酸酸中毒

Citric acid cycle

卡 24 · 汇合点，也是原料库 —— 两个身份都要说

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有氧，而且随时 糖、脂、氨基酸都变成 acetyl-CoA 到这里汇合。在线粒体基质。 ⚠ 循环本身不用氧，但离了氧就停 —— 因为 NADH 要靠呼吸链换回 NAD⁺。
  2. 八步 — 中间物是难点，酶名可以现推 acetyl-CoA ＋ oxaloacetate →[citrate synthase] citrate →[aconitase] isocitrate →[isocitrate dehydrogenase|NAD⁺ · −CO₂] α-ketoglutarate α-ketoglutarate →[α-KG dehydrogenase|NAD⁺ · −CO₂] succinyl-CoA →[succinyl-CoA synthetase|GDP＋Pᵢ] succinate →[succinate dehydrogenase|FAD · 内膜上] fumarate →[fumarase|＋H₂O] malate →[malate dehydrogenase|NAD⁺] oxaloacetate 口诀 Can I Keep Selling Sex For Money, Officer?
  3. 碳怎么走 — 进两个，出两个 4 ＋ 2 ＝ 6 → 掉一个碳 5 → 再掉一个 4。 ⚠ acetyl-CoA 和草酰乙酸是「合体」不是「转化」：OAA 是托盘，转一圈还回来，净增 0。 ⚠ 而且第一圈掉的那两个 CO₂ 不是刚进来的那两个碳。
  4. 账 — 一圈四样 2 CO₂ · 3 NADH · 1 FADH₂ · 1 GTP。 换成 ATP：你的书说 12，现代算法 10。 ⚠ 陷阱问法：「循环产多少 ATP」→ 它直接产的只有 1 个 GTP，其余都是呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）造的。
  5. 三个关卡 ＋ 刹车 citrate synthase · isocitrate dehydrogenase（最关键）· α-KG dehydrogenase。 三个的刹车都是 NADH —— 所以真正决定转速的是呼吸链跑不跑得动。 Ca²⁺ 开其中两个：肌肉一收缩，钙进来，循环立刻提速。
  6. 特殊的一步 — 琥珀酸脱氢酶 ⚠ 循环里唯一长在内膜上的酶，而且它就是呼吸链复合体 Ⅱ。 它不用 NAD⁺ 用 FAD，被结构类似的丙二酸竞争性抑制。
  7. 两用性 — 它同时是原料库 中间物随时被抽走去做别的：α-酮戊二酸 → 谷氨酸· 草酰乙酸 → 天冬氨酸、糖异生· 琥珀酰-CoA → 血红素· 柠檬酸 → 出线粒体做脂肪酸。 ⚠ 抽走了就得补回来，这叫回补反应（anaplerotic）， 最主要的一个是 pyruvate carboxylase（丙酮酸 ＋ CO₂ → 草酰乙酸）。 这个词书上有（p140）。

Say this

The citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) runs in the mitochondrial matrix and it is the convergence point — carbohydrate, fat and amino acids all arrive as acetyl-CoA. Each turn gives two CO₂, three NADH, one FADH₂ and one GTP. ⚠ Note that the cycle itself makes only that one GTP directly; everything else is made later by the chain. Three steps are irreversible: citrate synthase, isocitrate dehydrogenase, which is the main control point, and α-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase, and all three are braked by NADH. Succinate dehydrogenase is the only membrane-bound enzyme of the cycle and it is respiratory Complex II (also called succinate dehydrogenase). And the cycle is amphibolic: its intermediates are constantly withdrawn for biosynthesis, so they have to be replaced by anaplerotic reactions, mainly pyruvate carboxylase.

约 50 秒 · 「只直接产 1 个 GTP」和「两用性」是两个区分度高的点

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hodek

① 糖、脂肪、蛋白质最后都在这里烧，它的中间物又被拿去合成别的分子 amphibolic

Sugar, fat and protein are all broken down to acetyl-CoA and burned here, and at the same time the cycle's intermediates are taken away to build amino acids, fatty acids, glucose and haem, so the cycle is called amphibolic.

amphibolic ＝ 既分解又合成。

② 草酰乙酸每转一圈都重新生成，所以少量就能一直转 oxaloacetate is regenerated

It is a cycle because oxaloacetate, which accepts the acetyl group at the start, is made again at the end of every turn, so a small amount keeps the cycle running.

这就是它叫「循环」的原因。

③ 饥饿时肝把草酰乙酸拿去做葡萄糖，乙酰 CoA 进不了循环，就变成酮体 ketone bodies

This is also why fasting changes the liver: oxaloacetate is used for gluconeogenesis, so acetyl-CoA cannot enter the cycle and is turned into ketone bodies, which the brain can use as fuel.

一句话接上糖异生和酮体两个主题。

④ 产生的 NADH 和 FADH₂ 交给呼吸链，大部分 ATP 在那里做，所以没氧就停 respiratory chain

The NADH and FADH₂ it produces are passed to the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain), which makes most of the ATP, so without oxygen the cycle stops.

循环本身不用氧，但 NADH 交不出去就停。

⑤ 细胞能量多就变慢，能量少就变快 regulation

Because of that link, the cycle slows down when ATP and NADH are high and speeds up when ADP and calcium rise, for example in working muscle.

一句话讲完调控。

⑥ 真核细胞在线粒体基质里跑，细菌在细胞质里跑 location

In eukaryotes it runs in the mitochondrial matrix; bacteria have no mitochondria, so they run it in the cytoplasm.

几乎所有用氧的生物都有它。

⑦ 植物和细菌还有乙醛酸循环，能把脂肪变成糖，动物没有 glyoxylate cycle

Plants and bacteria also have the glyoxylate cycle, which skips the two steps that release CO₂, so they can turn fat into sugar; animals lack it, so we cannot make glucose from fatty acids.

常考：动物不能把脂肪净转成葡萄糖。

⑧ Krebs 1937 年用鸽子胸肌发现，1953 年获诺贝尔奖 discovery

Hans Krebs described it in 1937 using pigeon breast muscle: a small amount of citrate raised oxygen use far more than it could account for, which showed it was being reused. He received the Nobel Prize in 1953.

加一点点柠檬酸，耗氧量涨得远超它本身 → 它在被反复使用。

⑨ 氟乙酸本身无毒，体内被转成氟柠檬酸后堵住循环 ⭐ Hodek fluoroacetate · lethal synthesis

Fluoroacetate, a rat poison, is harmless itself, but the cell converts it into fluorocitrate, which blocks the cycle; this is called lethal synthesis, and a foreign compound becoming more toxic after metabolism is the central idea of xenobiotic metabolism.

Hodek 做的就是外源物代谢。

⑩ 书外：某些脑肿瘤里异柠檬酸脱氢酶突变，产生 2-羟基戊二酸，改变基因表达 oncometabolite

In some brain tumours a mutated isocitrate dehydrogenase produces 2-hydroxyglutarate, which changes DNA and histone methylation and so gene expression. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Does the cycle need oxygen?" — Not directly, but it stops without it, because NADH cannot be reoxidised. · 不直接用，没氧就停。
  • "How much ATP does it make?" — Directly only one GTP per turn; the rest is made by the respiratory chain. · 直接只有 1 个 GTP。
  • "Can fat become glucose?" — Not in animals; plants can, with the glyoxylate cycle. · 动物不能，植物能。

定义

  • amphibolic — both breaks down and builds up · 两用性
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • glyoxylate cycle — a variant of the cycle in plants and bacteria that skips the two CO₂-releasing steps · 乙醛酸循环
  • lethal synthesis — the body's own enzymes turning a harmless compound into a poison · 致死合成
  • xenobiotic — a foreign compound, such as a drug or poison · 外源物

Respiratory chain & oxidative phosphorylation

卡 25 · 两件事，只靠质子梯度连着

  1. 什么时候跑 — 只要有氧和还原型载体 前面所有路交上来的 NADH 和 FADH₂ 都在这里兑现。在线粒体内膜上。 ⚠ 真正限制速度的不是氧，是 ADP —— 没有 ADP 可磷酸化，链就慢下来（呼吸控制）。
  2. 电子从哪进 — 两个入口 NADH 从复合体 Ⅰ 进；FADH₂ 从复合体 Ⅱ 进（琥珀酸脱氢酶就是它）。 ⚠ Ⅱ 不泵质子，所以从这儿进的电子少推一段，FADH₂ 才比 NADH 值钱少。
  3. 四个复合体 ＋ 两个搬运工 Ⅰ → Q → Ⅲ → cyt c → Ⅳ → O₂ 辅基：Ⅰ FMN · Ⅱ FAD · Ⅲ b/c₁ · Ⅳ a/a₃＋Cu（口诀 Fat Frogs Bite Ankles）。 ⚠ 泛醌 Q 是唯一的非蛋白组分，脂溶性，带 2 电子 ＋ 2 质子； cyt c 是内膜外侧的外周蛋白，一次只带 1 个电子。
  4. 泵出来多少质子 现代：Ⅰ 泵 4 ＋ Ⅲ 泵 4 ＋ Ⅳ 泵 2 ＝ 每个 NADH 10 个；FADH₂ 只走后两段，6 个。 你的书：Ⅳ 不算泵，每个 NADH 6 个 ≈ 3 ATP，FADH₂ 4 个 ≈ 2 ATP。 ⚠ 两套都要会，而且要说得出差在哪。
  5. ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase） — 一台被质子推着转的机器 F₀ 是嵌在膜里的质子通道，质子流过去让它转；F₁ 是伸进基质的头， 转动改变三个催化位点的构象，把 ADP ＋ Pᵢ 挤成 ATP。 ⚠ 每个 ATP 要 4 个质子：3 个用来转，第 4 个用来把磷酸运进基质。 这就是 10 ÷ 4 ＝ 2.5 的来历。
  6. 胞质的 NADH 怎么进来 — 两条穿梭，而且值钱不一样 NADH 本身过不了内膜，糖酵解造的那两个得换个方式进去： 苹果酸-天冬氨酸穿梭（肝、心）→ 在基质里还原出 NADH，值 2.5； 甘油磷酸穿梭（肌肉、脑）→ 交给 FAD，变成 FADH₂，只值 1.5。 ⚠ 这就是「一个葡萄糖 30 还是 32」的答案 —— 看用哪条穿梭。
  7. 毒物三类 — 分清楚才不会答反 ① 堵复合体：鱼藤酮 Ⅰ · 抗霉素 Ⅲ · 氰化物 / CO / 叠氮 Ⅳ → 链停，ATP 也停。 ② 解偶联（DNP、褐色脂肪的 UCP）：质子漏回去 → ATP 停，但链跑得更快、耗氧更多，能量变热。 ③ 堵合酶（寡霉素）：质子出不去、淤积 → ATP 和链一起停。 ⚠ ②和③的对比是最常考的一对。
