  8. ⭐ P/O ratio — 「一个 NADH 等于三个 ATP」说的就是这个数 P/O ＝ phosphorus to oxygen ratio（磷氧比）：每还原一个氧原子，能做出几个 ATP。 你的书（p146）给的是：每个 NADH 泵 6 个质子 ≈ 3 ATP，每个 FADH₂ 泵 4 个 ≈ 2 ATP， 总式写成 NADH ＋ H⁺ ＋ ½ O₂ ＋ 3 ADP ＋ 3 Pᵢ → NAD⁺ ＋ 3 ATP ＋ 4 H₂O ⚠ 现代的数是 2.5 和 1.5（每个 NADH 泵 10 个质子，合酶转一圈要 4 个——三个推转子、一个把磷酸运进来）。 所以这个比值不是整数，而「3 和 2」是老教材的取整。 ⚠ 两个数都要会说，而且要说清哪个是你书上的：先给书的 3 和 2，被追问再给 2.5 和 1.5 并说明为什么不是整数。
  9. ⭐ 干扰这条链只有两种方式，答反了就全错 一、抑制剂（inhibitor）——把链堵住。堵住之后耗氧停、ATP 也停， 上游全部停在还原态、下游全部停在氧化态。记法 R-A-C 堵 1-3-4： 鱼藤酮 rotenone 堵复合体 Ⅰ · 抗霉素 A antimycin A 堵 Ⅲ · 氰化物和一氧化碳堵 Ⅳ； 丙二酸 malonate 竞争性抑制琥珀酸脱氢酶（Ⅱ）；寡霉素 oligomycin 堵的不是链，是 ATP 合酶。 二、解偶联剂（uncoupler）——把梯度漏掉。2,4-二硝基苯酚 2,4-dinitrophenol 带着质子直接穿回基质， 不走合酶。结果是链跑得更快、耗氧更多，但一个 ATP 都不产，能量全变成热。 ⚠ 「解偶联」不等于「抑制」：抑制是耗氧停，解偶联是耗氧反而增加。这一句最容易答反。 棕色脂肪里的解偶联蛋白 thermogenin（UCP1）做的就是同一件事，那是生理性的产热。
  10. ⚠ 寡霉素那个实验 — 一句话证明「耦合的是梯度」 寡霉素只堵合酶，可是电子传递也跟着停了——因为质子出不去、梯度顶住了泵。 这时候加一个解偶联剂，电子传递立刻重新跑起来，但不产 ATP。 ⚠ 所以把两者连在一起的既不是酶、也不是某个化学中间物，而是质子梯度本身—— 这正是 Mitchell 化学渗透学说的证据。这一条书里一个字都没有，说的时候标 general。 「链只做一件事：把电子的落差换成膜外的质子。合酶只做一件事：把质子放回来换 ATP。」

Say this

The respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) and ATP synthesis are two separate things, coupled only through the proton gradient — that is chemiosmosis, and no chemical intermediate passes between them. Electrons from NADH enter at Complex I and from FADH₂ at Complex II (also called succinate dehydrogenase), which pumps no protons, and that is why FADH₂ is worth less. They pass along ubiquinone (also called coenzyme Q, CoQ), Complex III, cytochrome c and Complex IV (also called cytochrome c oxidase) to oxygen. On the modern count, ten protons are pumped per NADH and six per FADH₂, and the synthase needs four protons per ATP — three to turn it and one to bring the phosphate in — which gives 2.5 and 1.5. My own textbook counts six protons and about three ATP instead. ⚠ Cytosolic NADH cannot cross the membrane, so it enters by a shuttle: the malate–aspartate shuttle delivers NADH and the glycerol phosphate shuttle delivers FADH₂, and that is why a glucose is worth thirty or thirty-two depending on the tissue.

约 60 秒 · 穿梭那一句是这一段新加的重点

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hudeček、Martínková

① 电子经过多个载体逐步传给氧，每一步释放的能量才能被利用 stepwise transfer

Electrons pass from NADH to oxygen through several carriers, so the energy is released in several smaller steps, and each step can be coupled to proton pumping.

不是一步放完。

② 氧是最终电子受体，被还原成水，所以我们必须呼吸 oxygen as final acceptor

At the end of the chain, oxygen accepts the electrons because it has the most positive redox potential, and it is reduced to water; this is why we need to breathe.

「为什么要呼吸氧」。

③ 每个载体有自己的氧化还原电位，电子从电位低的流向电位高的 redox potential

The order of the carriers follows their redox potentials: electrons always move from the carrier with the more negative potential to the one with the more positive potential.

电位决定顺序。

④ 细胞色素有颜色，阻断一步后用分光光度计能看出哪些载体停在还原态 ⭐ Hudeček spectroscopy of cytochromes

The order was confirmed experimentally because cytochromes absorb visible light: when one step is blocked, a spectrophotometer shows the carriers before it reduced and those after it oxidised.

Hudeček 做光谱学。

⑤ 复合体 IV 的氧结合在血红素铁上，一氧化碳和氰化物也结合在这里 ⭐ Martínková haem and gases

In Complex IV, cytochrome c oxidase, oxygen binds to a haem iron, and carbon monoxide and cyanide are toxic because they bind at the same site.

Martínková 研究气体结合血红素。

⑥ 电子提前漏出会生成超氧阴离子，主要在复合体 I 和 III reactive oxygen species

Sometimes an electron leaks out early, mainly at Complexes I and III, and reduces oxygen to superoxide; superoxide dismutase, catalase and glutathione peroxidase then remove it.

清除最终靠 NADPH，接 PPP。

⑦ 线粒体内膜折叠成嵴，增加了呼吸链和 ATP 合酶的面积 cristae

The chain and ATP synthase (also called Complex V, F₀F₁-ATPase) sit in the inner membrane, which is folded into cristae to give a much larger surface area.

「内膜为什么折叠」。

⑧ 线粒体来自细菌，所以有自己的 DNA，编码部分呼吸链蛋白 mitochondrial DNA

Mitochondria descend from bacteria by endosymbiosis, which is why they have their own DNA coding for some subunits of the chain.

接细胞器、遗传主题。

⑨ 叶绿体也用电子传递链建立质子梯度，驱动 ATP 合酶 photosynthesis

Chloroplasts use the same principle: an electron transport chain in the thylakoid membrane builds a proton gradient that drives ATP synthase, but the electrons come from water, energised by light.

接光合作用主题。

⑩ 书外：化学渗透学说由 Peter Mitchell 提出，并获诺贝尔奖 Peter Mitchell

The idea that the proton gradient links the chain to ATP synthesis, chemiosmosis, was proposed by Peter Mitchell, who received the Nobel Prize for it. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书写了原理，没写名字。

⑪ ⭐ P/O 比就是每还原一个氧原子做出几个 ATP，书给 3 和 2，现代给 2.5 和 1.5 P/O ratio

The P/O ratio, the phosphorus to oxygen ratio, is how many ATP are made per oxygen atom reduced. My textbook gives three for NADH and two for FADH₂; the modern values are 2.5 and 1.5, because the proton counts do not divide evenly.

先给书的 3 和 2，被追问再给 2.5 和 1.5。

⑫ ⭐ 抑制剂把链堵住：耗氧停，ATP 也停 rotenone · antimycin A · cyanide · oligomycin

Rotenone blocks Complex I, antimycin A blocks Complex III, and cyanide and carbon monoxide block Complex IV, while oligomycin blocks not the chain but ATP synthase. With any of them, oxygen consumption and ATP synthesis both stop.

R-A-C 堵 1-3-4；寡霉素堵的是合酶。

⑬ ⭐ 解偶联剂把梯度漏掉：耗氧反而增加，但不产 ATP 2,4-dinitrophenol · thermogenin

2,4-dinitrophenol, an uncoupler, carries protons back across the membrane without passing through ATP synthase, so the chain runs faster and uses more oxygen while making no ATP, and the energy is released as heat. Thermogenin in brown fat does the same thing physiologically.

⚠ 解偶联 ≠ 抑制：一个耗氧增加，一个耗氧停。

⑭ ⭐ 书外：寡霉素停住电子传递，加解偶联剂又跑起来——耦合的是梯度本身 respiratory control

Oligomycin blocks only the synthase, yet electron transport stops as well, because the gradient builds up against the pumps. Add an uncoupler and transport restarts without any ATP being made, which shows that what couples them is the proton gradient itself. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

化学渗透学说的证据。

陷阱问法

  • "So the respiratory chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain） makes the ATP?" — No, it makes the proton gradient; ATP synthase makes the ATP. · 链建梯度，合酶做 ATP。
  • "Is oxygen burned?" — No, it is reduced to water. · 氧被还原成水。
  • "Does NADPH enter the chain?" — No, NADPH is used for biosynthesis. · 不进。

定义

  • redox potential — how strongly a molecule attracts electrons · 氧化还原电位
  • cytochrome — haem protein that carries electrons by changing the charge of its iron · 细胞色素
  • cytochrome c oxidase — Complex IV（= Complex IV = cytochrome c oxidase）, which passes electrons to oxygen · 细胞色素 c 氧化酶
  • superoxide — oxygen that has taken one extra electron · 超氧阴离子
  • cristae — folds of the inner mitochondrial membrane · 嵴
  • endosymbiosis — one cell living inside another; the origin of mitochondria · 内共生
  • thylakoid membrane — the internal membrane of the chloroplast · 类囊体膜
  • chemiosmosis — using a proton gradient across a membrane to make ATP · 化学渗透

Fatty acid oxidation

⚠ 她说太短的那一段，补全了 · 三步进门，一个循环，三个分叉

  1. 什么时候跑 — 空腹、长时间运动、饥饿 脂肪酸是身体最主要的能量来源，尤其是空腹的时候。 血糖一低，胰岛素降、胰高血糖素和肾上腺素升， 激素敏感性脂肪酶（HSL）把脂肪组织里的甘油三酯拆开， 放出游离脂肪酸，靠白蛋白在血里运到肌肉和肝。 ⚠ 拆下来的甘油另走一路：去肝里做糖异生。 ⚠ 而脑用不了脂肪酸 —— 这正是酮体存在的理由： 肝把脂肪酸烧出的 acetyl-CoA 改装成酮体送给脑。
  2. 活化 — 在胞质，先做成硫酯 fatty acid ＋ CoA ＋ ATP →[acyl-CoA synthetase] acyl-CoA ＋ AMP ＋ PPᵢ ⚠ ATP 断成 AMP，不是 ADP，焦磷酸随后又被水解掉 —— 所以这一步的代价是 两个高能磷酸键，不是一个。书说得很明白：游离脂肪酸进不了任何代谢通路，合成和分解都得先活化。
  3. 肉碱穿梭 — 真正的限速门 长链酰基-CoA 过不了线粒体内膜。外膜上的 CPT-1 把酰基换到肉碱上， translocase 把它换进去，内侧的 CPT-2 再换回 CoA。 ⚠ CPT-1 是整条路的限速步，而它被丙二酰-CoA 关掉 —— 那是脂肪酸合成的第一个产物。 合成一开，氧化的门就锁上。⚠ 中短链脂肪酸不用这道门，直接就进去了。
  4. 四步循环 — 氧 · 水 · 氧 · 切 acyl-CoA →[acyl-CoA dehydrogenase|FAD] trans-Δ²-enoyl-CoA →[enoyl-CoA hydratase|＋H₂O] 3-hydroxyacyl-CoA →[3-hydroxyacyl-CoA dehydrogenase|NAD⁺] 3-ketoacyl-CoA →[thiolase|＋CoA] acetyl-CoA ＋ acyl-CoA（短两个碳） 每轮出 1 FADH₂ ＋ 1 NADH ＋ 1 acetyl-CoA，轮数 ＝ 碳数 ÷ 2 − 1。 ⚠ 白拿的加分：前三步跟柠檬酸循环最后三步是同一套化学（脱氢 → 加水 → 再脱氢）。
  5. 分叉一 · 奇数碳 — 最后剩三个碳 偶数碳链切到最后正好是两个 acetyl-CoA，干净利落。 奇数碳链最后一轮剩下的是丙酰-CoA（3 碳），它得另走一条路： propionyl-CoA →[propionyl-CoA carboxylase|ABC] methylmalonyl-CoA →[methylmalonyl-CoA mutase|B₁₂] succinyl-CoA ⚠ 琥珀酰-CoA 是柠檬酸循环的中间物，能变成草酰乙酸，所以能做糖。 这是脂肪酸里唯一生糖的那一小块 —— 而「脂肪不能变成糖」的大原则还是对的， 因为那说的是偶数碳链给出的 acetyl-CoA。 ⚠ 顺带：这里是 B₁₂ 在代谢里露面的两处之一。
  6. 分叉二 · 超长链（C > 22）— 先去过氧化物酶体 线粒体处理不了太长的链。C22 以上的先在过氧化物酶体里切几轮，短了再交给线粒体。 ⚠ 那里的第一步不一样：用的是氧化酶，电子直接交给氧， 产物是 H₂O₂ 而不是 FADH₂（过氧化氢酶随后把它拆掉）。 所以那几轮不产 ATP，能量变成热。 ⚠ 病名知道就行：X 连锁肾上腺脑白质营养不良（超长链进不了过氧化物酶体）。
  7. 分叉三 · 不饱和 — 要多两个酶 天然双键是顺式，而循环只认反式 Δ²。 所以双键在奇数位时要一个异构酶把它搬正；在偶数位时还要一个 还原酶（用 NADPH）先把两个双键并成一个再搬。 ⚠ 每绕过一个双键，就少一次 FAD 脱氢，所以不饱和脂肪酸产的 ATP 略少。
  8. 总账 — 一整条棕榈酸（C16） 7 轮 → 8 acetyl-CoA ＋ 7 FADH₂ ＋ 7 NADH，再减去活化花掉的 2 个高能键。 现代算法 ≈ 106 ATP；按你书的折算率（acetyl-CoA 12、NADH 3、FADH₂ 2）≈ 129。 ⚠ 报数之前先说是按哪套算的。

Say this

Fatty acids are the body’s main fuel, especially when fasting. When blood glucose falls, insulin drops and glucagon rises, hormone-sensitive lipase breaks down the stored triacylglycerol (also called triglyceride, neutral fat), and the free fatty acids travel to the tissues bound to albumin. ⚠ The brain cannot use them, which is exactly why the liver turns them into ketone bodies. Beta-oxidation (also called β-oxidation, fatty acid oxidation) itself happens in the mitochondrial matrix, and there are two things to get through first. The fatty acid must be activated to a thioester with coenzyme A, which costs two high-energy bonds because the ATP goes all the way to AMP. Then the acyl group has to cross the inner membrane on carnitine, and carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1) is the rate-limiting step of the whole pathway — it is inhibited by malonyl-CoA, so switching synthesis on switches oxidation off. The cycle itself is four steps: oxidation, hydration, oxidation and thiolysis, giving one FADH₂, one NADH and one acetyl-CoA per round, and the chain is two carbons shorter each time. An even-numbered chain ends as acetyl-CoA. An odd-numbered one leaves propionyl-CoA, which is carboxylated and then rearranged by a B12-dependent mutase into succinyl-CoA — and that is the only part of a fatty acid that can become glucose. Very long chains, above about twenty-two carbons, are shortened in the peroxisome first, where the first oxidation gives hydrogen peroxide instead of FADH₂, so that part yields heat rather than ATP.

约 70 秒 · ⚠ 开口先说「什么时候跑」，再说怎么跑 —— 考官想听的是你知道这条路为什么存在

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hodek

① 脂代谢包括分解、合成、胆固醇和运输四部分，β-氧化只是分解里的一条路 lipid metabolism vs fatty acid oxidation

Lipid metabolism has four parts: breakdown by lipases, β-oxidation and ketone bodies; synthesis by acetyl-CoA carboxylase and fatty acid synthase into triacylglycerol and phospholipids; cholesterol synthesis via HMG-CoA reductase; and transport in lipoproteins. β-oxidation is one route of the breakdown part.

分解 · 合成 · 胆固醇 · 运输。

② 脂肪酸比葡萄糖更还原，而且脂肪不带水储存，所以每克储能更多 fat as energy store

β-oxidation matters because fat is the main energy store: a fatty acid is more reduced than glucose and fat is stored without water, so the body holds about a day of glycogen but weeks of fat.

糖原约一天，脂肪几周。

③ 心脏主要用脂肪酸，脑几乎不用，红细胞不能用 tissues

The heart and resting skeletal muscle use mainly fatty acids; the brain uses almost none; red blood cells cannot use them because they have no mitochondria.

脑不用脂肪酸，所以需要酮体。

④ 空腹时肝氧化脂肪酸，为糖异生提供 ATP，多余的乙酰 CoA 变成酮体 gluconeogenesis · ketone bodies

Because the brain cannot use fatty acids, in fasting the liver oxidises them to supply the ATP for gluconeogenesis, and the acetyl-CoA it cannot use is turned into ketone bodies for the brain.

接「糖异生」和「酮体」。

⑤ 奇数碳脂肪酸最后的丙酰 CoA 需要维生素 B₁₂ 才能进入柠檬酸循环 vitamin B12

Not every fatty acid ends as acetyl-CoA: an odd-chain fatty acid leaves propionyl-CoA, which needs vitamin B12 to become succinyl-CoA, so in B12 deficiency methylmalonic acid accumulates in blood and urine.

接「维生素」。

⑥ 超长链脂肪酸先在过氧化物酶体里缩短；X 连锁肾上腺脑白质营养不良是 ABCD1 转运蛋白缺陷，超长链进不去 peroxisome · X-ALD

A second exception is very long chains, which are first shortened in the peroxisome; in X-linked adrenoleukodystrophy, an inherited disease, ABCD1, the peroxisomal transporter for these chains, is defective, so they accumulate and damage the brain white matter and the adrenal glands.

病名和转运蛋白名知道就行。

⑦ 天然不饱和脂肪酸的顺式双键需要异构酶改成反式才能继续 unsaturated fatty acids

A third exception is unsaturated fatty acids: their natural cis double bonds must be changed to trans by enoyl-CoA isomerase before β-oxidation can continue.

不饱和 → 多一个异构酶。

⑧ 植物和酵母的 β-氧化只在过氧化物酶体里进行 plants and yeast

In plants and yeast, all β-oxidation takes place in peroxisomes, and in a germinating oil seed the acetyl-CoA goes into the glyoxylate cycle to make sugar.

种子能把油变成糖。

⑨ 书外：内质网里的 P450 从甲基端氧化脂肪酸，叫 ω-氧化 ⭐ Hodek ω-oxidation

There is also a minor route in the endoplasmic reticulum, ω-oxidation, in which cytochrome P450 enzymes oxidise the methyl end of the chain. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · P450 既代谢药物也代谢脂肪酸。

⑩ 书外：肉碱缺乏和 MCAD 缺乏是遗传病，患病的孩子空腹会低血糖，而且没有酮体 carnitine · MCAD deficiency

When β-oxidation fails, fasting becomes dangerous: in carnitine deficiency or MCAD deficiency a fasting child develops hypoglycaemia without ketone bodies, and the treatment is to avoid fasting. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

低血糖 ＋ 无酮体 ＝ 脂肪酸氧化缺陷。

⑪ 书外：Knoop 是德国生化学家，1904 年用末端带苯环的脂肪酸喂狗，证明每次去掉两个碳 Knoop 1904

Franz Knoop, a German biochemist, showed the two-carbon steps in 1904: he fed dogs fatty acids with a benzene ring on the end: even chains gave phenylacetic acid and odd chains gave benzoic acid. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

偶数 → 苯乙酸，奇数 → 苯甲酸。

陷阱问法

  • "Is lipid metabolism the same as β-oxidation（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation）?" — No, β-oxidation is one breakdown route within it. · 不是，只是分解的一条路。
  • "Can the brain use fatty acids?" — Almost not; it uses glucose, and ketone bodies in prolonged fasting. · 几乎不能。
  • "Can fat become glucose?" — Only the glycerol and the propionyl-CoA from odd chains can. · 只有甘油和奇数碳末端。
  • "Does β-oxidation need oxygen?" — Not directly, but its NADH and FADH₂ are reoxidised only by the respiratory chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）. · 不直接用，没氧就停。

定义

  • lipase — an enzyme that hydrolyses fatty acids off glycerol · 脂肪酶
  • lipoprotein — a particle of lipid and protein that carries lipids in blood · 脂蛋白
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • methylmalonic acid — the compound that accumulates when the B12-dependent step fails · 甲基丙二酸
  • peroxisome — an organelle that shortens very-long-chain fatty acids · 过氧化物酶体
  • ABCD1 — the peroxisomal membrane transporter for very-long-chain fatty acids · ABCD1 转运蛋白
  • enoyl-CoA isomerase — the enzyme that moves a cis double bond to the trans position · 烯酰 CoA 异构酶
  • glyoxylate cycle — a variant of the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） in plants and bacteria that allows fat to become sugar · 乙醛酸循环
  • cytochrome P450 — a family of haem enzymes that insert an oxygen atom into molecules · 细胞色素 P450
  • carnitine — the molecule that carries long-chain acyl groups into the mitochondrion · 肉碱
  • MCAD — medium-chain acyl-CoA dehydrogenase · 中链酰基 CoA 脱氢酶
  • hypoglycaemia — abnormally low blood glucose · 低血糖

Fatty acid synthesis

⚠ 她点名模糊的那一条 · 四步，第一步就是把碳运出线粒体

  1. 什么时候跑 — 吃饱、胰岛素高的时候糖多到烧不完的时候，多余的碳要存成脂肪。胰岛素开这条路，胰高血糖素关它。⚠ 跟 β-氧化严格反相：不可能同时跑，因为丙二酰-CoA 会把氧化的门锁上。
  2. Citrate shuttle — 把碳运出线粒体acetyl-CoA 在线粒体里，合成在胞质，它过不了内膜。于是跟草酰乙酸缩合成柠檬酸坐转运体出来，胞质里 ATP-citrate lyase 花一个 ATP 再把它劈回 acetyl-CoA。⚠ 草酰乙酸回程走「苹果酸 → malic enzyme → 丙酮酸」，顺手造出一部分 NADPH，其余的从磷酸戊糖途径来。
  3. Acetyl-CoA carboxylation — 承诺步骤acetyl-CoA ＋ HCO₃⁻ ＋ ATP →[acetyl-CoA carboxylase|ABC：ATP · biotin · CO₂] malonyl-CoA限速步，调控全压在这个酶上：柠檬酸开它 · 棕榈酰-CoA 关它 · AMPK 磷酸化关它 · 胰岛素开它。⚠ 而且 malonyl-CoA 反手关掉 CPT-1——合成一开，氧化的门就锁上。
  4. Fatty acid synthase complex — 真正长链子的地方动物的 FAS 是一条多功能肽链，摆臂是 ACP（那条臂来自泛酸）。每轮四步：缩合（同时放掉一个 CO₂）→ 还原（NADPH）→ 脱水 → 再还原（NADPH），链子长两个碳。⚠ 必考的怪事：第 2 步加上的 CO₂ 在这里又放掉了，根本没进产物——它是用来买反应的，脱羧释放的能量推着缩合往前走，换来这一步不可逆。
  5. 后加工 — 加长和加双键FAS 只做到 C16 棕榈酸，转七轮之后硫酯酶把它切下来。更长的链和双键都在内质网另外加。⚠ 人做不出 C9 以后的双键，所以亚油酸和亚麻酸是必需脂肪酸。

Say this

Fatty acid synthesis happens in four stages. First, the carbon has to leave the mitochondrion. Acetyl-CoA cannot cross the inner membrane, so it comes out as citrate, and ATP-citrate lyase splits it back in the cytosol. The oxaloacetate returns as pyruvate, and that return trip makes some of the NADPH. Second is the committed step: acetyl-CoA carboxylase uses biotin and one ATP to make malonyl-CoA. Third is the fatty acid synthase complex. Each round is condensation, reduction, dehydration, reduction, it spends two NADPH, and the chain grows by two carbons. Seven rounds give palmitate. Fourth, anything longer or unsaturated is added afterwards in the ER. Overall: eight acetyl-CoA, seven ATP and fourteen NADPH per palmitate.

约 45 秒 · 总账 8 / 7 / 14 一定要说出来

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hodek、Martínek、Lipovová

① 饭后胰岛素高时，肝和脂肪组织把多余的葡萄糖变成脂肪酸 fed state · insulin

Fatty acid synthesis runs after a meal, when insulin is high, mainly in the liver and adipose tissue, and turns surplus glucose into fat; glucagon inhibits it.

胰岛素促进，胰高血糖素抑制。

② 乙酰 CoA 以柠檬酸的形式从线粒体运到细胞质 citrate shuttle

Acetyl-CoA is made in the mitochondrion but fatty acids are made in the cytosol, so acetyl-CoA leaves as citrate, and ATP-citrate lyase splits citrate back into acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate in the cytosol.

这就是柠檬酸穿梭。

③ 乙酰 CoA 羧化酶是限速步，用生物素把乙酰 CoA 变成丙二酰 CoA acetyl-CoA carboxylase · committed step

In the cytosol the committed and rate-limiting step is acetyl-CoA carboxylase, which uses biotin and ATP to turn acetyl-CoA into malonyl-CoA.

全路的调控都在这个酶上。

④ 柠檬酸和胰岛素激活它；棕榈酰 CoA、胰高血糖素和 AMPK 抑制它 regulation of ACC

That is why the citrate matters twice: citrate activates acetyl-CoA carboxylase, and so does insulin; the product palmitoyl-CoA inhibits it, and glucagon and AMPK inhibit it by phosphorylation.

原料多 → 激活；产物多、能量少 → 抑制。

⑤ 丙二酰 CoA 抑制 CPT-I，所以合成和氧化不会同时进行 malonyl-CoA inhibits CPT-I

Malonyl-CoA also inhibits CPT-I, the enzyme that brings fatty acids into mitochondria, so a cell that is making fatty acids is not oxidising them at the same time.

接「脂肪酸氧化」。

⑥ 合成与 β-氧化的区别：细胞质对基质、ACP 对 CoA、NADPH 对 NAD⁺/FAD、D 型对 L 型 synthesis vs β-oxidation

So synthesis is not β-oxidation (also called fatty acid oxidation) reversed: it is in the cytosol not the matrix, the chain is held on ACP not CoA, it uses NADPH not NAD⁺ and FAD, and its intermediates are D not L isomers.

地点 · 载体 · 辅酶 · 构型。

⑦ NADPH 主要来自磷酸戊糖途径 NADPH from the pentose phosphate pathway

The NADPH comes mainly from the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway), and part from malic enzyme, so liver and adipose tissue run the pentose phosphate pathway actively.

接「磷酸戊糖途径（= pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway）」。

⑧ 人不能合成亚油酸和亚麻酸，它们的产物变成前列腺素，阿司匹林抑制环氧合酶 essential fatty acids · eicosanoids

Humans cannot make linoleic and linolenic acid, so they are essential; from them come eicosanoids such as prostaglandins, whose synthesis aspirin, a painkiller, blocks by inhibiting cyclooxygenase.

接「激素」。

⑨ 植物在叶绿体里合成脂肪酸，用的是多个独立的酶；动物的是一条多功能肽链 type I vs type II synthase

Plants and bacteria use a type II system of separate enzymes, in plants located in the chloroplast; animals use a type I synthase, one multifunctional polypeptide.

动物一条肽链，植物细菌多个酶。

⑩ 书外：异烟肼是抗结核药，抑制结核杆菌的 II 型脂肪酸合成，阻断细胞壁分枝菌酸的合成 isoniazid · mycolic acids

Because the bacterial system is different from ours, it is a drug target: isoniazid, a tuberculosis drug, blocks fatty-acid synthesis in Mycobacterium tuberculosis, so it cannot make the mycolic acids of its cell wall. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 细菌与人的酶不同 → 可以做药。

⑪ 书外：内质网的去饱和酶从细胞色素 b5 得到电子，P450 也用细胞色素 b5 ⭐ Hodek · Martínek cytochrome b5

Double bonds are added later in the endoplasmic reticulum by desaturases, which receive electrons from cytochrome b5, the same protein that interacts with cytochrome P450. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · Martínek 做过 P450 与 b5 的对接。

⑫ 书外：在大肠杆菌里换上植物的硫酯酶，可以生产中链脂肪酸做生物燃料 ⭐ Lipovová metabolic engineering

Chain length is set by the thioesterase that releases the chain, so engineers express plant thioesterases in E. coli to produce medium-chain fatty acids for biofuels. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 通往她的蛋白质工程。

陷阱问法

  • "Is it β-oxidation（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation） in reverse?" — No: different compartment, carrier, coenzyme and stereochemistry. · 不是倒着跑。
  • "What is the rate-limiting enzyme?" — Acetyl-CoA carboxylase, not fatty acid synthase. · 是 ACC。
  • "Can amino acids become fat?" — Yes, those that give acetyl-CoA. · 能，经乙酰 CoA。
  • "Why don't synthesis and oxidation run together?" — Malonyl-CoA inhibits CPT-I. · 丙二酰 CoA 抑制 CPT-I。

定义

  • ATP-citrate lyase — the cytosolic enzyme that splits citrate into acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate · ATP-柠檬酸裂解酶
  • committed step — the first irreversible step unique to a pathway · 关键步
  • biotin — the vitamin that carries CO₂ in carboxylases · 生物素
  • malonyl-CoA — the three-carbon donor for chain growth · 丙二酰 CoA
  • AMPK — AMP-activated protein kinase, activated when ATP is low · AMP 激活的蛋白激酶
  • CPT-I — carnitine palmitoyltransferase（= carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1） I, which moves fatty acids into mitochondria · 肉碱棕榈酰转移酶 I
  • ACP — acyl carrier protein, which holds the growing chain · 酰基载体蛋白
  • malic enzyme — converts malate to pyruvate and makes NADPH · 苹果酸酶
  • eicosanoids — signalling molecules made from twenty-carbon fatty acids · 二十碳类
  • cyclooxygenase — the enzyme that starts prostaglandin synthesis · 环氧合酶
  • mycolic acids — very long fatty acids of the mycobacterial cell wall · 分枝菌酸
  • desaturase — an enzyme that introduces a double bond into a fatty acid · 去饱和酶
  • thioesterase — the enzyme that releases the finished fatty acid · 硫酯酶

Triacylglycerol & phospholipid synthesis

⚠ 脂质合成的第二块 · 造好的脂肪酸接下来去哪儿 —— 一个岔路口决定储能还是造膜

  1. 什么时候跑 — 吃饱之后 胰岛素高的时候：糖多、能量够，多余的碳要存起来。 脂肪组织存成脂肪，肝把造好的脂肪打包成 VLDL 送出去。 ⚠ 跟 β-氧化正好反相：那条是空腹跑的，这条是饱食跑的。
  2. 两个原料先各自就位 脂肪酸要先活化成 acyl-CoA（跟氧化前一样，先做硫酯）。 骨架是 甘油-3-磷酸，它有两个来源：糖酵解的 DHAP 还原而来（大多数组织只能这样）， 或者甘油直接磷酸化（⚠ 只有肝和肾有甘油激酶，脂肪组织没有）。
  3. 接上两条脂肪酸 → 磷脂酸 甘油-3-磷酸的 C1 和 C2 各接一条脂肪酸，得到 磷脂酸（phosphatidic acid）。 ⚠ 这是整条路的岔路口，两边从这里分开。
  4. 岔路 A — 去掉磷酸，接第三条 → 甘油三酯（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat） 磷酸酶把 C3 上的磷酸切掉得到二酰甘油，再接第三条脂肪酸 → 三酰甘油。 完全非极性，所以能挤在一起成油滴，这就是为什么它适合储能。
  5. 岔路 B — 留着磷酸，接极性头 → 磷脂 磷酸上再接一个极性头基：胆碱、乙醇胺、丝氨酸、肌醇。 得到甘油磷脂，两亲性——一头亲水一头疏水，所以它才能排成双层膜。 ⚠ 储能要非极性，造膜要两亲性，同一个前体分两条路，就是为了这个差别。
  6. 另一条系统 — 鞘脂不走甘油 鞘脂的骨架是鞘氨醇，不是甘油，而且脂肪酸是用酰胺键挂上去的，不是酯键。 C1 上挂磷酸胆碱就是鞘磷脂，挂糖就是糖鞘脂（脑苷脂、神经节苷脂）。

Say this

Both storage fat and membrane phospholipids are built after a meal, when insulin is high, and they share the same beginning. The backbone is glycerol-3-phosphate, and two fatty acids are esterified onto it to give phosphatidic acid. That is the branch point. If the phosphate is removed and a third fatty acid added, the product is a triacylglycerol (also called triglyceride, neutral fat), which is completely non-polar and therefore good for storage. If the phosphate is kept and a polar head group such as choline or ethanolamine is attached, the product is a glycerophospholipid, which is amphipathic and therefore forms bilayers. Sphingolipids are a separate system: the backbone is sphingosine rather than glycerol, and the fatty acid is attached through an amide bond.

约 45 秒 · 「磷脂酸是岔路口」那一句是骨架

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① 游离脂肪酸是去污剂，会破坏膜，所以以甘油三酯形式储存 why triacylglycerol

Free fatty acids act as detergents and damage membranes, so cells store them as triacylglycerol, which is neutral and forms lipid droplets.

中性 → 可以安全储存。

② 胰脂肪酶在肠道消化食物中的甘油三酯；奥利司他是减肥药，抑制胰脂肪酶 pancreatic lipase · orlistat

Before storage comes digestion: pancreatic lipase, the intestinal fat-digesting enzyme, hydrolyses dietary triacylglycerol, with bile acids as emulsifiers. Orlistat, a weight-loss drug, inhibits pancreatic lipase.

接「胆固醇 → 胆汁酸」。

③ 肠道重新合成的甘油三酯装进乳糜微粒，肝合成的装进 VLDL chylomicrons · VLDL

After absorption, the intestinal cells make triacylglycerol again and send it out in chylomicrons, while triacylglycerol made in the liver is sent out in VLDL.

乳糜微粒 ＝ 食物来的，VLDL ＝ 肝造的。

④ 毛细血管壁上的脂蛋白脂肪酶把甘油三酯水解，脂肪酸进入肌肉和脂肪细胞 lipoprotein lipase

In the capillaries of muscle and adipose tissue, lipoprotein lipase hydrolyses the triacylglycerol in these particles, and the fatty acids enter the cells; insulin increases this enzyme in adipose tissue.

接「脂蛋白」。

⑤ 空腹时激素敏感性脂肪酶分解储存的甘油三酯；胰高血糖素和肾上腺素促进，胰岛素抑制 lipolysis

In fasting the stored fat is broken down again, which is lipolysis: adipose triglyceride lipase and hormone-sensitive lipase are activated by glucagon and adrenaline and inhibited by insulin.

接「脂肪酸氧化」。

⑥ 书外：瘦素是脂肪组织分泌的激素，告诉下丘脑储存了多少脂肪 leptin

Leptin, a hormone secreted by adipose tissue, tells the hypothalamus how much fat is stored and reduces appetite. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 脂肪组织也是内分泌器官。

⑦ UCP1 是棕色脂肪线粒体里的蛋白，让质子漏回基质，脂肪酸氧化的能量变成热而不是 ATP brown fat · UCP1

Brown adipose tissue, in newborns and hibernating animals, contains UCP1 (thermogenin), which lets protons return to the matrix without ATP synthase (also called Complex V, F₀F₁-ATPase), so the energy of fat oxidation is released as heat.

接「呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）」的解偶联。

⑧ 乙醇代谢使肝细胞 NADH 升高，促进甘油三酯合成，导致脂肪肝 ⭐ Hodek alcoholic fatty liver

Too much triacylglycerol in the liver is fatty liver; with alcohol, alcohol dehydrogenase raises NADH in the liver, which favours triacylglycerol synthesis and inhibits fatty acid oxidation (also called β-oxidation).

乙醇代谢在 Hodek 的考纲里。

⑨ 磷脂酶 C 把膜磷脂 PIP₂ 切成 IP₃ 和二酰甘油两个第二信使 phospholipase C

Phospholipids are also a source of signals: after a hormone binds its receptor, phospholipase C cleaves PIP₂ into IP₃, which releases calcium, and diacylglycerol, which activates protein kinase C.

接「激素信号」。

⑩ 书外：二棕榈酰磷脂酰胆碱是肺表面活性物质的主要成分，早产儿缺少会发生呼吸窘迫综合征 lung surfactant

Dipalmitoylphosphatidylcholine (also called lecithin), a phospholipid, is the main component of lung surfactant, and premature babies who lack it develop respiratory distress syndrome. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

⑪ 书外：Tay–Sachs 病是遗传病，缺氨基己糖苷酶 A，GM2 神经节苷脂在神经细胞里堆积 ⭐ Lipovová Tay–Sachs · hexosaminidase A

Sphingolipids are degraded in lysosomes. Tay–Sachs disease, an inherited lysosomal disease, is caused by missing β-hexosaminidase A, so the GM2 ganglioside accumulates in nerve cells. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 氨基己糖苷酶是 Lipovová 研究的酶。

⑫ 书外：工业上脂肪酶用于洗涤剂，甘油三酯经转酯化制成生物柴油 ⭐ Lipovová industrial lipases · biodiesel

Lipases are also industrial enzymes: they are added to laundry detergents, and biodiesel is made by transesterification of triacylglycerol with methanol. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 通往她的应用酶学。

⑬ 油料种子把甘油三酯储存在油体里；叶绿体膜主要由糖脂构成 plants

In plants, oil seeds store triacylglycerol in oil bodies, and chloroplast thylakoid membranes are made mainly of galactolipids rather than phospholipids.

接「光合作用」。

陷阱问法

  • "Are fat and lipid the same?" — No, fat (triacylglycerol) is one class of lipid. · 脂肪只是脂质的一类。
  • "Is cholesterol a phospholipid?" — No, it is a sterol and contains no glycerol. · 不是，没有甘油。
  • "Why do phospholipids form bilayers but triacylglycerol forms droplets?" — Only phospholipids have a polar head group. · 只有磷脂有极性头。

定义

  • detergent — a molecule with a polar head and a hydrophobic tail that dissolves membranes · 去污剂
  • pancreatic lipase — the intestinal enzyme that digests dietary triacylglycerol · 胰脂肪酶
  • orlistat — a drug that inhibits pancreatic lipase · 奥利司他
  • chylomicron — the lipoprotein that carries dietary fat from the intestine · 乳糜微粒
  • VLDL — very-low-density lipoprotein, carrying triacylglycerol made in the liver · 极低密度脂蛋白
  • lipoprotein lipase — the capillary enzyme that hydrolyses triacylglycerol in lipoproteins · 脂蛋白脂肪酶
  • lipolysis — hydrolysis of stored triacylglycerol to fatty acids and glycerol · 脂解
  • leptin — a hormone from adipose tissue that reduces appetite · 瘦素
  • UCP1 / thermogenin — the proton channel of brown fat that produces heat · 解偶联蛋白 1
  • alcohol dehydrogenase — the liver enzyme that oxidises ethanol and produces NADH · 乙醇脱氢酶
  • IP₃ / diacylglycerol — the two second messengers made from PIP₂ · 三磷酸肌醇／二酰甘油
  • lung surfactant — the phospholipid layer that stops the lung alveoli collapsing · 肺表面活性物质
  • ganglioside — a sphingolipid with a sugar chain containing sialic acid · 神经节苷脂
  • transesterification — exchanging the alcohol part of an ester · 转酯化
  • galactolipid — a lipid whose polar head is galactose · 半乳糖脂

Cholesterol

⚠ 脂质合成的第三块 · 全部的碳都来自 acetyl-CoA

  1. 什么时候跑 — 细胞缺胆固醇的时候 膜要长、要做类固醇激素或胆汁酸的时候。吃进来的胆固醇多了，这条路就被关掉—— 典型的终产物反馈。⚠ 大部分在肝里进行，胞质和内质网。
  2. 三个 acetyl-CoA 拼成 HMG-CoA 2 acetyl-CoA →[thiolase] acetoacetyl-CoA →[HMG-CoA synthase|＋acetyl-CoA] HMG-CoA ⚠ 这两步跟酮体生成一模一样，差别只在地点：线粒体里做出来的去当酮体，胞质里做出来的去当胆固醇。 HMG-CoA 是这个岔路口。
  3. 限速步 — HMG-CoA 还原酶 HMG-CoA →[HMG-CoA reductase|2 NADPH] mevalonate 这是承诺步骤，也是全条路唯一值得记的酶。 关它的有三样：胆固醇本身（反馈）· AMPK 磷酸化（没能量别造）· 他汀。 ⚠ 他汀就是这个酶的竞争性抑制剂，全世界卖得最多的药之一。
  4. 甲羟戊酸 → 五碳单元 → 三十碳 → 环化 甲羟戊酸变成异戊烯基（5 碳），一段段接成 角鲨烯（30 碳），再环化成四环骨架， 最后修剪成胆固醇（27 碳）。⚠ 细节不用背，记住「五碳砖块 → 三十碳链 → 环化」这个形状就够。
  5. 往下还有三条路 胆固醇是类固醇激素、胆汁酸和维生素 D 的共同前体。 ⚠ 而在膜里它的角色是调节流动性——温度高时限制它、温度低时防止它冻住，两个方向的缓冲。

Say this

Every carbon of cholesterol comes from acetyl-CoA. Three of them are joined into HMG-CoA, and that intermediate is a branch point: made in the mitochondrion it goes to ketone bodies, made in the cytosol it goes to cholesterol. The committed and rate-limiting step is HMG-CoA reductase, which uses two NADPH to make mevalonate. It is inhibited by cholesterol itself, switched off by AMPK phosphorylation, and it is the target of the statins, which are competitive inhibitors of it. After that, five-carbon isoprene units are joined into squalene, which is cyclised into the four-ring sterol. Cholesterol then tunes membrane fluidity and is the precursor of steroid hormones, bile acids and vitamin D.

约 45 秒 · HMG-CoA 那个岔路口和他汀是两个必说

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Hudeček

① 细胞膜需要胆固醇，但血中胆固醇过高会在动脉壁形成斑块 atherosclerosis

Every animal cell needs cholesterol for its membranes, but too much cholesterol in the blood is deposited in artery walls as plaques, which is atherosclerosis, so its level is tightly regulated.

既必需，又不能多。

② 书外：LDL 把胆固醇从肝运到组织，HDL 把多余的运回肝 LDL · HDL

Cholesterol travels in lipoproteins: LDL carries it from the liver to the tissues, and HDL carries the excess back to the liver. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · LDL 高有害，HDL 高有益。

③ 书外：细胞通过 LDL 受体摄取 LDL；受体缺陷叫家族性高胆固醇血症 · Brown 和 Goldstein 1985 年诺奖 LDL receptor

Cells take up LDL by the LDL receptor; a defective receptor causes familial hypercholesterolaemia with early heart attacks, and Brown and Goldstein received the Nobel Prize for this in 1985. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 受体介导的内吞。

④ 他汀是降胆固醇药，抑制 HMG-CoA 还原酶后，肝细胞增加 LDL 受体，从血中摄取更多 LDL statins

This is also how statins, the main cholesterol-lowering drugs, work: when HMG-CoA reductase is inhibited, liver cells make more LDL receptors and remove more LDL from the blood.

自己合成少 → 从血中摄取多。

⑤ 书外：依折麦布是降胆固醇药，抑制肠道吸收胆固醇 ezetimibe

Ezetimibe, a second cholesterol-lowering drug, works differently: it blocks the intestinal transporter NPC1L1, so less dietary cholesterol is absorbed. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 他汀管合成，依折麦布管吸收。

⑥ 胆固醇的环不能被分解成 CO₂，只能在肝里转成胆汁酸排出 bile acids

The body cannot break the sterol rings down to CO₂, so the only way to remove cholesterol is to convert it in the liver into bile acids, which are secreted in bile and also emulsify dietary fat.

唯一的排出途径是胆汁。

⑦ 胆汁酸、类固醇激素和活性维生素 D 都由细胞色素 P450 羟化胆固醇得到 ⭐ Hodek cytochrome P450

The first step from cholesterol to bile acids, to steroid hormones and to active vitamin D is each done by a cytochrome P450 enzyme, so P450 enzymes make all the cholesterol products.

P450 不只代谢药物。

⑧ 真菌用麦角固醇，唑类抗真菌药抑制真菌里合成它的那个 P450 ⭐ Hodek ergosterol · azoles

Fungi make ergosterol instead of cholesterol, and azole antifungals, drugs such as fluconazole, inhibit the fungal P450 enzyme that makes it; plants make phytosterols such as sitosterol.

同一条路，不同物种，可以做药。

⑨ 同样的五碳异戊二烯单位还合成泛醌、多萜醇、蛋白质的异戊烯基锚，植物里还合成类胡萝卜素 isoprenoids

Before cholesterol, the pathway makes isoprene units, which are also used for ubiquinone (also called coenzyme Q, CoQ), dolichol, the prenyl anchors of proteins such as Ras, and in plants for carotenoids.

胆固醇只是其中一个产物。

⑩ 胆固醇调节膜流动性，可用 DPH 的荧光各向异性测量 ⭐ Hudeček fluorescence anisotropy

In the membrane cholesterol buffers fluidity, and fluidity is measured by the fluorescence anisotropy of the probe DPH: the less freely DPH rotates, the higher the anisotropy.

先说测的物理量：转动快慢。

⑪ 书外：Bloch 和 Lynen 阐明胆固醇合成，1964 年诺奖；远藤章从青霉菌里发现第一个他汀 discovery

Konrad Bloch and Feodor Lynen worked out cholesterol synthesis and received the 1964 Nobel Prize, and Akira Endo found the first statin, mevastatin, in the fungus Penicillium citrinum. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

陷阱问法

  • "Is cholesterol a fat?" — No, it is a sterol, an alcohol. · 不是脂肪，是固醇。
  • "Does all our cholesterol come from food?" — No, most is made in the body, mainly in the liver. · 大部分自己合成。
  • "Can cholesterol be used for energy?" — No, it is only excreted as bile acids. · 不能，只能排出。

定义

  • atherosclerosis — cholesterol-rich plaques in artery walls · 动脉粥样硬化
  • LDL / HDL — low- and high-density lipoproteins, the blood carriers of cholesterol · 低／高密度脂蛋白
  • LDL receptor — the cell-surface protein that binds LDL for uptake · LDL 受体
  • familial hypercholesterolaemia — an inherited defect of the LDL receptor · 家族性高胆固醇血症
  • statin — a drug that inhibits HMG-CoA reductase · 他汀
  • ezetimibe — a drug that blocks intestinal cholesterol uptake · 依折麦布
  • bile acids — cholesterol derivatives that emulsify fat in the intestine · 胆汁酸
  • cytochrome P450 — a family of haem enzymes that insert an oxygen atom into molecules · 细胞色素 P450
  • ergosterol — the main sterol of fungi · 麦角固醇
  • isoprene unit — the five-carbon building unit of cholesterol and other isoprenoids · 异戊二烯单位
  • ubiquinone — the lipid electron carrier of the respiratory chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain） · 泛醌（= ubiquinone = coenzyme Q = CoQ）
  • dolichol — a long isoprenoid that carries sugars for N-glycosylation · 多萜醇
  • fluorescence anisotropy — a measure of how freely a fluorescent molecule rotates · 荧光各向异性
  • DPH — diphenylhexatriene, a fluorescent probe that enters membranes · 二苯基己三烯

Vitamins & coenzymes

⚠ 2026-09-27 她点名：完全没碰过 · 卡 31

Must say

The structural idea is one sentence: a water-soluble vitamin is the part of a coenzyme the body cannot make. So you do not memorise vitamins — you memorise which coenzyme each becomes, and what that coenzyme carries.

这一句是整章的钥匙。不是背维生素，是背「它变成哪个辅酶、那个辅酶搬什么」。

Numbers

B₁ thiamine → TPP (thiamine pyrophosphate), carries an aldehyde (the decarboxylations). B₂ riboflavin → FAD / FMN (flavin adenine dinucleotide / flavin mononucleotide), carries hydrogen. B₃ niacin → NAD⁺ / NADP⁺ (nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, and its phosphate), carries hydride. B₅ pantothenate → CoA (coenzyme A), carries acyl groups. B₆ pyridoxine → PLP (pyridoxal phosphate), carries amino groups. B₇ biotin carries CO₂. B₉ folate → THF (tetrahydrofolate), carries one-carbon units. B₁₂ cobalamin carries methyl groups. Vitamin C is the odd one out — not a coenzyme, but the reductant that keeps the iron of collagen hydroxylases working.

⚠ 两套名字都要认：考官可能说 B₁，也可能说 thiamine，还可能只说 TPP——同一样东西的三个叫法。
B₃ 还有个老名字 vitamin PP（也叫尼克酸／烟酰胺）；B₇ 偶尔被写成 vitamin H。
口诀 Naked Rats Take Pictures Before Party ＝ Niacin · Riboflavin · Thiamine · Pantothenate · Biotin · Pyridoxine。

Hook

Water-soluble ones are coenzyme parts; the fat-soluble ones, A D E K, are not. They act as a light receptor, a hormone, an antioxidant and a clotting cofactor. Deficiencies worth the name only: thiamine → beriberi, niacin → pellagra, vitamin C → scurvy (collagen cannot be hydroxylated), vitamin D → rickets, vitamin K → bleeding.

⚠ A D E K 脂溶性，其余水溶性 —— 这个分界本身就是一道题。

Say this

A water-soluble vitamin is the part of a coenzyme that the body cannot make, so each one is worth remembering as a pair: which coenzyme it becomes, and what that coenzyme carries. B one, thiamine, becomes TPP, thiamine pyrophosphate, which carries an aldehyde. B two, riboflavin, becomes FAD, which carries hydrogen. B three, niacin, becomes NAD-plus, and the split matters — NADH goes to the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain), NADPH goes to biosynthesis. B five, pantothenate, becomes coenzyme A, carrying acyl groups. B six, pyridoxine, becomes PLP, pyridoxal phosphate, carrying amino groups. B seven is biotin, which carries CO₂. B nine, folate, becomes tetrahydrofolate, carrying one-carbon units. And B twelve carries methyl groups. The fat-soluble ones, A, D, E and K, are not coenzyme parts, and because they are stored rather than excreted, A and D can be toxic in excess.

约 55 秒 · 「辅酶里身体造不出来的那一截」是开口第一句 · ⚠ 编号和名字一起说，两套都能接得上

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 4 位考官：Hudeček、Martínková、Ryšlavá、Hodek

① 维生素是必须从食物获得的有机物；B 族和 C 溶于水，A、D、E、K 溶于脂肪 water- vs fat-soluble

Vitamins are organic molecules that must come from food; the B group and vitamin C are water-soluble, and most B vitamins become coenzymes, while A, D, E and K are fat-soluble and mostly are not.

先分两组，再一个一个说。

② B1 硫胺素变成 TPP，丙酮酸脱羧要它；缺了得脚气病，酗酒者得 Wernicke 脑病 thiamine · beriberi

Starting with the B group, thiamine, vitamin B1, becomes TPP, needed to convert pyruvate to acetyl-CoA; its deficiency causes beriberi, with nerve and heart damage, and in alcoholics Wernicke encephalopathy.

糖代谢卡住，神经和心脏最先受累。

③ B2 核黄素变成 FAD 和 FMN，参与氧化还原；缺了口角炎 ⭐ Hudeček riboflavin · flavins

Next, riboflavin, vitamin B2, becomes FAD and FMN for redox reactions; its deficiency causes angular cheilitis, cracks at the corners of the mouth, and flavins are yellow and fluorescent, which is used to measure them.

黄色、会发荧光 → 可以用光谱测。

④ B3 烟酸变成 NAD⁺ 和 NADP⁺；缺了糙皮病：皮炎、腹泻、痴呆 niacin · pellagra

Like riboflavin, niacin, vitamin B3, becomes redox coenzymes, NAD⁺ and NADP⁺; its deficiency causes pellagra — dermatitis, diarrhoea and dementia — and the body can make some niacin from tryptophan.

三个 D。

⑤ B5 泛酸变成辅酶 A；几乎所有食物都有，很少缺乏 pantothenic acid · coenzyme A

Pantothenic acid, vitamin B5, becomes coenzyme A, which carries acyl groups in fat and carbohydrate metabolism; it is in almost every food, so deficiency is very rare.

名字来自希腊语「到处」。

⑥ B6 吡哆醇变成 PLP，转氨和血红素合成第一步都要它；异烟肼会引起 B6 缺乏 ⭐ Martínková pyridoxine · PLP

Pyridoxine, vitamin B6, becomes PLP, used in transamination, neurotransmitter synthesis and the first step of haem synthesis; its deficiency causes neuropathy and anaemia, and isoniazid, a tuberculosis drug, can cause it.

血红素从这一步开始 → Martínková。

⑦ B7 生物素是羧化酶的辅酶；大量吃生蛋清会缺，因为抗生物素蛋白结合它 biotin · avidin

Biotin, vitamin B7, is the coenzyme of the carboxylases, such as acetyl-CoA carboxylase and pyruvate carboxylase; deficiency is rare, but avidin, a protein of raw egg white, binds biotin and can cause it.

实验室的 biotin–streptavidin 是同一种结合。

⑧ B9 叶酸变成四氢叶酸，合成 DNA 碱基要它；缺了巨幼细胞贫血和神经管缺陷 folate · neural-tube defects

Folate, vitamin B9, becomes tetrahydrofolate, which carries one-carbon units for making DNA bases; its deficiency causes megaloblastic anaemia and, in pregnancy, neural-tube defects, so women take folic acid before conception.

孕前补叶酸。

⑨ 甲氨蝶呤是抗癌药，抑制二氢叶酸还原酶 methotrexate

Because dividing cells need folate, methotrexate, an anticancer drug, inhibits dihydrofolate reductase and stops DNA synthesis in fast-growing cells.

抗维生素也能当药。

⑩ B12 钴胺素只由微生物合成，吸收要内因子；缺了恶性贫血和神经损伤 cobalamin · pernicious anaemia

Cobalamin, vitamin B12, works with folate and in odd-chain fatty acid breakdown; it is made only by microorganisms and absorbed only with intrinsic factor from the stomach. Its lack causes pernicious anaemia and nerve damage.

素食者和胃有问题的人容易缺。

⑪ 维生素 C 是脯氨酰羟化酶需要的还原剂，也是抗氧化剂；缺了坏血病 ⭐ Ryšlavá ascorbic acid · scurvy

After the B group, vitamin C, ascorbic acid, is not a coenzyme but a reductant for prolyl hydroxylase, which stabilises collagen, and a major antioxidant in plants; its deficiency causes scurvy, with bleeding gums.

人不能自己合成 C，大多数动物能。

⑫ 维生素 A 变成视黄醛（视觉）和视黄酸（调控基因）；缺了夜盲，过量致畸 retinol · night blindness

Moving to the fat-soluble group, vitamin A, retinol, gives retinal, the light-absorbing part of rhodopsin, and retinoic acid, which regulates genes. Its deficiency causes night blindness, and excess causes birth defects.

视觉 ＋ 基因调控。

⑬ 维生素 D 在皮肤经紫外线生成，肝和肾里的 P450 把它活化成骨化三醇；缺了佝偻病 ⭐ Hodek calciferol · rickets

Vitamin D is made in the skin by ultraviolet light and activated to calcitriol by P450 enzymes in the liver and kidney; it increases calcium absorption, and its deficiency causes rickets in children and osteomalacia in adults.

其实像激素 · P450 活化 → Hodek。

⑭ 维生素 E 生育酚是膜里的抗氧化剂，防止脂质过氧化；很少缺乏 tocopherol · antioxidant

Vitamin E, α-tocopherol, is the main antioxidant inside membranes and prevents lipid peroxidation; deficiency is rare and causes neuropathy and haemolytic anaemia in premature babies.

和维生素 C 一起抗氧化。

⑮ 维生素 K 用于凝血因子的 γ-羧化；缺了出血；华法林是抗凝药，抑制它的再生 ⭐ Hodek vitamin K · warfarin

Vitamin K is needed for γ-carboxylation of clotting factors; its deficiency causes bleeding, so newborns receive an injection. Warfarin, an anticoagulant, blocks the recycling of vitamin K.

抗凝药 ＝ 抗维生素 K。

⑯ 水溶性维生素多了从尿排出，脂溶性的存在肝和脂肪里，A 和 D 过量会中毒 storage and toxicity

This is the practical difference between the two groups: excess water-soluble vitamins are excreted in urine, while fat-soluble vitamins are stored in liver and fat, so A and D can become toxic.

B12 是例外，肝里能存几年。

⑰ 书外：Eijkman 发现吃精米的鸡得脚气病，1929 年诺奖；Funk 1912 年提出 vitamine discovery

Christiaan Eijkman, a Dutch physician, found that chickens fed polished rice developed a beriberi-like disease, and received the 1929 Nobel Prize; Casimir Funk introduced the word "vitamine" in 1912. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

陷阱问法

  • "Do vitamins provide energy?" — No, they help the enzymes that release energy. · 不供能。
  • "Is every vitamin a coenzyme?" — No; most B vitamins are, but A, C and D are not. · A、C、D 不是辅酶。
  • "Is vitamin D really a vitamin?" — With enough sunlight we make it, so it acts like a hormone. · 晒太阳能自己合成。
  • "Why do vegans need B12 supplements?" — Plants contain no B12. · 植物里没有。

定义

  • coenzyme — a small organic molecule that carries a group or electrons for an enzyme · 辅酶
  • TPP — thiamine pyrophosphate, the coenzyme form of B1 · 焦磷酸硫胺素
  • Wernicke encephalopathy — brain damage from thiamine deficiency, mainly in alcoholics · 韦尼克脑病
  • flavins — FAD and FMN, made from riboflavin · 黄素
  • PLP — pyridoxal phosphate, the coenzyme form of B6 · 磷酸吡哆醛
  • avidin — an egg-white protein that binds biotin tightly · 抗生物素蛋白
  • tetrahydrofolate — the coenzyme form of folate, carrying one-carbon units · 四氢叶酸
  • megaloblastic anaemia — anaemia with large immature red cells from slow DNA synthesis · 巨幼细胞贫血
  • neural-tube defect — failure of the embryonic spinal cord or brain to close · 神经管缺陷
  • dihydrofolate reductase — the enzyme that regenerates tetrahydrofolate · 二氢叶酸还原酶
  • intrinsic factor — a stomach protein needed to absorb B12 · 内因子
  • prolyl hydroxylase — the enzyme that hydroxylates proline in collagen · 脯氨酰羟化酶
  • rhodopsin — the light receptor of rod cells, containing retinal · 视紫红质
  • calcitriol — the active hormone form of vitamin D · 骨化三醇
  • osteomalacia — soft bones in adults from lack of vitamin D · 骨软化症
  • lipid peroxidation — oxidative damage to membrane fatty acids · 脂质过氧化
  • γ-carboxylation — adding a carboxyl group to glutamate so a protein binds calcium · γ-羧化

Who carries what

七个基团载体 —— 把上面那张维生素表反过来查

Must say

Every activated group travels on a dedicated carrier. Acyl on CoA · CO₂ on biotin · amino on PLP · one carbon on tetrahydrofolate · methyl on SAM · aldehyde on TPP · sugar on UDP.

Can Bears Play Tennis? Some Try Upstairs.

Numbers

Two electron carriers, and the split is the whole point: NADH goes to the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) to make ATP; NADPH goes to biosynthesis.

Hook

Several of these carriers contain a nucleotide that takes no part in the chemistry — it is a handle for the enzyme to grip, not cargo.

Say this

Activated groups do not travel alone — each one has a dedicated carrier, and remembering the pairs is faster than remembering the pathways. Acyl groups travel on CoA (coenzyme A), CO₂ on biotin, amino groups on PLP (pyridoxal phosphate), one-carbon units on THF (tetrahydrofolate), methyl groups on SAM (S-adenosylmethionine), aldehydes on TPP (thiamine pyrophosphate), and sugars on UDP (uridine diphosphate). For electrons there are two pools and the split is the whole point: NADH goes to the respiratory chain to make ATP, and NADPH goes to biosynthesis. ⚠ Several of these carriers contain a nucleotide that takes no part in the chemistry at all — it is a handle for the enzyme to grip, not cargo.

约 40 秒 · 「NADH 烧、NADPH 造」那一句最常用

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Ryšlavá、Hudeček

① ATP 的磷酸之间是磷酸酐键，水解时放出大量能量 phosphoanhydride bonds

ATP's three phosphates are joined by phosphoanhydride bonds, and hydrolysing one releases a lot of energy because the products are more stable than ATP, not because the bond is strong.

能量来自产物更稳定。

② 产物更稳定有三个原因：负电荷排斥减小、共振稳定、水合更好 charge, resonance, hydration

The products are more stable for three reasons: the negative charges repel each other less, the released phosphate is stabilised by resonance, and the products are better hydrated.

电荷排斥 · 共振 · 水合。

③ ATP 的磷酸基团转移势处于中间，所以既能接受磷酸也能给出磷酸 intermediate group-transfer potential

Because of this, ATP's phosphate group-transfer potential is high but not the highest: it can receive phosphate from phosphoenolpyruvate and 1,3-bisphosphoglycerate, and give phosphate to glucose and other acceptors.

这是 ATP 能当能量通货的原因。

④ ATP 不断被合成和消耗，长期储能用的是脂肪和糖原 ATP is turned over, not stored

ATP is made and used continuously, so the cell keeps only a small amount, and long-term energy storage is in fat and glycogen.

所以吃 ATP 没有用。

⑤ GTP、UTP、CTP 也做同样的磷酸转移，只是碱基不同 other nucleoside triphosphates

Other nucleoside triphosphates have the same chemistry with a different base: UTP activates sugars as UDP-glucose, GTP is used in protein synthesis, and CTP activates lipid head groups.

接糖原合成、翻译、磷脂合成。

⑥ NADPH 比 NADH 多一个磷酸，这个磷酸不参加反应，只让酶能区分二者 the extra phosphate

For electrons there are two carriers: NADPH has one extra phosphate, which takes no part in the redox chemistry but lets enzymes distinguish NADPH from NADH.

化学相同，被不同的酶识别。

⑦ 细胞让 NAD⁺ 多数处于氧化态，用于分解代谢，NADH 交给呼吸链 NAD⁺ in catabolism

Because enzymes can tell them apart, the cell keeps NAD⁺ mostly oxidised, ready to accept electrons in breakdown, and the resulting NADH goes to the respiratory chain to make ATP.

分解 → NADH → 呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）。

⑧ 细胞让 NADPH 多数处于还原态，用于生物合成和抗氧化 NADPH in biosynthesis and defence

At the same time it keeps NADPH mostly reduced, ready to donate electrons for fatty-acid and cholesterol synthesis and to keep glutathione reduced for antioxidant defence.

合成 ＋ 抗氧化。

⑨ NADPH 来自磷酸戊糖途径、苹果酸酶和光合作用的光反应 ⭐ Ryšlavá sources of NADPH

NADPH is produced by the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway), by malic enzyme, and in plants by the light reactions of photosynthesis; NADP-malic enzyme is one of the enzymes Ryšlavá studies.

接 PPP、脂肪合成、光合作用。

⑩ NADH 和 NADPH 在 340 nm 有吸收，氧化型没有，所以能用分光光度计跟踪反应 ⭐ Hudeček absorbance at 340 nm

Both reduced forms absorb light at 340 nm and the oxidised forms do not, so any enzyme that uses them can be followed in a spectrophotometer.

Hudeček 做光谱学。

⑪ 书外：磷酸肌酸是肌肉里的储备，在运动最初几秒快速再生 ATP phosphocreatine

Phosphocreatine, a compound stored in muscle, is used by creatine kinase to regenerate ATP in the first seconds of exercise. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the energy stored in the bond?" — No, it comes from the products being more stable than ATP. · 来自产物更稳定。
  • "Why does the cell need two electron carriers?" — One carrier cannot be mostly oxidised and mostly reduced at the same time. · 一种载体不能同时多数氧化又多数还原。
  • "Does NADPH go to the respiratory chain?" — No, it is used for biosynthesis and antioxidant defence. · 不进呼吸链。

定义

  • phosphoanhydride bond — the bond between two phosphate groups · 磷酸酐键
  • resonance — electrons spread over several atoms, which stabilises a molecule · 共振
  • hydration — being surrounded by water molecules · 水合
  • group-transfer potential — how readily a molecule donates a group such as phosphate · 基团转移势
  • catabolism — breakdown of molecules that releases energy · 分解代谢
  • glutathione — a small thiol-containing peptide that removes peroxides · 谷胱甘肽
  • malic enzyme — converts malate to pyruvate and produces NADPH · 苹果酸酶
  • phosphocreatine — muscle compound that regenerates ATP from ADP · 磷酸肌酸

Ketone bodies

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 肝内生成、肝外利用

  1. 什么时候造 — acetyl-CoA 多到循环吃不下饥饿或糖尿病时脂肪大量分解，草酰乙酸又被抽去做糖异生，于是 acetyl-CoA 进不了柠檬酸循环，只能改道。
  2. 生成（肝线粒体）2 acetyl-CoA →[thiolase] acetoacetyl-CoA →[HMG-CoA synthase|限速] HMG-CoA →[HMG-CoA lyase] acetoacetate乙酰乙酸再被还原成 β-羟丁酸（血里最多的那个），或者自发脱羧成丙酮。⚠ 丙酮是废气不是燃料，从呼吸里出去。
  3. 利用（肝外）β-hydroxybutyrate →[BDH|NAD⁺] acetoacetate →[thiophorase (SCOT)|借 succinyl-CoA 的 CoA] acetoacetyl-CoA →[thiolase] 2 acetyl-CoA⚠ 肝自己用不了，因为肝没有 thiophorase——这一句解释了为什么肝造了却全送走。
  4. 谁在吃心和肌肉平时就吃；脑正常只吃葡萄糖，长期饥饿才改用酮体。⚠ 红细胞永远不能用——没有线粒体。

Say this

Ketone bodies are made in the liver and used outside it. They are made when acetyl-CoA is produced faster than the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) can take it, in starvation and in diabetes. There are three: acetoacetate, β-hydroxybutyrate and acetone, but only the first two are fuel — acetone is waste and is breathed out. The committed enzyme is HMG-CoA synthase, in the liver mitochondrion. The tissues that use them convert them back to acetyl-CoA with thiophorase, and the liver cannot use them itself precisely because it lacks that enzyme. The heart and muscle use them routinely; the brain only after prolonged starvation, and red cells never, because they have no mitochondria.

约 40 秒 · 「肝没有 thiophorase」是这题最值钱的一句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hudeček

① 脑不能用脂肪酸，肝把脂肪酸转成酮体供脑使用 why ketone bodies exist

The brain cannot use fatty acids, so in fasting the liver converts their acetyl-CoA into ketone bodies, which are water-soluble and travel in blood without albumin.

脂肪 → 脑能用的形式。

② 长期饥饿时脑改用酮体，减少葡萄糖需求，因此减少肌肉蛋白分解 protein sparing

Because of that, in prolonged fasting the brain uses ketone bodies for a large part of its energy, so less glucose is needed from gluconeogenesis and less muscle protein is broken down.

省葡萄糖，也省肌肉蛋白。

③ 酮体和胆固醇的前两步相同，线粒体里生成酮体，细胞质里生成胆固醇 HMG-CoA branch point

The first steps, up to HMG-CoA, are the same as in cholesterol synthesis; in the mitochondrion HMG-CoA gives ketone bodies, in the cytosol it gives cholesterol.

接「胆固醇」。

④ 亮氨酸和赖氨酸是纯生酮氨基酸，只能变成酮体或脂肪，不能变成葡萄糖 ketogenic amino acids

Leucine and lysine, two amino acids, also feed this pathway: they are purely ketogenic, so their carbons can become ketone bodies or fat but never glucose.

接「氨基酸代谢」。

⑤ 饥饿时的酮症是正常的；1 型糖尿病缺胰岛素时发生酮症酸中毒 ketosis vs ketoacidosis

Mild ketosis in fasting is normal, but in untreated type 1 diabetes there is no insulin to inhibit lipolysis, so ketone bodies rise until blood pH falls, which is diabetic ketoacidosis.

区别在于有没有胰岛素。

⑥ 书外：SGLT2 抑制剂是降糖药，可以在血糖不高时引起酮症酸中毒 SGLT2 inhibitors

SGLT2 inhibitors, diabetes drugs such as empagliflozin, are a modern cause: they can cause ketoacidosis with nearly normal blood glucose. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 血糖不高也可能酮症酸中毒。

⑦ 酮症酸中毒时呼出丙酮，呼气有水果味 acetone on the breath

In ketoacidosis acetone, which forms spontaneously from acetoacetate, is breathed out and gives the breath a fruity smell.

丙酮不是燃料，从肺排出。

⑧ 尿试纸测乙酰乙酸，血酮仪测 β-羟丁酸 how it is measured

Clinically, urine test strips detect acetoacetate by the nitroprusside reaction, and blood meters measure β-hydroxybutyrate.

尿测乙酰乙酸，血测 β-羟丁酸。

⑨ β-羟丁酸脱氢酶是实验室测定用的酶，反应产生 NADH，在 340 nm 测吸光度 ⭐ Hudeček NADH absorbance at 340 nm

β-Hydroxybutyrate dehydrogenase, the enzyme of the laboratory assay, converts β-hydroxybutyrate to acetoacetate and reduces NAD⁺ to NADH, and NADH is measured by its absorbance at 340 nm.

先说测的物理量：340 nm 吸光度。

⑩ 书外：生酮饮食是极低碳水化合物饮食，用于治疗儿童难治性癫痫 ketogenic diet

The ketogenic diet, a very low-carbohydrate diet, keeps ketone bodies high and is used to reduce seizures in children with drug-resistant epilepsy. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

⑪ 书外：β-羟丁酸还能抑制组蛋白去乙酰化酶，改变基因表达 histone deacetylases

β-Hydroxybutyrate is also a signal: it inhibits histone deacetylases, so fasting changes gene expression. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 点到为止。

陷阱问法

  • "Are all three ketone bodies ketones?" — No, β-hydroxybutyrate is a hydroxy acid, not a ketone. · β-羟丁酸不是酮。
  • "Does the liver use ketone bodies?" — No, it lacks thiophorase. · 肝不用。
  • "Is ketosis always a disease?" — No, it is normal in fasting; ketoacidosis is the disease. · 酮症 ≠ 酮症酸中毒。
  • "Can ketone bodies become glucose?" — No. · 不能。

定义

  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate, β-hydroxybutyrate and acetone · 酮体
  • albumin — the blood protein that carries free fatty acids · 白蛋白
  • HMG-CoA — the six-carbon intermediate shared by ketone body and cholesterol synthesis · HMG-CoA
  • ketogenic — able to form ketone bodies or fat but not glucose · 生酮的
  • ketosis — raised ketone bodies in blood · 酮症
  • ketoacidosis — ketone bodies high enough to lower blood pH · 酮症酸中毒
  • SGLT2 inhibitor — a diabetes drug that makes the kidney excrete glucose · SGLT2 抑制剂
  • nitroprusside — the reagent on urine strips that reacts with acetoacetate · 硝普盐
  • absorbance — the amount of light a sample absorbs at one wavelength · 吸光度
  • histone deacetylase — an enzyme that removes acetyl groups from histones · 组蛋白去乙酰化酶
  • thiophorase — the enzyme outside the liver that activates acetoacetate with CoA · 硫激酶（SCOT）

Gluconeogenesis

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 七步共用，四个酶绕过三道坎

  1. 什么时候跑 — 空腹超过几小时、饥饿、剧烈运动之后肝糖原只够撑十几个小时，之后血糖全靠这条路。胰高血糖素和皮质醇开它，胰岛素关它。⚠ 为什么非做不可：脑只烧葡萄糖，红细胞只能烧葡萄糖——它们停不得。
  2. 第一道坎 — 丙酮酸 → PEP，要两个酶两步走pyruvate →[pyruvate carboxylase|ABC · 线粒体] oxaloacetate →[PEP carboxykinase|GTP · −CO₂] PEP⚠ 先加一个 CO₂ 再把它拿掉，跟脂肪酸合成那一步是同一个把戏：花钱买不可逆。而且 acetyl-CoA 开启 pyruvate carboxylase——脂肪够烧了就去造糖。
  3. 中间七步 — 直接借糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）的酶倒着跑这七步本来就近平衡，浓度一变方向就反，不需要另外的酶。
  4. 第二道坎 — 果糖-1,6-二磷酸酶fructose-1,6-bisP →[fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase|水解，不是逆激酶] fructose-6-P⚠ 被果糖-2,6-二磷酸关掉，而同一个分子开启 PFK-1——一个分子管住正反两条路。
  5. 第三道坎 — 葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶glucose-6-P →[glucose-6-phosphatase|肝 · 肾] glucose⚠ 只有肝和肾有这个酶，所以只有它们能把葡萄糖放进血里。肌肉没有。

Say this

Gluconeogenesis makes glucose from lactate, glycerol and glucogenic amino acids, mainly in the liver and kidney. Seven of the steps are simply glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) running backwards, because those steps are near equilibrium. Three steps are irreversible and need four different enzymes. Pyruvate is carboxylated to oxaloacetate by pyruvate carboxylase, using biotin and ATP, and then PEP carboxykinase spends GTP to make phosphoenolpyruvate. Fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase and glucose-6-phosphatase then hydrolyse the two phosphates off. The cost is four ATP, two GTP and two NADH per glucose, and what that cost buys is direction.

约 40 秒 · 四个酶的名字 ＋ 4/2/2 这个代价

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Ryšlavá

① 糖酵解有三步不可逆，糖异生在这三处换用别的酶 bypass reactions

Glycolysis has three irreversible steps, catalysed by hexokinase, phosphofructokinase-1 and pyruvate kinase, so gluconeogenesis goes around each of them with different enzymes.

三个不可逆的酶，三处绕道。

② 正反两条路的酶不同，所以可以分别开关 reciprocal regulation

Because the enzymes are different, the two pathways can be switched on and off separately, so the cell does not run both at once and waste ATP in a futile cycle.

接「调控」主题。

③ 胰高血糖素和皮质醇开启糖异生，胰岛素关闭它 hormones

The order comes from hormones: glucagon and cortisol switch gluconeogenesis on in fasting, and insulin switches it off after a meal.

谁下令。

④ 原料是乳酸、氨基酸和甘油 lactate, amino acids, glycerol

The starting materials are lactate from muscle and red blood cells, alanine and other amino acids from muscle protein, and glycerol from fat tissue.

每种原料来自一个器官。

⑤ 动物不能用脂肪酸做葡萄糖，只能用甘油 fatty acids are not a source

Fatty acids are not on that list: in animals, fatty acids give only acetyl-CoA, which cannot be turned into glucose, so only the glycerol part of fat can be used.

常考。

⑥ 空腹时先用肝糖原，肝糖原用完后靠糖异生 glycogen first

In fasting the liver first breaks down its glycogen, and when glycogen runs low, gluconeogenesis becomes the main source of blood glucose.

接「糖原」主题。

⑦ 同时肝做酮体，拆氨基酸也让尿素循环加快 ketone bodies · urea cycle

At the same time the liver makes ketone bodies from fatty acids, and because amino acids are being broken down, the urea cycle (also called ornithine cycle, Krebs–Henseleit cycle) runs faster.

接「酮体」「氨基酸」两个主题。

⑧ 主要在肝里做，长期饥饿时肾也做 liver and kidney

Most gluconeogenesis happens in the liver; in long starvation the kidney contributes more.

只有这两个器官有葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。

⑨ 书外：牛的葡萄糖大部分来自糖异生 ruminants

In cows, microbes in the rumen ferment dietary sugar into propionate, and the cow's liver makes most of its glucose from propionate by gluconeogenesis. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑩ 乙醇在肝里分解产生大量 NADH，抑制糖异生 ⭐ Hodek ethanol · hypoglycaemia

Alcohol dehydrogenase breaks down ethanol in the liver and produces a lot of NADH, which inhibits gluconeogenesis, so drinking on an empty stomach can cause hypoglycaemia. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

Hodek 考纲里有乙醇代谢。

⑪ 植物用丙酮酸磷酸双激酶一步把丙酮酸变成 PEP ⭐ Ryšlavá pyruvate phosphate dikinase

Plants have pyruvate phosphate dikinase, which turns pyruvate into PEP in one step; C4 plants use it to regenerate the PEP that PEP carboxylase uses to capture CO₂.

她研究的酶之一，接光合作用。

⑫ 书外：2 型糖尿病时肝在血糖已经很高时仍在做葡萄糖 type 2 diabetes

In type 2 diabetes the liver responds poorly to insulin and keeps making glucose even when blood glucose is already high. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is it just glycolysis in reverse?" — Only partly; most steps are shared, but the three irreversible steps use different enzymes. · 三步用不同的酶。
  • "Can muscle raise blood glucose?" — No; it lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, so it sends lactate or alanine to the liver. · 肌肉没有葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。
  • "Why does the body need it?" — The brain and red blood cells need glucose, and liver glycogen runs out. · 脑和红细胞要葡萄糖。

定义

  • bypass reaction — a different enzyme step used instead of an irreversible glycolytic step · 绕道反应
  • futile cycle — two opposite pathways running at once, wasting ATP · 无效循环
  • reciprocal regulation — one pathway switched on while the opposite one is switched off · 相互调控
  • hypoglycaemia — blood glucose that is too low · 低血糖
  • propionate — a three-carbon acid made by rumen microbes · 丙酸
  • pyruvate phosphate dikinase — a plant enzyme making PEP directly from pyruvate · 丙酮酸磷酸双激酶
  • type 2 diabetes — diabetes where tissues respond poorly to insulin · 2 型糖尿病

Glycogen

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 合成和分解各四步，共用一套开关

  1. 什么时候跑 — 饭后存、空腹取饭后胰岛素高 → 存；空腹或运动、胰高血糖素和肾上腺素高 → 取。⚠ 肝的糖原是给全身的（维持血糖），肌肉的糖原只给自己（供自己收缩）——两个器官存的是同一样东西，用途完全不同。
  2. 合成 — 先活化，再接上去glucose-6-P →[phosphoglucomutase] glucose-1-P →[UDP-glucose pyrophosphorylase|＋UTP] UDP-glucose →[glycogen synthase|α-1,4] glycogen⚠ 葡萄糖不能直接接上去，必须先做成 UDP-葡萄糖。分支由分支酶做成 α-1,6。
  3. 分解 — 细胞内是磷酸解，不是水解glycogen →[glycogen phosphorylase|＋Pᵢ · α-1,4] glucose-1-P →[phosphoglucomutase] glucose-6-P⚠ 用磷酸而不是水去切，所以出来的是磷酸糖，省掉一次激酶。分支点由脱支酶处理，那里放出的是游离葡萄糖。⚠ 消化道里才是水解（淀粉酶），这个二分是考点。
  4. 出口 — 只有肝能放糖进血glucose-6-P →[glucose-6-phosphatase|只有肝和肾] glucose⚠ 肌肉没有这个酶，所以肌糖原只给肌肉自己用。
  5. 调控 — 一套级联，两个相反的结果磷酸化之后：磷酸化酶开（b→a），合成酶关（a→b）。所以肾上腺素和胰高血糖素一来，拆的开、造的关，绝不会同时跑——这就是避免无效循环。

Say this

Glycogen synthesis needs the glucose activated first, as UDP-glucose. Then glycogen synthase makes the α-1,4 chain and a branching enzyme makes the α-1,6 branches. Breakdown inside the cell is not hydrolysis but phosphorolysis: glycogen phosphorylase uses inorganic phosphate, so the product is already glucose-1-phosphate. In the digestive tract it is hydrolysis, by amylases. Only the liver and kidney have glucose-6-phosphatase, so muscle glycogen can never raise blood glucose. And the two directions share one cascade: phosphorylation activates the phosphorylase and inactivates the synthase, so the cell never runs both at once.

约 40 秒 · 磷酸解 vs 水解、肌肉没有磷酸酶，两句必说

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Lipovová

① 葡萄糖存成一个大分子，是为了不改变细胞的渗透压 osmotic pressure

Glucose is stored as glycogen because thousands of free glucose molecules would raise the osmotic pressure and draw water into the cell, while one glycogen molecule does not.

存成大分子，不吸水。

② 糖原分支多、末端多，所以能很快放出葡萄糖 branches

Glycogen has many α-1,6 branches, so it has many chain ends, and glycogen phosphorylase can release glucose from many ends at the same time.

分支多 ＝ 分解快。

③ 糖原蛋白是一个蛋白，每个糖原分子都从它上面开始合成 glycogenin

Glycogenin, a small protein, is the starting point of every glycogen molecule: it attaches the first glucose units to itself.

起点是一个蛋白。

④ 细胞里用磷酸切，产物是葡萄糖-1-磷酸；肠道里用水切，产物是游离葡萄糖 phosphorolysis vs hydrolysis

Inside the cell, glycogen phosphorylase cuts with phosphate, so the product is glucose-1-phosphate, which saves an ATP and cannot leave the cell; in the gut, amylase cuts with water, so the product is free glucose that can be absorbed.

细胞内：磷酸解。肠道：水解。

⑤ 唾液淀粉酶和胰淀粉酶切直链的 α-1,4 键 salivary and pancreatic amylase

In the gut, starch and glycogen from food are first cut by salivary α-amylase in the mouth and then by pancreatic α-amylase in the small intestine; both cut α-1,4 bonds.

嘴里一次，小肠一次。

⑥ 小肠上皮表面的麦芽糖酶、蔗糖酶-异麦芽糖酶、乳糖酶做最后一步 brush-border enzymes

The last step happens on the surface of the intestinal cells: maltase, sucrase-isomaltase and lactase release single sugars, and the isomaltase part cuts the α-1,6 branch points.

只有单糖能被吸收。

⑦ 书外：大多数成年人乳糖酶活性下降，这就是乳糖不耐受 lactose intolerance

Most adults produce much less lactase, so lactose reaches the colon, where bacteria ferment it and cause gas and diarrhoea; this is lactose intolerance. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑧ 纤维素是 β-1,4 键，人没有切它的酶 cellulose

Cellulose is also made of glucose, but its bonds are β-1,4, and humans have no enzyme that cuts them.

同样是葡萄糖，键不同。

⑨ 植物把葡萄糖存成淀粉 starch

Plants store glucose as starch, which has the same α-1,4 and α-1,6 bonds as glycogen but fewer branches.

接光合作用主题。

⑩ Cori 夫妇研究糖原分解获诺贝尔奖，Sutherland 在同一过程中发现了 cAMP discovery

Carl and Gerty Cori received a Nobel Prize for how glycogen is broken down, and Earl Sutherland discovered cAMP, the first known second messenger, while studying how adrenaline activates glycogen phosphorylase.

「怎么发现的」那一问。

⑪ 书外：缺葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶是 von Gierke 病，缺肌糖原磷酸化酶是 McArdle 病 glycogen storage diseases

In von Gierke disease the liver lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, so fasting blood glucose falls; in McArdle disease muscle lacks glycogen phosphorylase, so exercise causes cramps. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

肝型：空腹低血糖。肌型：运动抽筋。

⑫ 淀粉酶和乳糖酶都属于糖苷酶 ⭐ Lipovová glycosidases

Amylase and lactase are glycosidases, enzymes that hydrolyse the bond between sugars, and industry uses α-amylase to turn starch into glucose syrup.

她研究糖苷酶。

陷阱问法

  • "Is glycogen phosphorylase a glycosidase?" — No; it uses phosphate, not water. · 不是，它用磷酸不用水。
  • "Why can we not digest cellulose?" — Its bonds are β-1,4, and we have no enzyme for them. · β 键，没有酶。
  • "Why is glucose made into UDP-glucose first?" — Glucose must be activated before glycogen synthase can add it to the chain. · 先活化才能接上。

定义

  • osmotic pressure — the pull of water towards the side with more dissolved particles · 渗透压
  • glycogenin — the protein on which a glycogen molecule is built · 糖原蛋白
  • phosphorolysis — cutting a bond with phosphate instead of water · 磷酸解
  • hydrolysis — cutting a bond with water · 水解
  • brush border — the surface of the intestinal cells where the last digestive enzymes sit · 刷状缘
  • glycosidase — an enzyme that hydrolyses the bond between sugars · 糖苷酶
  • second messenger — a small molecule inside the cell, such as cAMP, that carries a hormone's signal · 第二信使
  • glycogen storage disease — an inherited lack of one enzyme of glycogen metabolism · 糖原贮积病

Amino acid breakdown & the urea cycle

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 氮和碳分开处理

  1. 什么时候跑 — 一直在跑，而且吃高蛋白或饥饿时更快氨基酸没法储存——多出来的只能烧掉或转成糖和脂肪，而氮必须处理掉。⚠ 饥饿时肌肉蛋白被拆来做糖异生，氮的负担反而更重。游离氨有神经毒性，所以这条路不能停。
  2. 摘氨基 — 转氨，再集中脱氨各种氨基酸把氨基交给 α-酮戊二酸，变成谷氨酸（转氨酶，辅酶 PLP，来自 B6）；谷氨酸再由谷氨酸脱氢酶放出游离氨。这两步合起来叫联合脱氨。
  3. 把氨运到肝 — 两条运输线肌肉走丙氨酸-葡萄糖循环，脑和其他组织走谷氨酰胺。⚠ 游离氨有毒，绝不在血里裸奔。
  4. 尿素循环（= urea cycle = ornithine cycle = Krebs–Henseleit cycle） — 两步在线粒体，三步在胞质NH₄⁺ ＋ HCO₃⁻ →[CPS I|2 ATP · 限速] carbamoyl phosphate →[ornithine transcarbamoylase] citrullinecitrulline →[argininosuccinate synthetase|＋aspartate · ATP→AMP] argininosuccinate →[argininosuccinate lyase|−fumarate] arginine →[arginase] urea ＋ ornithine⚠ 两个氮来路不同：一个是游离氨，一个由天冬氨酸带进来。CPS I 要 N-乙酰谷氨酸才开工——有活干才开机。
  5. 碳骨架去哪剩下的碳架按去向分两类：生糖的进丙酮酸或柠檬酸循环的中间物，生酮的变 acetyl-CoA。⚠ 纯生酮只有两个：Leu 和 Lys。

Say this

Amino acid breakdown separates the nitrogen from the carbon skeleton. The nitrogen is collected by transamination onto glutamate — the transaminases all need pyridoxal phosphate, vitamin B6 — and then released as free ammonia by glutamate dehydrogenase. Because ammonia is toxic it travels to the liver as alanine from muscle or as glutamine from other tissues. In the liver the urea cycle (also called ornithine cycle, Krebs–Henseleit cycle) runs with its first two steps in the mitochondrion and the last three in the cytosol. The control enzyme is carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I, switched on by N-acetylglutamate. The two nitrogens of urea come from different places: one from free ammonia, one from aspartate. It costs four ATP per urea, although the summary equation writes three, because one ATP is broken all the way to AMP.

约 50 秒 · 两个氮的来路 ＋ 4 对 3 那个说破

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Ryšlavá、Martínková、Lipovová

① 九个必需氨基酸人不能合成，必须从食物来，其余的自己合成 essential vs non-essential

Amino acids are not only broken down but also synthesised: nine amino acids are essential, because we cannot make their carbon skeletons and must eat them, and the others are non-essential, because we make them ourselves. The synthesis side is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

口诀 PVT TIM HaLL（Phe Val Thr Trp Ile Met His Leu Lys）。合成这半是书外。

② 氨先接到 α-酮戊二酸上变成谷氨酸，再接到谷氨酸上变成谷氨酰胺 glutamate dehydrogenase · glutamine synthetase

Nitrogen enters as ammonia: glutamate dehydrogenase adds it to α-ketoglutarate to make glutamate, and glutamine synthetase adds a second one to make glutamine; from glutamate it is passed to the other amino acids by transamination.

跟分解走的是同一个中转站，方向相反。

③ 植物和细菌用 GS/GOGAT 吸收氨；氮气只有固氮细菌能变成氨 ⭐ Ryšlavá GS/GOGAT · nitrogen fixation

Plants and bacteria take up ammonia mainly through GS/GOGAT, glutamine synthetase followed by glutamate synthase; and nitrogenase, an enzyme found only in some bacteria, turns nitrogen gas into ammonia, which is nitrogen fixation.

固氮在她的关键词里。

④ 二十种氨基酸的碳骨架只来自糖酵解、柠檬酸循环和戊糖磷酸途径的几个中间物 families

Once the nitrogen is in place, the carbon skeletons come from only a few intermediates of glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway), the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) and the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway), so the amino acids are grouped into families by their starting molecule.

按出发的中间物分家族。

⑤ α-酮戊二酸 → Glu、Gln、Pro、Arg；草酰乙酸 → Asp、Asn、Met、Thr、Lys α-ketoglutarate · oxaloacetate families

From α-ketoglutarate come glutamate, glutamine, proline and arginine; from oxaloacetate, through aspartate, come asparagine, methionine, threonine and lysine.

这两个都是柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）的中间物。

⑥ 丙酮酸 → Ala、Val、Leu、Ile；3-磷酸甘油酸 → Ser、Gly、Cys pyruvate · 3-phosphoglycerate families

From pyruvate come alanine, valine, leucine and isoleucine; from 3-phosphoglycerate come serine, glycine and cysteine.

这两个都是糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）的中间物。

⑦ 甘氨酸由丝氨酸去掉一个碳生成，这个碳交给四氢叶酸 serine → glycine

In that last family, glycine is made from serine by serine hydroxymethyltransferase, which removes one carbon and hands it to tetrahydrofolate; tetrahydrofolate then uses that carbon to make nucleotides.

同事问过、你卡住过的那一题。

⑧ 苯丙氨酸、酪氨酸、色氨酸走莽草酸途径，只有植物和微生物有；草甘膦是除草剂，堵的就是这条路 shikimate pathway · glyphosate

Phenylalanine, tyrosine and tryptophan are made from PEP and erythrose-4-phosphate by the shikimate pathway, which only plants and microbes have. Glyphosate, a weedkiller, blocks the enzyme EPSP synthase in this pathway, which is why it kills plants but not humans.

草甘膦：植物有这条路，人没有。

⑨ 组氨酸来自核糖-5-磷酸；人用苯丙氨酸羟化酶把苯丙氨酸变成酪氨酸，缺这个酶是苯丙酮尿症 histidine · tyrosine · PKU

Histidine is made from ribose-5-phosphate. Humans make tyrosine from phenylalanine with phenylalanine hydroxylase, and when that enzyme is missing, phenylalanine builds up: this is phenylketonuria.

新生儿都筛查苯丙酮尿症。

⑩ 酪氨酸变成肾上腺素和多巴胺，色氨酸变成血清素，组氨酸变成组胺 precursors

Amino acids are also the starting material for signalling molecules: tyrosine becomes adrenaline and dopamine, tryptophan becomes serotonin, and histidine becomes histamine.

接激素主题。

⑪ 精氨酸被一氧化氮合酶转成一氧化氮，一氧化氮被含血红素的蛋白感受 ⭐ Martínková nitric oxide

Arginine is also the source of nitric oxide: nitric oxide synthase makes it, and nitric oxide acts as a signal by binding to the iron of haem proteins.

她研究血红素怎样感受气体。

⑫ 尿素循环的调控步骤是第一步：氨甲酰磷酸合成酶 I，由 N-乙酰谷氨酸开启 committed step

Back to breakdown: "the control enzyme" of the urea cycle means its regulated, committed step, the first one. Carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I works only when N-acetylglutamate is present, and N-acetylglutamate rises after a protein-rich meal.

蛋白吃得多 → N-乙酰谷氨酸多 → 第一步开。

⑬ 鱼排氨，鸟和爬行动物排尿酸，哺乳动物排尿素 ammonia · uric acid · urea

Not every animal makes urea: fish release ammonia directly into the water, birds and reptiles excrete uric acid, which needs little water, and mammals excrete urea.

排什么，和能用多少水有关。

⑭ 书外：天冬酰胺酶分解天冬酰胺，用来治疗急性淋巴细胞白血病 ⭐ Lipovová asparaginase

Asparaginase, an enzyme that breaks down asparagine, is used to treat acute lymphoblastic leukaemia, because these leukaemia cells cannot make enough asparagine themselves. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

她做过天冬酰胺酶的项目。

陷阱问法

  • "Where does glycine come from?" — From serine, by serine hydroxymethyltransferase, which passes one carbon to tetrahydrofolate. · 丝氨酸去一个碳。
  • "Why does glyphosate not harm us?" — Humans do not have the shikimate pathway. · 人没有这条路。
  • "Is tyrosine essential?" — No; we make it from phenylalanine, except in phenylketonuria. · 不是，由苯丙氨酸做。
  • "Which step of the urea cycle is regulated?" — The first one, carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I, switched on by N-acetylglutamate. · 第一步。

定义

  • essential amino acid — an amino acid we cannot make and must eat · 必需氨基酸
  • transamination — moving an amino group from an amino acid to a keto acid · 转氨
  • GS/GOGAT — glutamine synthetase plus glutamate synthase, the main ammonia uptake route in plants and bacteria · 谷氨酰胺合成酶/谷氨酸合酶途径
  • nitrogen fixation — turning nitrogen gas into ammonia, by nitrogenase in some bacteria · 固氮
  • tetrahydrofolate — the folate coenzyme that carries one-carbon units · 四氢叶酸
  • shikimate pathway — the plant and microbial route to phenylalanine, tyrosine and tryptophan · 莽草酸途径
  • phenylketonuria — an inherited lack of phenylalanine hydroxylase · 苯丙酮尿症
  • committed step — the first irreversible step unique to a pathway · 定向步骤
  • N-acetylglutamate — the molecule that activates carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I · N-乙酰谷氨酸

Photosynthesis

卡 27 · 28 · 两半在两个隔间，跟线粒体「膜上发电、旁边花钱」同一个故事

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有光的时候（而固碳白天一直在跑） 定义先行：把辐射能生物性地转化成化学能（书自己的定义）。 光同时驱动两件事：把一个供体氧化、把 CO₂ 还原成碳水化合物。 H₂D ＋ A →[pigments] H₂A ＋ D 绿色植物里供体是水，所以才放氧。
  2. 光反应 · 电子链 — 在类囊体膜上 PS Ⅱ 吸光，电子被打出去，留下的空穴靠裂解水补上 —— 氧就是这么来的。 电子经 PQ → cyt b₆f（泵质子进类囊体腔）→ PC → PS Ⅰ， 在 PS Ⅰ 再吸一次光，经 Fd → FNR 把 NADP⁺ 还原成 NADPH。 口诀 Two Quick Boys Climb One Fir, Fetch Nuts。 ⚠ 二号在一号前面干活 —— 编号按发现顺序，不按工作顺序。这句本身是考点。
  3. 光合磷酸化 — 同一台机器，方向相反 质子被泵进类囊体腔，再经 ATP 合酶流回基质做 ATP。 ⚠ 跟线粒体是同一种酶、同一个原理，只是质子梯度的方向反过来（这里是腔里酸）。 ⚠ 环式电子传递：电子从 Fd 绕回 b₆f，只产 ATP 不产 NADPH —— 细胞用它来调两者的比例。
  4. 固碳 · Calvin 循环（= Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions） — 在 stroma 三步 Cows Run Round：羧化 → 还原 → 再生。 rubisco 把 CO₂ 接到核酮糖-1,5-二磷酸（5 碳）上，六碳产物立刻裂成 两个 3-磷酸甘油酸（3 碳） —— C3 这个名字就是这么来的。 ⚠ NADPH 只花在「还原」，ATP 花在「还原」和「再生」。 rubisco 是地球上丰度最高的酶。
  5. rubisco 的毛病 — 它还会抓氧 名字里的 oxygenase 不是白叫的：O₂ 会跟 CO₂ 抢同一个活性位点， 抓到氧就走光呼吸，白白浪费能量和碳。温度高、气孔关的时候更严重。
  6. 两种对策 — C4 分地方，CAM 分时间 都是先用 PEP 羧化酶把 CO₂ 抓住（它不认氧），浓缩之后再交给 rubisco。 C4：叶肉细胞抓，运到维管束鞘细胞放 —— 分地方。 CAM：夜里开气孔抓、存成苹果酸，白天关气孔放 —— 分时间。 ⚠ 三者只动固碳这一半，光反应完全一样。⚠ CAM 书里没有，要标 general。
  7. 总式和那个正号 12 H₂O ＋ 6 CO₂ →[light · pigments] C₆H₁₂O₆ ＋ 6 O₂ ＋ 6 H₂O ΔG°′ ＝ +2867 kJ/mol。⚠ 正号 ＝ 吸能，这就是为什么非要有光； 说成负号等于把光合说成了呼吸。 ⚠ 书写十二个水而不是六个，就是为了说明氧来自水，不是 CO₂。

Say this

Photosynthesis is the biological conversion of radiant energy into chemical energy, and it happens in two halves in two places. The light reactions run in the thylakoid membrane: photosystem II absorbs light, and the electron hole it leaves is filled by splitting water, which is where the oxygen comes from. The electrons pass through plastoquinone, cytochrome b₆f, plastocyanin and photosystem I to ferredoxin and NADP reductase, giving NADPH, while the protons pumped into the lumen drive an ATP synthase (also called Complex V, F₀F₁-ATPase) — the same machine as in the mitochondrion. The fixation of CO₂ is the Calvin cycle (also called Calvin–Benson cycle, dark reactions, light-independent reactions), and it runs in the stroma in three stages: carboxylation by rubisco, reduction, and regeneration, and it spends exactly the ATP and NADPH the light reactions made. The old name "dark reactions" is misleading: they run in daylight, they just do not use light directly. The first stable product is a three-carbon acid, 3-phosphoglycerate, which is why ordinary plants are called C3 plants. Rubisco also grabs oxygen, which wastes carbon in photorespiration, so some plants first capture CO₂ with PEP carboxylase, which ignores oxygen, and only then hand it to rubisco: C4 plants separate the two steps in space — mesophyll cells capture, bundle-sheath cells release — and CAM plants separate them in time — they capture at night and release by day with the stomata shut. The light reactions are the same in all three. ⚠ The oxygen comes from water, not from carbon dioxide, and my textbook makes that point by writing twelve waters rather than six.

约 90 秒 · 「氧来自水」和「二号在一号前面」是两个必说；Calvin 循环点名和 C3/C4/CAM 是 09-29 补的

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Ryšlavá、Martínková、Hudeček

① 叶绿素吸收蓝光和红光，反射绿光，所以叶子是绿的 chlorophyll absorbs blue and red

Chlorophyll absorbs mainly blue and red light and reflects green light, which is why leaves look green.

吸蓝、吸红、反射绿。

② 类胡萝卜素吸收叶绿素吸收不了的光，还保护叶子不受强光损伤 carotenoids

Carotenoids, the yellow and orange pigments, absorb light that chlorophyll misses and protect the leaf against excess light; in autumn, when chlorophyll is broken down, their colour becomes visible.

辅助色素 ＋ 保护。秋天叶子变黄就是它。

③ 大部分色素只吸收光，把能量传给反应中心，化学反应在反应中心开始 antenna · reaction centre

Most of these pigments form the antenna: they only absorb light and pass the energy to the reaction centre, a special chlorophyll pair where the electron is actually released.

像卫星天线把信号汇到一个接收点。

④ 电子从水到 NADPH 要吸收两次光，经过两个光系统，能量图呈 Z 形 Z-scheme

The electron from water needs energy from light twice, once at photosystem II and once at photosystem I, and the energy diagram of this path has the shape of the letter Z.

两个光系统串联。

⑤ 环式光合磷酸化时电子绕光系统 I 回流，只产 ATP，不产 NADPH cyclic photophosphorylation

Because the Calvin cycle needs more ATP than NADPH, electrons from photosystem I sometimes flow back to cytochrome b₆f instead of to NADP⁺, which makes extra ATP only; this is cyclic photophosphorylation.

用来调 ATP 和 NADPH 的比例。

⑥ rubisco 结合氧气时发生光呼吸，消耗能量并释放已固定的 CO₂ photorespiration

In the Calvin cycle, rubisco sometimes binds oxygen instead of CO₂; recovering the product is called photorespiration, it uses ATP, releases CO₂ that was already fixed, and involves the chloroplast, peroxisome and mitochondrion.

高温、干旱、气孔关闭时更严重。

⑦ rubisco 催化很慢，分不清 CO₂ 和 O₂，所以植物合成大量 rubisco slow and abundant

That is also why plants make so much rubisco: rubisco is very slow and discriminates poorly between CO₂ and O₂, so plants compensate with quantity, and it is one of the most abundant proteins on Earth. The speed trade-off is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

慢、不专一 → 靠数量。

⑧ 叶绿体和线粒体都有双层膜、自己的 DNA 和核糖体，都来自被吞入的细菌 endosymbiosis

Chloroplasts and mitochondria both have two membranes, their own DNA and their own ribosomes, because both come from bacteria taken up by an early cell: chloroplasts from a cyanobacterium, mitochondria from an α-proteobacterium. This is endosymbiosis.

两者都用 ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase），只是质子梯度方向相反。

⑨ 大气中的氧气来自光合作用，最初对大多数生物有毒 the oxygen atmosphere

The oxygen in the atmosphere was produced by photosynthesis billions of years ago, and at first oxygen was toxic to most organisms.

氧最初是毒。

⑩ 光能超过 Calvin 循环能用的量时，电子交给氧气，生成活性氧 ⭐ Ryšlavá reactive oxygen species

When light delivers more electrons than the Calvin cycle can use, electrons pass to oxygen and form reactive oxygen species, such as superoxide and hydrogen peroxide, which superoxide dismutase, catalase and peroxidases remove.

她研究植物胁迫和抗氧化酶。

⑪ 叶绿素和血红素都是卟啉环，叶绿素中心是镁，血红素中心是铁 ⭐ Martínková porphyrin: Mg vs Fe

Chlorophyll and haem are both porphyrins; chlorophyll has magnesium in the centre and haem has iron.

同一种环，中心金属不同。

⑫ 叶绿素用吸光度测含量，用荧光测光系统 II 的效率 ⭐ Hudeček absorbance · fluorescence

Chlorophyll content is measured by absorbance, and chlorophyll fluorescence is used to measure how efficiently photosystem II is working. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

先说测的是什么物理量。

陷阱问法

  • "Why are leaves green?" — Chlorophyll absorbs blue and red light and reflects green. · 反射绿光。
  • "Do plants respire?" — Yes, day and night; they have mitochondria. · 植物也一直在呼吸。
  • "Is photosynthesis respiration in reverse?" — Only the overall equation; the pathways, enzymes and compartments are different. · 只有总式是反的。

定义

  • carotenoid — a yellow or orange accessory pigment that also protects against excess light · 类胡萝卜素
  • antenna — pigments that absorb light and pass the energy to the reaction centre · 天线色素
  • reaction centre — the chlorophyll pair where light energy releases an electron · 反应中心
  • Z-scheme — the energy diagram of photosystem II and photosystem I in series · Z 图
  • cyclic photophosphorylation — electrons returning from photosystem I to cytochrome b₆f, making ATP only · 环式光合磷酸化
  • photorespiration — rubisco using O₂ instead of CO₂, which wastes energy and fixed carbon · 光呼吸
  • endosymbiosis — a bacterium living inside another cell and becoming an organelle · 内共生
