  • reactive oxygen species — highly reactive forms of oxygen, such as superoxide and hydrogen peroxide · 活性氧
  • porphyrin — the ring structure of haem and chlorophyll · 卟啉

Metabolic regulation

卡 32 · 四问

Must say

Four questions: where control sits, how it acts, what the cell senses, who gives the order. Control sits only at the steps that are far from equilibrium, because only there does the enzyme set the net flux.

Numbers

A reaction is near equilibrium when Q and K′eq are within one or two orders of magnitude — true of more than half the enzymes of glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway).

Hook

Three cross-pathway signals are worth more than any list: citrate shuts down glycolysis and opens fat synthesis; malonyl-CoA shuts down fat oxidation; acetyl-CoA shuts down PDH and opens gluconeogenesis.

Say this

I would answer this as four questions: where control sits, how it acts, what the cell senses, and who gives the order. Where: only at steps that are far from equilibrium, because at a near-equilibrium step the forward and reverse rates are both large, so adding enzyme speeds up both and the net flux barely moves. In practice a reaction is near equilibrium when Q and K′eq are within one or two orders of magnitude, and that is true of more than half the enzymes of glycolysis. How: slowly by changing the amount of enzyme, or quickly by changing the activity of enzyme that already exists — allosterically, covalently, by an inhibitor, or by a regulatory protein — and these are not mutually exclusive. What it senses: not absolute amounts but ratios — ATP to ADP, NADH to NAD-plus — and each ratio is a permit. Who: inside a cell those signals are enough; between tissues it is hormones.

约 55 秒 · 开口先报「四个问题」，结构就在你手里

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Martínková、Lipovová、Hodek

① 代谢调控是为了不浪费能量，并保持 ATP 和血糖稳定 homeostasis

Metabolism is regulated so that the cell does not waste energy and keeps ATP and blood glucose stable; keeping the internal state stable is called homeostasis.

为什么要调：省能量 ＋ 保持稳定。

② 改变酶活性只要几秒到几分钟，改变酶的量要几小时 time scales

To do this, changing an enzyme's activity takes seconds to minutes, while changing its amount through gene expression takes hours.

快调活性，慢调数量。

③ 终产物抑制通路的第一个定向步骤 feedback inhibition

The most common activity control is feedback inhibition: the end product inhibits the first committed step of its own pathway, so no intermediates are made that are not needed.

关在第一步，不关在中间。

④ 方向相反的通路放在不同的区室里 compartmentation

A second, structural control is compartmentation: for example, fatty acid synthesis runs in the cytosol and fatty acid oxidation (also called β-oxidation) in the mitochondria.

分开放，就不会同时跑。

⑤ 丙二酰 CoA 抑制肉碱酰基转移酶 I，使脂肪酸不能进线粒体被氧化 malonyl-CoA

Because of that separation, the cell can control transport: malonyl-CoA, the first intermediate of fatty acid synthesis, inhibits carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1), so new fatty acids are not carried into the mitochondria and oxidised.

合成一开，氧化就关。

⑥ 胰高血糖素和肾上腺素通过 cAMP 起作用；类固醇激素进入细胞改变基因表达 second messengers

Between tissues, hormones give the order: glucagon and adrenaline stay outside the cell and act through the second messenger cAMP, while steroid hormones such as cortisol enter the cell and change gene expression.

两条路：第二信使，或直接进核。

⑦ 胰岛素表示进食、储存；胰高血糖素和肾上腺素表示空腹或应激、释放 insulin vs glucagon

Insulin signals the fed state and promotes storage, while glucagon and adrenaline signal fasting or stress and promote release of glucose and fatty acids.

一对相反的命令。

⑧ 同一个反应在不同器官由不同的同工酶催化 isozymes

The same reaction can be regulated differently in different tissues through isozymes (also called isoenzyme); for example, liver uses glucokinase and muscle uses hexokinase to phosphorylate glucose.

接「器官分工」主题。

⑨ 别构调节剂结合在活性中心以外，改变酶的构象 ⭐ Martínková allostery

An allosteric regulator binds at a site other than the active site and changes the enzyme's conformation; haem sensor proteins transmit their signal by the same principle.

她的框架：一个位点结合，构象变化传到另一处。

⑩ 底物浓度低于 Km 时，反应速度随底物浓度变化 ⭐ Lipovová Km

Even without a regulator, an enzyme working below its Km speeds up when the substrate concentration rises, so substrate supply itself controls the rate.

接酶动力学。

⑪ 药物等外源物诱导肝合成更多细胞色素 P450 ⭐ Hodek enzyme induction

Changing enzyme amount is also how the liver responds to foreign compounds: drugs such as phenobarbital induce the synthesis of more cytochrome P450 enzymes.

「改数量」的例子，通往他的主场。

⑫ 书外：AMP 升高时 AMP 激活的蛋白激酶打开分解代谢、关闭合成代谢 AMP-activated protein kinase

When ATP falls, AMP rises and activates AMP-activated protein kinase, which switches on catabolic pathways and switches off synthetic ones. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the slowest step the only control point?" — No; control is usually shared by several steps. · 控制分摊在几步上。
  • "Is allosteric inhibition the same as competitive?" — No; an allosteric regulator binds at a different site. · 结合位点不同。
  • "Why regulate the first committed step?" — So that no energy is spent on intermediates that are not needed. · 不浪费中间物。

定义

  • homeostasis — keeping the internal state stable · 稳态
  • feedback inhibition — an end product inhibiting an early step of its own pathway · 反馈抑制
  • committed step — the first irreversible step unique to a pathway · 定向步骤
  • compartmentation — separating pathways into different parts of the cell · 区室化
  • second messenger — a small molecule inside the cell, such as cAMP, that carries a hormone's signal · 第二信使
  • isozyme — a different form of an enzyme that catalyses the same reaction · 同工酶（= isoenzyme = isozyme）
  • allosteric — acting at a site other than the active site by changing conformation · 别构
  • Km — the substrate concentration giving half the maximum rate · 米氏常数（= Km = Michaelis constant）
  • enzyme induction — increased synthesis of an enzyme in response to a signal · 酶诱导

Who eats what

器官分工 —— 常被当作串场题

Must say

The liver is the switchboard: after a meal it stores, in starvation it releases glycogen, runs gluconeogenesis and makes ketone bodies for everyone else.

Numbers

The brain normally burns only glucose and takes about 20% of the oxygen; only in prolonged starvation does it switch to ketone bodies.

Hook

Red cells have no mitochondria — glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) only, and that is also why they depend on the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway) for NADPH.

Say this

The tissues do not all eat the same thing, and the differences are the point. The liver is the switchboard: after a meal it stores glycogen and makes fat; in starvation it releases glucose, runs gluconeogenesis, and makes ketone bodies for everyone else — though it cannot use them itself. The brain normally burns only glucose and takes about a fifth of the body’s oxygen; only after prolonged starvation does it switch partly to ketone bodies. Red cells have no mitochondria, so they depend entirely on glycolysis, and that is also why they depend on the pentose phosphate pathway for NADPH. Muscle keeps its glycogen for itself, because it lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, and in hard exercise it sends lactate to the liver through the Cori cycle (also called lactic acid cycle).

约 45 秒 · 这一段是串场题，四个器官各一句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Martínková

① 空腹先用肝糖原，然后靠糖异生，长期饥饿时靠酮体 fed → fasting → starvation

After a meal the body stores fuel; in the first hours of fasting the liver uses glycogen; then it relies on gluconeogenesis; and in long starvation ketone bodies take over so that less muscle protein is broken down.

时间线一句话。

② 脂肪组织储存甘油三酯，空腹时释放脂肪酸和甘油 adipose tissue

The fatty acids for that come from adipose tissue, which stores triacylglycerol (also called triglyceride, neutral fat) and releases fatty acids and glycerol in fasting; it also secretes the hormone leptin. Leptin is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

脂肪组织也分泌激素。

③ 心脏主要烧脂肪酸，也用乳酸和酮体 heart

The heart has very many mitochondria and burns mainly fatty acids, but it also uses lactate and ketone bodies.

什么都能烧，主要是脂肪酸。

④ 慢肌纤维靠有氧氧化，快肌纤维靠糖酵解 slow vs fast fibres

Skeletal muscle has two main fibre types: slow red fibres use oxidative metabolism for long activity, and fast white fibres use glycolysis for short, intense activity.

红肌耐力，白肌爆发。

⑤ 肌肉用磷酸肌酸在运动最初几秒重新生成 ATP creatine phosphate

In the first seconds of effort, muscle regenerates ATP from creatine phosphate by the enzyme creatine kinase.

最快的 ATP 来源。

⑥ 肌肉把乳酸和丙氨酸送到肝，肝把葡萄糖送回肌肉 Cori cycle · glucose–alanine cycle

After that, working muscle sends lactate and alanine to the liver, and the liver turns them into glucose and sends it back; the liver pays the ATP cost.

Cori 循环（= Cori cycle = lactic acid cycle） ＋ 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环（= glucose–alanine cycle = Cahill cycle）。

⑦ 肾在长期饥饿时也做葡萄糖，并把氨排入尿中调节酸碱平衡 kidney

The kidney also makes glucose in long starvation, and it releases ammonia from glutamine into the urine to help control acid–base balance.

第二个能放葡萄糖的器官。

⑧ 肝做酮体，但肝缺少利用酮体的酶，所以自己不能用 ketone bodies

The liver makes ketone bodies but lacks succinyl-CoA:3-ketoacid CoA transferase, the enzyme needed to use them, so all of them go to other organs.

肝做，给别的器官用。

⑨ 肝用细胞色素 P450 处理药物、乙醇等外源物 ⭐ Hodek xenobiotic metabolism

The liver is also the main site where drugs, ethanol and other xenobiotics are processed, mostly by cytochrome P450 enzymes.

通往他的主场。

⑩ 红细胞从糖酵解的旁路做 2,3-二磷酸甘油酸，降低血红蛋白对氧的亲和力 ⭐ Martínková 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate

Red blood cells make 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate (also called 2,3-BPG, 2,3-DPG) in a side branch of glycolysis, and it lowers the oxygen affinity of haemoglobin, so oxygen is released in the tissues.

代谢和血红蛋白在这里接上。

⑪ 书外：褐色脂肪用解偶联蛋白 1 产热，不产 ATP brown fat · UCP1

Brown adipose tissue contains uncoupling protein 1, which lets protons return across the inner mitochondrial membrane without making ATP, so the energy is released as heat. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑫ 1 型糖尿病没有胰岛素，肝按饥饿状态大量做酮体，血液变酸 diabetic ketoacidosis

In type 1 diabetes there is no insulin, so the liver behaves as in starvation and makes so many ketone bodies that the blood becomes acidic; this is diabetic ketoacidosis.

把前面的饥饿时间线反过来用。

陷阱问法

  • "Why does the brain not use fatty acids?" — Fatty acids cross the blood–brain barrier poorly. · 过不了血脑屏障。
  • "Why do red cells not use ketone bodies?" — They have no mitochondria. · 没有线粒体。
  • "Why does muscle not release its glycogen as glucose?" — It lacks glucose-6-phosphatase. · 缺葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。

定义

  • triacylglycerol — the storage form of fat, three fatty acids on glycerol · 甘油三酯（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat）
  • leptin — a hormone from adipose tissue that reports how much fat is stored · 瘦素
  • creatine phosphate — a high-energy phosphate compound that regenerates ATP in muscle · 磷酸肌酸
  • acid–base balance — keeping blood pH in its normal range · 酸碱平衡
  • xenobiotic — a foreign compound, such as a drug or poison · 外源物
  • 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate（= 2,3-BPG = 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate = 2,3-DPG） — a red-cell molecule that lowers haemoglobin's oxygen affinity · 2,3-二磷酸甘油酸
  • uncoupling protein 1 — a protein in brown fat that lets protons return without making ATP · 解偶联蛋白 1
  • diabetic ketoacidosis — acidic blood caused by too many ketone bodies when insulin is missing · 糖尿病酮症酸中毒
  • blood–brain barrier — the tight wall of brain capillaries that keeps many molecules out · 血脑屏障

Post-translational modification

卡 13 · 06 · 基因只写了序列，功能是修饰出来的（⚠ 这一节原来一页纸上没有）

  1. 什么时候跑 — 翻译当中和翻译之后 一条刚合成的肽链还不是成品。折叠、切割、加基团之后才有功能。 ⚠ 一个基因因此能给出好几种不同的蛋白。
  2. 信号肽（= signal peptide = signal sequence） — 先决定这条蛋白去哪儿 N 端一段 15–30 个氨基酸，中间是一段疏水核心，它就是邮寄地址。 SRP 在翻译刚开头就认出它，把核糖体暂停一下，拖到 ER 膜上的受体那儿， 新生肽链一边合成一边穿过转运通道进 ER 腔（共翻译转运），进去之后信号肽酶把它切掉。 ⚠ 没有信号肽的蛋白就留在胞质里 —— 有没有这一段，决定了这条蛋白的一生走哪条路。 ⚠ 你自己会用到：想让蛋白分泌到周质或培养基，就在前面接一段信号肽（pelB、OmpA 这些）。
  3. N-糖基化 — 在 ER 开始，在 Golgi 完成 ⚠ 正因为蛋白是这样一边合成一边进 ER 的，糖基转移酶才能趁它还没折叠起来就下手 —— 上一步和这一步是连着的，不是两件事。 ⚠ 核心糖链是预先造好的，挂在一个叫 多萜醇（dolichol）的脂类载体上等着。 蛋白正在进入 ER 的时候（共翻译），这条现成的糖链认出序列 Asn-X-Ser/Thr（⚠ X 可以是任何氨基酸，但不能是脯氨酸）， 整条转移到天冬酰胺侧链的酰胺氮上。 ⚠ N 就是从 Asn 来的 —— 接在氮上。之后在 Golgi 里被修剪和加工成最终形态。
  4. O-糖基化 — 在 Golgi，规矩完全不同 接在 Ser 或 Thr 的羟基氧上，一个糖一个糖地加， ⚠ 没有共识序列，也不用脂载体 —— 跟 N-糖基化的三处差别正好成对比： 接在氮 vs 接在氧 · 整条转移 vs 逐个添加 · 有序列要求 vs 没有。
  5. 磷酸化 — 最常见的开关 加在 Ser · Thr · Tyr 的羟基上（⚠ 三个都有 −OH，这就是为什么是它们）。 激酶加上、磷酸酶拿掉，可逆，所以适合当开关。 ⚠ 记那两个反向的例子：糖原磷酸化酶磷酸化后开，PDH 磷酸化后关。
  6. 其余几种，各有各的用处 乙酰化（组蛋白，中和赖氨酸的正电，染色质松开）· 甲基化（组蛋白和 DNA，调控）· 泛素化（降解的标签，送去蛋白酶体）· 羟化（胶原的脯氨酸和赖氨酸，⚠ 需要维生素 C，缺了就是坏血病）· γ-羧化（凝血因子的谷氨酸，⚠ 需要维生素 K）· 蛋白水解激活（酶原切成活性酶，如胰蛋白酶原 → 胰蛋白酶，不可逆）。
  7. 为什么这跟你的实验有关 ⚠ 大肠杆菌不做糖基化，也不太会做二硫键。 所以要表达一个需要糖基化才有功能的真核蛋白，就得换宿主 （酵母、昆虫细胞、哺乳动物细胞）。这是选表达系统时第一个要问的问题。

Say this

A newly made polypeptide is not yet a finished protein, and post-translational modification is why one gene can give several different products. Where a protein is modified depends first on where it is sent, and that is decided by a signal peptide (also called signal sequence): a short hydrophobic stretch at the N-terminus that is recognised by SRP as soon as it emerges, so the ribosome is brought to the ER membrane and the chain is threaded into the ER as it is being made. The signal peptide is then cut off. And that is exactly why N-glycosylation can be co-translational. N-glycosylation begins in the ER and is completed in the Golgi. The core sugar chain is pre-assembled on a lipid carrier called dolichol, and while the protein is still entering the ER that whole chain is transferred onto the amide nitrogen of an asparagine, at the sequence Asn–X–Ser or Thr, where X can be anything except proline. O-glycosylation is different: it happens in the Golgi, the sugars are added one at a time onto the hydroxyl oxygen of serine or threonine, and there is no consensus sequence. The most common regulatory modification is phosphorylation, on the hydroxyls of serine, threonine and tyrosine, and it is reversible, which is what makes it a switch. ⚠ And practically: E. coli does not glycosylate, so a protein that needs its sugars has to be expressed in a eukaryotic host.

约 55 秒 · 「预先造好挂在多萜醇上」和「X 不能是脯氨酸」是两个细节分

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 4 位考官：Martínková、Šulc、Lipovová、Hodek

① 修饰一个已有的蛋白比合成一个新蛋白快得多 speed of regulation

Modifying an existing protein, for example by phosphorylation, takes seconds, while making a new one takes much longer, so modification is how a cell responds quickly.

快，而且多数可逆。

② 二硫键主要在内质网形成，因为内质网是氧化环境，胞质是还原环境 disulfide bonds in the ER

Disulfide bonds, links between two cysteines, depend on location: the ER lumen is oxidising and the cytosol is reducing. So they form in secreted proteins such as antibodies and insulin, helped by protein disulfide isomerase.

分泌蛋白多二硫键，胞质蛋白少。

③ calnexin 是内质网膜蛋白，通过糖链检查蛋白有没有折叠好，折错的被送去降解 ER quality control · ERAD

Calnexin, a membrane protein in the same compartment, uses the N-linked sugar chain to check folding: it holds the protein until it folds. Misfolded proteins are sent back to the cytosol and degraded, which is called ERAD.

糖链也用来查折叠。

④ 泛素是小蛋白，连成链标记的蛋白被蛋白酶体降解 ubiquitin · proteasome

Ubiquitin, a small protein, marks them for that degradation: a chain of it is attached to lysine residues, and the proteasome destroys the tagged protein; cyclins are removed this way to move the cell cycle on.

接细胞周期。

⑤ 胰岛素原切成胰岛素，凝血酶原切成凝血酶，胰蛋白酶原在肠里才被切开 proteolytic activation

Cutting is also used to switch proteins on: proinsulin is cut into insulin and C-peptide, prothrombin into thrombin in blood clotting, and trypsinogen is activated only in the intestine by enteropeptidase, so the pancreas does not digest itself.

切一刀才有活性，不可逆。

⑥ 细菌的二组分系统磷酸化的是组氨酸 ⭐ Martínková histidine kinase

Phosphorylation is not only on serine, threonine and tyrosine: in bacterial two-component systems a histidine kinase phosphorylates its own histidine and passes the phosphate to an aspartate of a response regulator.

Martínková 的 AfGcHK 就是这样的血红素激酶。

⑦ Ras 蛋白加上法尼基脂链才能固定在膜上 lipidation · farnesylation

A lipid can also be attached: Ras carries a farnesyl group that holds it on the inner face of the plasma membrane, and GPI anchors hold some proteins on the outer face.

修饰决定蛋白在哪儿。

⑧ 每种修饰增加一个固定质量，质谱能测出来，基因序列看不出来 ⭐ Šulc mass spectrometry

None of these modifications is visible in the gene sequence, but each adds a defined mass, so mass spectrometry detects them — phosphorylation, for example, adds about 80 daltons.

通往 Šulc。

⑨ 酵母展示的蛋白经过内质网分泌到表面，所以能形成二硫键、被糖基化 ⭐ Lipovová yeast display · host choice

This matters in my own work: in yeast display my protein is fused to Aga2, a yeast cell-wall protein, and the fusion passes through the ER to the surface, so it can form disulfide bonds and be glycosylated, although yeast adds many more mannose residues than human cells.

选宿主是 Lipovová 的问题。

⑩ 肝对外源物也加基团：葡萄糖醛酸、硫酸根、谷胱甘肽 ⭐ Hodek phase II conjugation

The liver attaches groups to foreign small molecules too: in phase II of xenobiotic metabolism it adds glucuronic acid, sulfate or glutathione, which makes them water-soluble so they can be excreted.

Hodek 的第二相。

⑪ 书外：甘露糖-6-磷酸把酶送进溶酶体，缺了就是 I-cell 病 mannose-6-phosphate

A mannose-6-phosphate group added in the Golgi sends enzymes to the lysosome; in I-cell disease this group is missing, the enzymes are secreted, and undigested material builds up. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Does E. coli glycosylate?" — Essentially no. · 基本不做。
  • "Why do cytosolic proteins rarely have disulfides?" — The cytosol is reducing. · 胞质是还原环境。
  • "Is proteolytic activation reversible?" — No, the cut is permanent. · 不可逆。

定义

  • protein disulfide isomerase — an ER enzyme that forms and rearranges disulfide bonds · 蛋白二硫键异构酶
  • calnexin — an ER membrane protein that binds glycoproteins until they fold · 钙联蛋白
  • ERAD — ER-associated degradation of misfolded proteins · 内质网相关降解
  • proteasome — a large protease complex that degrades ubiquitin-tagged proteins · 蛋白酶体
  • response regulator — the protein that receives the phosphate in a two-component system · 响应调节蛋白
  • farnesyl group — a 15-carbon lipid chain attached to some proteins · 法尼基
  • GPI anchor — a glycolipid that fixes a protein to the outer membrane surface · GPI 锚
  • xenobiotic — a foreign compound, such as a drug · 外源物
  • lysosome — the digestive organelle of the cell · 溶酶体

Shuttles & inter-organ cycles

⚠ 散在各条路里、单独看都很小，但合起来只有七个 —— 它们回答的是同一个问题：膜挡住了这个分子，细胞怎么把它弄过去

  1. 总规矩 — 过不去就换个身份 膜不让某个分子过，细胞的办法从来不是硬闯，而是把它变成另一个能过的分子，到对面再变回来。 ⚠ 七个穿梭全是这一招，区别只在换成了什么、以及回程怎么走。
  2. 柠檬酸穿梭 — 把 acetyl-CoA 运出线粒体 acetyl-CoA 过不了内膜 → 跟草酰乙酸缩合成柠檬酸坐转运体出来 → 胞质里 ATP-citrate lyase 劈回来。 ⚠ 回程顺手造 NADPH（草酰乙酸 → 苹果酸 → malic enzyme → 丙酮酸）。 给脂肪酸合成用的。
  3. 肉碱穿梭 — 把长链脂酰基运进线粒体 方向正好反过来。CPT-1 把酰基换到肉碱上 → translocase 换进去 → CPT-2 换回 CoA。 ⚠ CPT-1 是 β-氧化的限速门，被丙二酰-CoA 关掉 —— 所以这两个穿梭是互锁的：一个开，另一个就关。
  4. 苹果酸-天冬氨酸穿梭 — 把胞质的 NADH 运进去（肝、心） NADH 本身过不去 → 在胞质把草酰乙酸还原成苹果酸进去 → 在基质再氧化回去， 在里面交出一个 NADH。⚠ 值 2.5 个 ATP。
  5. 甘油磷酸穿梭 — 同样的事，但便宜一档（肌肉、脑） 把氢交给 DHAP 变成甘油-3-磷酸，在膜上交给 FAD → 出来的是 FADH₂。 ⚠ 只值 1.5 个 ATP。 ⚠ 这两条的差别，就是「一个葡萄糖 30 还是 32」的全部答案。
  6. Cori 循环（= Cori cycle = lactic acid cycle） — 把乳酸从肌肉搬到肝（器官之间） 肌肉缺氧产乳酸 → 血 → 肝做糖异生变回葡萄糖 → 血 → 回肌肉。 ⚠ 这不是省能量，是转移负担：肝花 6 个 ATP 造出的葡萄糖，肌肉烧了只得 2 个。 肌肉把账单寄给了肝。
  7. 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环（= glucose–alanine cycle = Cahill cycle） — 同一条路，顺便把氮也捎上 肌肉里丙酮酸接上一个氨基变成丙氨酸再送去肝； 肝把氨基摘下来进尿素循环，碳架去糖异生。 ⚠ 一趟车运两样东西：Cori 循环只运碳，这一条碳和氮一起运。
  8. 尿素循环（= urea cycle = ornithine cycle = Krebs–Henseleit cycle）那一对进出 — 跨两个区室 瓜氨酸要从线粒体出到胞质，鸟氨酸要从胞质回线粒体。 ⚠ 所以尿素循环「两步在线粒体、三步在胞质」不是随口一说，中间真有一对转运体在跑。

Say this

Several pathways are split across a membrane, and the cell always solves that the same way: it converts the molecule into something that can cross, and converts it back on the other side. Acetyl-CoA leaves the mitochondrion as citrate for fatty acid synthesis, and the return leg also generates NADPH. Fatty acyl groups go the other way on carnitine, and carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1) is the rate-limiting step, inhibited by malonyl-CoA — so the two shuttles are interlocked. Cytosolic NADH cannot cross either: the malate–aspartate shuttle delivers NADH and is worth 2.5 ATP, while the glycerol phosphate shuttle delivers FADH₂ and is worth only 1.5, which is why a glucose is worth thirty or thirty-two depending on the tissue. Between organs, the Cori cycle (also called lactic acid cycle) sends lactate from muscle to the liver and glucose back, and the glucose–alanine cycle (also called Cahill cycle) does the same journey but carries the amino nitrogen as well.

约 55 秒 · 「过不去就换个身份」是这一整节的那句总纲

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Ryšlavá

① 线粒体内膜几乎不让任何分子通过，因为它要维持质子梯度 impermeable inner membrane

All these shuttles exist because the inner mitochondrial membrane must keep the proton gradient, so it is almost impermeable, while the outer membrane has porins and lets small molecules through.

内膜不通，外膜通。

② ATP 也要靠 ADP/ATP 转位酶运出线粒体，这要消耗一部分质子梯度 ADP/ATP translocase

For the same reason ATP leaves the matrix on the ADP/ATP translocase, in exchange for ADP, and phosphate enters with a proton, so part of the gradient is spent on transport.

所以现代 ATP 数是 2.5 和 1.5 这样的小数。

③ 叶绿体用磷酸丙糖/磷酸转运体把糖运出去 ⭐ Ryšlavá triose phosphate/phosphate translocator

Chloroplasts use the same principle: the triose phosphate/phosphate translocator exports triose phosphate to the cytosol and brings in one phosphate for each.

一出一进。

④ C4 植物在叶肉细胞和维管束鞘细胞之间用苹果酸运 CO₂ ⭐ Ryšlavá C4 · malic enzyme

In C4 plants CO₂ is fixed by PEP carboxylase in mesophyll cells and carried as malate to bundle-sheath cells, where malic enzyme releases it — the same malic enzyme that makes NADPH in the citrate shuttle.

PEPC 和苹果酸酶是 Ryšlavá 的酶。

⑤ 红细胞没有线粒体，一直产乳酸，所以 Cori 循环安静时也在转 red blood cells

Back in animals, red blood cells have no mitochondria, so they always end glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) with lactate, and the Cori cycle runs even at rest.

不只是运动时。

⑥ 肝合成酮体给脑和肌肉用，肝自己缺少琥珀酰 CoA 转移酶（thiophorase）所以不能用 ketone bodies

The liver also sends ketone bodies to the brain and muscle in fasting, but it cannot use them itself because it lacks thiophorase, the enzyme that activates acetoacetate.

肝只出不用。

⑦ 多数组织以谷氨酰胺的形式把氨运到肝和肾 glutamine

Besides alanine from muscle, most tissues send ammonia to the liver and kidney as glutamine, made by glutamine synthetase, because free ammonia is toxic.

接尿素循环。

⑧ 饥饿时胰高血糖素使丙二酰 CoA 下降，CPT-1 解除抑制，脂肪酸进入线粒体 glucagon · insulin

The carnitine shuttle is under hormonal control: in fasting glucagon inhibits acetyl-CoA carboxylase, malonyl-CoA falls and CPT-1 is released; after a meal insulin does the opposite.

接激素调控。

⑨ 肉碱或 CPT 缺陷时，饥饿会引起肌无力和低血糖 carnitine deficiency

When carnitine or CPT is deficient, long-chain fatty acids cannot be oxidised, so fasting or long exercise causes muscle weakness and hypoglycaemia.

病。

⑩ 书外：昆虫飞行肌主要用甘油磷酸穿梭 insect flight muscle

The glycerol phosphate shuttle is especially active in insect flight muscle, where it transfers electrons quickly although it gives less ATP. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Can NADH cross the inner membrane?" — No, only its electrons are carried across by a shuttle. · 只运电子。
  • "Does the Cori cycle save energy?" — No, the liver spends more ATP than the muscle gains. · 不省能量。
  • "Why is the outer membrane permeable?" — It contains porins. · 有孔蛋白。

定义

  • proton gradient — the H⁺ difference across the inner membrane that drives ATP synthase（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase） · 质子梯度
  • porin — a channel protein in the outer membrane · 孔蛋白
  • triose phosphate — a three-carbon sugar phosphate, the main sugar exported from chloroplasts · 磷酸丙糖
  • C4 plant — a plant that first fixes CO₂ into a four-carbon acid in mesophyll cells · C4 植物
  • thiophorase — the enzyme that activates acetoacetate with CoA, absent in liver · 琥珀酰 CoA 转移酶
  • CPT-1 — carnitine palmitoyltransferase（= carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1） I, the controlled entry step of fatty acids into mitochondria · 肉碱棕榈酰转移酶 I
  • hypoglycaemia — low blood glucose · 低血糖

Central dogma

Replication & repair

卡 16 · 一条连续、一条断成片，都因为只能 5′→3′

  1. 什么时候跑 — S 期，而且只跑一次 半保留：每条新双链都是一条老链配一条新链。 ⚠ 新链永远 5′→3′ 延伸 —— 后面所有的麻烦都是这一条规矩逼出来的。
  2. 开链 — 三个零件先上 解旋酶把双链拉开（烧 ATP）· SSB 撑住单链不让它回粘 · 拓扑异构酶在前面把拧紧的超螺旋切开再接上（不然越解越拧死）。
  3. 引物 — 聚合酶不会从零开始 DNA 聚合酶只能往已有的 3′-OH 上接，所以要 primase 先做一小段 RNA 引物。 ⚠ 用 RNA 当引物是故意的：它后面会被整段换掉，等于把最容易出错的开头部分丢弃。
  4. 延伸 — 领头连续，随从断成冈崎片段 模板反平行，而新链只能 5′→3′，所以一条能顺着叉一直走（领头链）， 另一条只能倒着一段一段做（随从链）。原核主力是 pol Ⅲ： α 亚基聚合 · ε 亚基 3′→5′ 校对 · β 亚基是滑动夹（把酶箍在模板上，决定持续合成能力）。
  5. 收尾 — 换引物、封口 pol Ⅰ 用它的 5′→3′ 外切酶把 RNA 引物切掉、同时用聚合酶活性补上 DNA； 连接酶把缺口接成磷酸二酯键。
  6. 保真 — 三道关，各降几个数量级 碱基配对约 10⁻⁴–10⁻⁵ → 校对再降 2–3 个数量级 → 错配修复再降。 ⚠ 错配修复怎么认出哪条是新链？细菌靠 GATC 的半甲基化（新链还没来得及甲基化）， 真核靠新链上的缺口。
  7. 修复五类 — Don't Be Nervous, Mum's Driving 直接逆转（光复活酶拆 UV 二聚体）· BER（糖苷酶挖单个碱基 → AP 位点 → AP 内切酶）· NER（大块损伤两侧各一刀；⚠ 坏了得着色性干皮病）· MMR · 双链断裂（HR 用姐妹染色单体、经 Holliday junction，准；NHEJ 直接接，快但出错）。 ⚠ 书上只有半句（p079：pol Ⅰ 切除 UV 嘧啶二聚体再补缺口）—— 那就是切除修复，书只是没给名字。先说书那句，再挂通用名。
  8. 真核多出来的麻烦 — 末端 线性染色体的随从链末端，引物一拆就缺一小段，每复制一次短一点。 端粒是那段可牺牲的重复序列，端粒酶（自带 RNA 模板的逆转录酶）能补回去 —— 大多数体细胞不表达它，干细胞和癌细胞表达。

Say this

Replication is semiconservative and semidiscontinuous, and the reason for the second one is that the new strand can only grow 5′ to 3′. Helicase opens the duplex, single-strand binding protein holds it open, and topoisomerase relieves the supercoiling ahead of the fork. Because DNA polymerase cannot start from nothing, primase lays down a short RNA primer. Pol III then extends — continuously on the leading strand, in Okazaki fragments on the lagging strand — with its ε subunit proofreading and its β subunit acting as a sliding clamp. Pol I replaces the primers and ligase seals the nicks. Accuracy comes in three layers: base pairing, proofreading, and mismatch repair, and mismatch repair knows which strand is new because the new strand is not yet methylated.

约 55 秒 · 「只能 5′→3′，所以随从链断成片」是这段的因果

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① 胞嘧啶脱氨会变成尿嘧啶，DNA 用胸腺嘧啶，所以 DNA 里的 U 一定是损伤 why thymine

DNA uses thymine instead of uracil because cytosine can deaminate to uracil; any uracil in DNA is therefore damage and is removed by uracil-DNA glycosylase.

接碱基切除修复。

② 细菌环状染色体一个复制起点，真核染色体有很多个 one origin vs many

A bacterial chromosome is circular with one origin, oriC; eukaryotic chromosomes are long and linear, so they have many origins, each used only once per cell cycle.

环形没有末端问题。

③ Meselson 和 Stahl 用 ¹⁵N 重同位素证明复制是半保留的 Meselson–Stahl

Meselson and Stahl showed that replication is semiconservative: bacteria grown in ¹⁵N were moved to ¹⁴N, and after one generation all DNA had an intermediate density in a caesium chloride gradient.

「怎么知道的」。

④ PCR 里加热代替解旋酶，合成引物代替引物酶，Taq 聚合酶负责延伸 PCR

The same logic is used in PCR: heat separates the strands instead of helicase, synthetic DNA primers replace primase, and heat-stable Taq polymerase extends them.

接「克隆与 PCR」。

⑤ AZT 和阿昔洛韦是缺少 3′-OH 的核苷类似物，使 DNA 链终止 AZT · acyclovir

Chain termination is also a drug strategy: AZT against HIV and acyclovir against herpes are nucleoside analogues that lack a usable 3′-OH, like the ddNTPs in Sanger sequencing.

和 Sanger 测序同一原理。

⑥ 环丙沙星抑制细菌的 DNA 旋转酶，依托泊苷抑制人的拓扑异构酶 Ⅱ ciprofloxacin · etoposide

Topoisomerases are drug targets too: ciprofloxacin, a quinolone, inhibits bacterial DNA gyrase (also called DNA gyrase, topoisomerase II (bacteria)), and the cancer drug etoposide inhibits human topoisomerase II.

接抗生素和抗癌药。

⑦ 苯并芘是烟草烟雾里的致癌物，被细胞色素 P450 活化成能结合 DNA 的环氧化物 ⭐ Hodek benzo[a]pyrene · DNA adduct

DNA can also be damaged by what the liver does to foreign compounds: benzo[a]pyrene, from tobacco smoke, is turned by cytochrome P450 into an epoxide that forms a DNA adduct, and if nucleotide excision repair misses it, a mutation results.

通往 Hodek。

⑧ 病毒由核酸和蛋白衣壳组成，有的外面还有来自宿主的脂质包膜 virus · capsid · envelope

A virus is a genome of DNA or RNA inside a protein capsid, in some viruses surrounded by a lipid envelope taken from the host membrane, and it can replicate only inside a host cell.

离开细胞不能复制。

⑨ DNA 病毒用 DNA 聚合酶，RNA 病毒带 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶，逆转录病毒用逆转录酶 DNA · RNA · retrovirus

How a virus replicates depends on its genome: DNA viruses such as herpesviruses use a DNA polymerase, RNA viruses such as influenza bring an RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, and retroviruses such as HIV use reverse transcriptase and integrase to put DNA into the host genome.

宿主细胞没有后两种酶。

⑩ 大多数 RNA 病毒的聚合酶没有校对，所以突变快；冠状病毒有校对 no proofreading

Because most RNA-dependent RNA polymerases have no proofreading, RNA viruses mutate fast; coronaviruses are the exception, with a proofreading exonuclease, which is why their genome can be about thirty thousand nucleotides long.

保真度限制基因组长度。

⑪ 噬菌体可以裂解宿主，也可以作为原噬菌体整合进染色体；DE3 就是 λ 原噬菌体 lytic vs lysogenic

Bacteriophages, viruses of bacteria, either replicate and lyse the cell, or integrate into the chromosome as a prophage, which is lysogeny; the DE3 in BL21(DE3) is a λ prophage carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene.

接我自己的表达菌株。

⑫ 易错 PCR 故意降低保真度来建突变库 ⭐ Lipovová error-prone PCR

In the lab I sometimes want the opposite of fidelity: error-prone PCR, with Taq, extra manganese and unequal dNTPs, makes a random mutant library, but it favours transitions over transversions.

Lipovová 出过这道题。

⑬ 书外：细菌用 CRISPR 保存噬菌体 DNA 片段，下次用 Cas9 切掉同一种噬菌体 CRISPR–Cas

Bacteria store short phage DNA sequences in their CRISPR locus, and Cas9 uses RNA copies of them to cut the same phage on the next infection. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is a virus alive?" — It cannot replicate outside a host cell. · 离开细胞不能复制。
  • "Does proofreading mean no mutations?" — No, far fewer. · 只是少很多。
  • "Why not use Taq for cloning?" — It has no 3′→5′ proofreading. · 不校对。

定义

  • deamination — loss of an amino group; turns cytosine into uracil · 脱氨
  • oriC — the single origin of replication in E. coli · 大肠杆菌复制起点
  • nucleoside analogue — a drug that resembles a nucleoside and is built into DNA · 核苷类似物
  • DNA gyrase — the bacterial topoisomerase that relieves supercoiling · DNA 旋转酶（= DNA gyrase = topoisomerase II (bacteria)）
  • DNA adduct — a chemical bound covalently to DNA · DNA 加合物
  • capsid — the protein shell of a virus · 衣壳
  • envelope — a lipid membrane around some viruses, taken from the host · 包膜
  • integrase — the retroviral enzyme that inserts viral DNA into the host genome · 整合酶
  • prophage — a phage genome integrated in the host chromosome · 原噬菌体
  • transition — a purine-to-purine or pyrimidine-to-pyrimidine mutation · 转换

Viruses

横向卡 x-vir · 先问「基因组是什么形态、到 mRNA 要走几步」，这一句定了它必须自带什么酶

  1. 定义 — 核酸装在蛋白外壳里，只能在活细胞内复制 衣壳是包住基因组的蛋白外壳；有的病毒外面还有一层包膜，是从宿主的膜上带走的脂膜，病毒的表面蛋白插在上面。 它缺三样：核糖体、代谢酶、产能机制，所以自己既造不出蛋白也造不出 ATP。 ⚠ 「算不算生命」的答案是有争议，通常说成处于生命边缘的实体。别说「最小的生物」。
  2. 分类 — Baltimore 七类，判据只有一句 判据：基因组是什么形态，以及从它到 mRNA 要走几步。 七类：双链 DNA · 单链 DNA · 双链 RNA · 正链 RNA · 负链 RNA · 经过 DNA 的单链 RNA · 经过 RNA 的双链 DNA。 最后两类是逆转录的。⚠ 不按长相、不按宿主、不按疾病分 —— 先把判据说出口。
  3. 复制周期 — 六步，只在第四步分家 吸附受体 → 进入 → 脱壳 → 复制与表达 → 组装 → 释放（裂解或出芽）。 七类的差别只落在第四步：基因组形态决定这一步必须干什么。 吸附那一步决定宿主范围，因为受体决定哪些细胞能被感染。
  4. 正链与负链 — 基因组本身能不能当 mRNA 读 正链：序列和方向跟 mRNA 一样，宿主核糖体直接翻译，所以裸基因组就有感染性。 负链：是信使的互补链，必须先被抄成正链；宿主细胞没有 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶，所以颗粒里必须自带。 同一个判据，两个相反的后果 —— 这是最好的一问一答。
  5. 逆转录病毒 — RNA 先变成 DNA，再插进宿主基因组 HIV 这类逆转录病毒用逆转录酶把 RNA 基因组抄成 DNA，再由整合酶插进宿主染色体。 宿主细胞既没有逆转录酶，也没有整合酶。 这一类顺带把中心法则补完：RNA → DNA 是原始版本里没有的流向。
  6. 突变快 — 大多数 RNA 病毒的聚合酶不校对 没有校对，每复制一次错很多，所以基因组长度被保真度封住。 冠状病毒是例外：聚合酶复合体里有带外切酶活性的校对模块，基因组能到约三万个核苷酸，是已知 RNA 病毒里最长的。 ⚠ 有校对不等于不变异，只是每个碱基出错少。
  7. 噬菌体 — 感染细菌的病毒，两条路 裂解周期：劫持宿主机器造噬菌体、组装、裂解细胞放出子代。 溶原周期：基因组整合进细菌染色体成为原噬菌体，跟着被动复制，受到胁迫信号再转入裂解。两条路都能走的叫温和噬菌体，λ 是典型。 BL21(DE3) 里的 DE3 就是一个 λ 原噬菌体 —— 接我自己的表达菌株。
  8. 负链病毒自带的那台机器 — 真底物不是裸 RNA 负链基因组读不了，宿主细胞又没有 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶，所以颗粒里必须预先装好自己那一个。 那个酶是一条很大的蛋白（常记作 L），而且它对裸露的 RNA 活性很低：真底物是核糖核蛋白——基因组被核蛋白整根包起来之后的形态。 L 还要靠一个搭档亚基（很多科里是磷蛋白）才能接近模板。为什么要包起来：包着的基因组不会被宿主的先天免疫传感器当成裸露的外来 RNA 认出来，也不容易被降解；代价是聚合酶得边走边把外壳掀开再盖回去。
  9. 负链病毒的两个花招 — 抢帽与转录梯度 抢帽：有些分节段的负链病毒不自己造帽子，而是从宿主的前体 mRNA 上切下一段带帽的短片段当引物起头 —— 所以病毒信使最前面那几个碱基来自宿主。（冠状病毒走的是另一条路：自己带甲基转移酶造帽。） 转录梯度：聚合酶只从基因组一端的单一位点进入，每过一个基因间隔区都有一定概率掉下来，概率会累积。 所以离入口近的基因转录得多，远的少 —— 表达量由基因排列的顺序决定。⚠ 这里没有任何调控，梯度只是几何的后果。
  10. 冠状病毒的因果链 — 有校对 → 基因组能最长 → 才需要一套新办法 冠状病毒的每一个特点都从一条因果链推下来，说的时候先给因果再给名字： 聚合酶复合体里有一个带外切酶活性的校对模块 → 突破了「保真度封住长度」这个上限，基因组能到约三万个核苷酸 → 基因组这么长，有用的基因离头部很远，就必须有一套高效表达下游基因的办法 → 那个办法就是不连续转录。 ⚠ 校对不等于修复：校对发生在合成的当时，由聚合酶那一侧自己完成；修复是事后另一套酶去改。
  11. 一个移码位点 — 一条信使读出两条多聚蛋白，比例固定 基因组前三分之二编码两条多聚蛋白。第一条读到一个终止密码子就停；但它上游有一段滑动序列，下游有一个 RNA 假结。 假结挡住核糖体、造出张力，于是一部分核糖体往回退一个核苷酸、换到新的读框，直接读过那个终止密码子，读出更长的第二条。效率大约三成。 ⚠ 这不是移码突变：移码突变是插入或缺失造成的错误，这里是基因组自带的机制，而且比例固定——那正是病毒控制两组蛋白用量的办法。
  12. 不连续转录 — 一整套嵌套信使，共用同一段前导序列 为什么需要它：真核核糖体基本只翻译一条信使最前面那个阅读框，所以要表达靠后的基因，就得做一条以那个基因开头的信使。 怎么做到：合成负链时，聚合酶走到两个基因之间的调控序列处发生模板跳跃，跳到基因组一端那段共享的前导序列上接着延伸。 在不同的间隔处各跳一次，就得到一整套彼此嵌套、末端都带着同一段前导的信使；在哪个间隔跳，决定哪个基因排在最前面。 ⚠ 这里的「前导序列」和操纵子衰减子的前导同名不同物，被问到主动说破。
  13. 双膜囊泡 — 把复制现场关起来 复制不是在胞质里裸着做的：几个病毒的跨膜蛋白把宿主的膜掰成双膜囊泡，复制在囊泡内部进行。 目的是藏起来：复制中间体里有双链 RNA，而胞质里的先天免疫传感器认的正是双链 RNA——正常细胞质里不该有长的双链 RNA，所以它是一个干净的「外来」信号。 囊泡上有专门的孔道把新合成的 RNA 运到胞质去。⚠ 这不是细胞原来就有的细胞器：脂来自宿主，形状是病毒搭的。
  14. ⭐ 两类关键蛋白（一）结构蛋白 — 被装进颗粒的那四个 先把分界说清楚：结构蛋白是被包装进病毒颗粒里、随颗粒一起出去的那些；非结构蛋白不进颗粒，是在被感染的细胞里现做出来、负责把基因组抄出来的那些。 结构蛋白四个： 刺突 S——结合受体、驱动融合； 膜蛋白 M——数量最多，决定颗粒的形状； 包膜蛋白 E——很小，管出芽； 核蛋白 N——把基因组结合成核糖核蛋白（跟负链病毒那一格是同一个道理：包起来才藏得住）。 N 还值得多一句：它有内在无序区，能发生液液相分离，一般认为这既跟基因组包装有关，也跟拮抗先天免疫有关。⚠ 无序不等于没功能，那正是它能结合很多不同伙伴的原因。
  15. ⭐ 两类关键蛋白（二）非结构蛋白 — 干活的那一组，按功能记六样 按功能记，别按编号记： ① 聚合酶核心（合成 RNA）· ② 两个病毒蛋白酶（把多聚蛋白切开）· ③ 解旋酶（拆开前方的二级结构）· ④ 带外切酶活性的校对模块 · ⑤ 甲基转移酶（给信使加帽）· ⑥ 核酸内切酶。它们组装成转录复制复合体。 蛋白酶那一条要说出后半句：正因为能自己切开，才可以把所有东西编成两条长多聚蛋白——用少数几个阅读框装很多蛋白（和酶原、propeptide 是同一个逻辑）。 ⚠ 解旋酶是单独一个亚基，不是聚合酶的一个结构域。加帽要紧是因为没帽子的信使在真核细胞里基本不被翻译，而且帽子让它看起来像宿主自己的信使。 第三组是辅助蛋白：不搭颗粒、也不直接合成 RNA，主要拮抗干扰素通路、干扰免疫信号；很多在培养皿里不是复制必需的，但决定病毒在真实宿主里能不能站住。
  16. 刺突 — 一类融合蛋白，两半分工，还有一道构象的门 刺突是三聚体，两半各管一件事：S1 结合受体，S2 把病毒膜和细胞膜融合。 受体结合结构域在 S1 上，有关闭和开放两种构象，只有开放的那个能碰到受体——所以结合这一步是被构象变化把着门的（接别构和 Kd：表观亲和力里混着这个平衡）。 S1 与 S2 交界处有一个蛋白酶切位点：在细胞表面就被切开，就在表面融合；进了内体才被切，就跟内体膜融合——切在哪儿，就在哪儿融合。不同冠状病毒用不同的受体，受体本身就是宿主范围的决定因素。
  17. ⭐ ACE2 是什么 —— 它本来是一个酶，跟病毒毫无关系 ACE2 ＝ 血管紧张素转化酶 2，长在细胞膜上，朝着细胞外面。 它的本职工作是管血压：血管紧张素 Ⅱ 升血压，ACE2 把它降解掉，所以 ACE2 是降压的那一头。 ⚠ ACE 和 ACE2 是两个不同的酶，方向相反：ACE 造出血管紧张素 Ⅱ，ACE2 拆掉它。 两者是同源蛋白，但降压药里的 ACE 抑制剂不抑制 ACE2——这一句最容易被问住。
  18. ⭐ 病毒怎么用它 —— 受体只是一块认得出的表面，跟酶活性无关 刺突结合的是 ACE2 的外表面，不是它的活性位点，所以病毒不需要 ACE2 的酶活性， 抑制这个酶也挡不住感染。这一句是落点：受体对病毒来说只是一块认得出的表面，这个蛋白自己干什么活，病毒不关心。 ACE2 长在肺、肠、肾、心和血管上，所以感染不只是呼吸道的事。 不同物种的 ACE2 有小差别，结合的强弱就不一样，所以它是宿主范围的决定因素之一。

Say this

A virus is a piece of nucleic acid inside a protein capsid, in some viruses wrapped in a lipid envelope taken from the host membrane. It has no ribosomes, no metabolism and no way to make energy, so it can replicate only inside a living cell. Viruses are grouped by the Baltimore classification: by what the genome is made of, and how many steps separate it from messenger RNA. That gives seven classes. Every virus runs the same six steps: attachment to a receptor, entry, uncoating, replication and expression, assembly, and release. The classes differ only in step four. A positive-strand RNA genome is read directly by the ribosome, so the naked genome is infectious. A negative-strand genome must be copied first, and because the host has no RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, the particle carries its own. Retroviruses, such as HIV, use reverse transcriptase to make DNA, and integrase inserts that DNA into the host genome.

约 60 秒 · 两个必说点：分类的判据是「到 mRNA 要走几步」；宿主没有 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶，所以负链病毒必须自带

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Ryšlavá、Hudeček

① 整合进宿主染色体的逆转录病毒 DNA 叫原病毒 provirus

Once inserted, the retroviral DNA is called a provirus, and it is copied together with the host chromosome at every cell division.

和噬菌体的「原噬菌体」是同一个思路。

② 大多数 RNA 病毒的聚合酶不校对，所以突变快；冠状病毒有校对 no proofreading

Most RNA-dependent RNA polymerases have no proofreading, so RNA viruses mutate fast. Coronaviruses are the exception: a proofreading exonuclease lets their genome reach about thirty thousand nucleotides.

保真度封住基因组长度。

③ RNA 病毒群体是一团相关序列，叫准种 quasispecies

A quasispecies is an RNA virus population seen as a cloud of related sequences, and selection acts on the whole cloud rather than on one genome.

群体的单位是分布，不是一条序列。

④ 抗病毒药打病毒自己的酶，因为宿主没有这些酶 acyclovir · AZT · protease inhibitors

Acyclovir against herpesviruses and AZT against HIV are nucleoside analogues that stop the viral DNA chain. HIV protease inhibitors block the cutting of the viral polyprotein; that one is general knowledge.

AZT、阿昔洛韦和 Sanger 测序同一原理。

⑤ 书外：奥司他韦抑制流感病毒的神经氨酸酶，新病毒放不出去 oseltamivir · neuraminidase

Oseltamivir, an influenza drug, inhibits neuraminidase, the viral enzyme that releases new particles from the cell surface. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

打的是第六步「释放」。

⑥ 书外：疫苗让免疫系统在感染之前先见过病毒的抗原 vaccines

A vaccine presents a killed or weakened virus, a viral protein, or an mRNA encoding that protein, so the immune system meets the antigen before the infection. That is general knowledge.

一句话够了。

⑦ 植物病毒遵守同样的规则；马铃薯 Y 病毒感染烟草 ⭐ Ryšlavá potato virus Y

Plant viruses follow the same rules. Potato virus Y, a positive-strand RNA virus, makes one long polyprotein that its own proteases cut; that detail is general knowledge. Ryšlavá's group studies how infected tobacco changes its metabolism.

接她的主场：逆境下代谢怎么变。

⑧ 噬菌体只把基因组注入细菌，衣壳留在外面 bacteriophage · Hershey–Chase

Bacteriophages, viruses of bacteria, attach to a specific surface receptor and inject only their genome, while the capsid stays outside. That observation lies behind the Hershey–Chase experiment.

证明遗传物质是 DNA，不是蛋白。

⑨ 噬菌体把宿主 DNA 带到另一个细菌里，叫转导 transduction

Transduction, one of the three routes of horizontal gene transfer, is when a phage carries host DNA from one bacterium into another.

另外两条：转化、接合。

⑩ BL21(DE3) 里的 DE3 是 λ 原噬菌体，带 T7 RNA 聚合酶基因 λ DE3

DE3 is a λ prophage in the E. coli chromosome carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene under a lacUV5 promoter, so IPTG switches on my pET construct.

没有 DE3，pET 质粒就不表达。

⑪ 噬菌体展示把蛋白挂在 M13 的衣壳蛋白上，基因装在颗粒里面 phage display

Phage display fuses a protein to an M13 coat protein, such as pIII, so the protein is outside and its DNA is packaged inside the same particle.

蛋白和基因连在一起，筛到就能读出来。

⑫ 书外：病毒载体把基因送进人细胞 viral vectors

Viral vectors, such as adenoviruses and lentiviruses with their disease genes removed, are used to deliver genes into human cells for gene therapy and vaccines. That is general knowledge.

病毒的进入本领被拿来用。

⑬ 书外：细菌用 CRISPR 保存噬菌体 DNA 片段，下次用 Cas9 切掉同一种噬菌体 CRISPR–Cas

Bacteria store short phage DNA sequences in their CRISPR locus, and Cas9 uses RNA copies of them to cut the same phage on the next infection. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑭ 负链病毒聚合酶真正的模板是核糖核蛋白，不是裸 RNA ribonucleoprotein template

The polymerase of a negative-strand virus, one very large protein called L, works on the ribonucleoprotein: the genome wrapped along its whole length in nucleoprotein.

包起来才不被当成外来 RNA。

⑮ 负链病毒的基因排列顺序本身就定了表达量 transcription gradient

The polymerase enters at one end and can fall off at every junction between two genes, so genes near the entry point are transcribed far more than distant ones.

没有调控，只是几何的后果。

⑯ 冠状病毒用一个移码位点从一条信使读出两条多聚蛋白 programmed frameshift

A slippery sequence and a downstream RNA pseudoknot make about thirty per cent of ribosomes change frame, so one message gives both polyproteins in a fixed ratio.

比例固定，是控制用量的办法。

⑰ 不连续转录做出一整套共享同一段前导序列的嵌套信使 discontinuous transcription · shared leader

While making the negative strand the polymerase switches template at a junction and lands on one shared leader sequence, producing a nested set of messages.

真核核糖体只读最前面那个阅读框。

⑱ 复制在双膜囊泡里进行，双链 RNA 被藏开胞质里的传感器 double-membrane vesicles

Viral membrane proteins bend host membranes into double-membrane vesicles, and replication inside them hides the double-stranded intermediates from the cytoplasmic sensors that recognise them.

脂是宿主的，形状是病毒的。

⑲ 四个结构蛋白搭出颗粒，随颗粒一起出去 spike · membrane protein · envelope protein

There are four structural proteins. The spike binds the receptor and drives fusion, the membrane protein is the most abundant and sets the particle's shape, the envelope protein works in budding.

结构蛋白＝被包装进颗粒的那些。

⑳ 书外 · general：核蛋白把基因组结合成核糖核蛋白 nucleoprotein

The nucleoprotein, the fourth structural protein, binds the genome into ribonucleoprotein, and its disordered regions allow phase separation linked to packaging and to blocking innate immunity. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

无序不等于没功能。

㉑ 非结构蛋白在被感染的细胞里现做，按功能记六样 non-structural proteins

The non-structural proteins are the working set: the polymerase core, two proteases, a helicase, the proofreading exonuclease module, capping methyltransferases and an endonuclease.

按功能记，别按编号记。

㉒ 这些非结构蛋白组装成转录复制复合体 replication–transcription complex

Together they assemble into the replication and transcription complex, and the proteases are what makes the polyprotein strategy work, because they cut the long chains apart.

解旋酶是单独的亚基，不是聚合酶的结构域。

㉓ 辅助蛋白既不搭颗粒也不合成 RNA，它们对付免疫 accessory proteins

The accessory proteins are a third group. They interfere with the interferon pathway and with immune signalling, and many are not required for replication in culture.

皿里看不出，体内才看得出。

㉔ 刺突分 S1 和 S2 两半，受体结合结构域有关闭和开放两种构象 S1 · S2 · receptor-binding domain

The spike is a trimer with two halves: S1 binds the receptor and S2 fuses the membranes. Only the open conformation of the receptor-binding domain reaches the receptor.

结合被构象把着门。

㉕ 书外 · general：S1 与 S2 交界处的切位点决定病毒走哪条进入路径 cleavage site · entry route

A protease cleavage site at the S1–S2 boundary decides whether fusion happens at the cell surface or inside an endosome. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

切在哪儿，就在哪儿融合。

㉖ ⭐ ACE2 本来是一个管血压的酶，跟病毒无关 angiotensin-converting enzyme 2

ACE2, angiotensin-converting enzyme 2, is an enzyme on the outer surface of the cell membrane, and its normal job is to help control blood pressure.

病毒之前它就在那儿，管血压。

㉗ ACE 升血压，ACE2 降血压，两者是同源蛋白但方向相反 ACE vs ACE2

ACE makes angiotensin II, which raises blood pressure, and ACE2 breaks angiotensin II down again; they are homologous proteins with opposite effects, and ACE inhibitors do not inhibit ACE2.

同源、反向。最常见的陷阱。

㉘ 刺突结合的是 ACE2 的外表面，不是活性位点，所以酶活性跟感染无关 receptor is a surface

The receptor-binding domain of the spike binds the outer surface of ACE2, away from its active site, so the virus does not need the enzyme activity and inhibiting the enzyme does not block infection.

受体 ＝ 认得出的表面，不是功能。

㉙ ACE2 长在肺、肠、肾、心和血管上，所以感染不只是呼吸道的事 tissue distribution

ACE2 is present on lung alveolar cells, intestinal epithelium, kidney, heart and blood vessels, which is why the infection is not only respiratory.

受体在哪儿，病毒就能到哪儿。

㉚ 不同物种的 ACE2 序列有小差别，结合强弱不同，所以它决定宿主范围 ⭐ Hudeček affinity and host range

Small sequence differences in ACE2 between species change how tightly the spike binds, measured as a dissociation constant, so the ACE2 sequence is one of the things that decides which species a coronavirus can infect.

接 Kd 那张卡：宿主范围是一个亲和力问题。

㉛ 书外 · general：可溶性 ACE2 当诱饵，抗体挡住同一个接触面 decoy · antibodies

Two treatments follow from this: soluble ACE2, which binds virus particles before they reach a cell, and monoclonal antibodies, which cover the same contact surface on the spike. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

知道机制就知道怎么挡。

㉜ 书外 · general：ACE2 在 2000 年被发现，2003 年才知道它是受体 discovery

ACE2 was found in 2000 as a homologue of ACE, and only in 2003 was it shown to be the receptor for the first SARS coronavirus; SARS-CoV-2 uses the same receptor. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

先有酶，后知道它被病毒用。

陷阱问法

  • "Is a virus alive?" — Disputed; it has no ribosomes, no metabolism and no energy supply, so it sits at the edge of life. · 有争议，别说「最小的生物」。
  • "Does negative-strand mean damaged?" — No; it is the complement of the message and needs one copying step. · 只是多走一步。
  • "Does proofreading mean coronaviruses do not mutate?" — No; fewer errors per base, but the genome is long and copies are many. · 少，不是没有。
  • "Is programmed frameshifting a frameshift mutation?" — No; it is built into the sequence and makes two proteins in a fixed ratio. · 机制，不是错误。

定义

  • capsid — the protein shell around the viral genome · 衣壳
  • envelope — a lipid membrane around some viruses, taken from the host · 包膜
  • RNA-dependent RNA polymerase — an enzyme that copies RNA from an RNA template; host cells lack it · RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶
  • reverse transcriptase — an enzyme that makes DNA from an RNA template · 逆转录酶
  • integrase — the retroviral enzyme that inserts viral DNA into the host genome · 整合酶
  • provirus — retroviral DNA integrated in the host chromosome · 原病毒
  • prophage — a phage genome integrated in the host chromosome · 原噬菌体
  • temperate phage — a phage that can enter either the lytic or the lysogenic cycle · 温和噬菌体
  • quasispecies — a virus population as a cloud of related sequences · 准种

Transcription

横向卡 x-trans · 不需要引物，这是跟复制最大的差别

  1. 什么时候跑 — 需要这个基因的产物时 ⚠ 转录不需要引物，RNA 聚合酶能从零开始。以 DNA 的模板链（反义链）为模板， 所以新 RNA 的序列跟编码链一致，只是 T 换成 U。
  2. 起始 — σ 亚基认门牌 原核全酶是 α₂ββ′σ。⚠ σ 不参与催化，它唯一的任务是认出启动子、定下起点。 原核启动子认的是两段：−10 区（TATAAT，又叫 Pribnow box）和 −35 区。 认准之后聚合酶把 DNA 撑开一个小泡，开始合成； 起始一旦迈出第一步，σ 就脱落，剩下核心酶 α₂ββ′ 负责延伸。
  3. 延伸 — 读 3′→5′，长 5′→3′ 核心酶沿模板链走，模板读的方向是 3′→5′，所以新 RNA 是 5′→3′ 长的（跟 DNA 复制同一个方向规则）。 前面边走边解链，后面边走边让 DNA 回粘 —— 泡跟着酶一起往前挪。
  4. 终止 — 两种办法让酶掉下来 不依赖 ρ 的：RNA 自己折成富含 GC 的发夹，后面跟一串 U；发夹把酶卡住，A–U 配对最弱，一松就掉。 依赖 ρ 的：ρ 是一个解旋酶，沿 RNA 追上聚合酶，把 RNA 从模板上拽下来。
  5. 真核 — 三套聚合酶各管一摊 Pol Ⅰ → rRNA（在核仁）· Pol Ⅱ → mRNA（最常考）· Pol Ⅲ → tRNA 和 5S rRNA。 ⚠ 记法「一老二新三转运」。鹅膏蕈碱专门毒 Pol Ⅱ。
  6. 加工 — 真核 mRNA 要做三件事 5′ 加帽（m⁷G，防外切酶降解 ＋ 帮核糖体识别）· 3′ 加 polyA 尾（不依赖模板，维持稳定）· 剪接（剪接体切掉内含子、拼上外显子）。 ⚠ 可变剪接让一个基因能出好几种蛋白 —— 这是真核复杂性的来源之一。
  7. 原核对真核，一句话对比 原核：转录和翻译同时进行（没有核膜隔开），mRNA 不加工、多顺反子。 真核：先在核里转录和加工，再运出去翻译，单顺反子。

Say this

Transcription needs no primer, which is the main difference from replication. It reads the template strand (also called antisense strand, non-coding strand), so the RNA matches the coding strand (also called sense strand, non-template strand) with U in place of T. In bacteria the holoenzyme is α₂ββ′σ, and the sigma subunit does not catalyse anything — its only job is to recognise the promoter, and it falls off once initiation has happened. The process has three stages. In initiation, sigma finds the promoter — in bacteria the −10 box (also called Pribnow box), TATAAT, and the −35 box — and the polymerase opens a short bubble of DNA. In elongation, the core enzyme reads the template 3′ to 5′, so the RNA grows 5′ to 3′, unwinding the DNA in front and letting it close again behind. Termination is either rho-independent — the RNA folds into a GC-rich hairpin followed by a run of U, and the weak A–U pairs let go — or rho-dependent, where the rho helicase chases the polymerase and pulls the RNA off. Eukaryotes have three polymerases: I makes rRNA, II makes mRNA, and III makes tRNA and 5S. Eukaryotic mRNA is then processed in three ways: a 5′ cap, a poly-A tail, and splicing out the introns (also called intervening sequence). And in bacteria, because there is no nucleus, transcription and translation happen at the same time.

约 75 秒 · 「不需要引物」和「σ 认门牌然后脱落」是两个抓手；起始 → 延伸 → 终止三段是 09-29 补的

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Martínková

① RNA 出错不遗传，所以 RNA 聚合酶不需要像 DNA 聚合酶那么准 fidelity

RNA polymerase has lower fidelity than DNA polymerase because an error in RNA affects only one short-lived copy and is not inherited.

跟复制对比。

② 利福平抑制细菌 RNA 聚合酶，不抑制人的，用来治结核 rifampicin

Because the bacterial RNA polymerase differs from ours, rifampicin inhibits only the bacterial enzyme, which makes it a main drug against tuberculosis.

选择性。

③ α-鹅膏蕈碱是毒鹅膏菌的毒素，抑制 RNA 聚合酶 Ⅱ，先损伤肝 α-amanitin

α-Amanitin, the toxin of the death cap mushroom, Amanita phalloides, blocks our own polymerase II; mRNA synthesis stops and the liver is damaged first. Actinomycin D, an antibiotic, instead binds DNA and blocks all transcription.

α-鹅膏蕈碱只抑制 Pol Ⅱ。

④ 真核的 RNA 聚合酶 Ⅱ 需要 TFIID 等通用转录因子识别 TATA 框 general transcription factors

Unlike bacterial sigma, polymerase II needs general transcription factors, starting with TFIID binding the TATA box (also called Goldberg–Hogness box), and enhancers far away increase transcription through DNA looping.

接「基因调控」。

⑤ 书外 · general：Roberts 1969 找到 ρ 因子，Shine–Dalgarno 1974，Pribnow 1975 找到 TATAAT，足迹法 1978，SV40 增强子 1981 how the sites were found

These sites were pinned down only later, and that history is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge. Roberts found rho in 1969 as the factor needed for termination in the test tube.

Shine and Dalgarno noticed in 1974 that the 3′ end of 16S rRNA is complementary to sequences before start codons. Pribnow found TATAAT in 1975 in the protected region of many promoters.

DNase footprinting, from 1978, shows where a protein sits on DNA. Enhancers were found in the SV40 virus in 1981.

足迹法：蛋白坐着的那段 DNA 不被 DNase 切，胶上留下一块空白。

⑥ 组蛋白乙酰化使染色质松开，基因才能转录 histone acetylation

The DNA also has to be accessible: histone acetyltransferases acetylate histone lysines, the chromatin opens, and transcription can start.

接表观遗传。

⑦ 古菌的 RNA 聚合酶和真核的 Pol Ⅱ 相似 archaea

Bacteria have one RNA polymerase, but the polymerase of archaea resembles eukaryotic polymerase II, which is evidence that eukaryotes are closer to archaea.

三域进化的证据。

⑧ 我的 pET 载体由 T7 噬菌体的 RNA 聚合酶转录 T7 RNA polymerase

In my expression system the gene is transcribed by T7 RNA polymerase from phage T7, a single-subunit enzyme that recognises only the T7 promoter and is very fast.

接我自己的表达系统。

⑨ 细菌不能剪接，所以表达真核基因要用逆转录酶做成 cDNA reverse transcriptase · cDNA

Bacteria cannot remove introns, so to express a eukaryotic gene I use reverse transcriptase to copy mRNA into cDNA, which has no introns.

遗传信息也能从 RNA 流回 DNA。

⑩ 转录量用 RT-qPCR 或 RNA-seq 测 RT-qPCR · RNA-seq

The same enzyme is used to measure transcription: RNA is reverse-transcribed and the cDNA is counted by RT-qPCR (also called real-time PCR (≠ RT-PCR)), or all transcripts are sequenced by RNA-seq.

「怎么测」。

⑪ 苯并芘和二噁英结合 AhR，诱导 P450 基因的转录 ⭐ Hodek AhR · P450 induction

Foreign compounds can change transcription too: benzo[a]pyrene and dioxin bind the aryl hydrocarbon receptor, AhR, which induces the gene for cytochrome P450 1A1.

Hodek 定量的正是 P450 1A1。

⑫ 细菌二组分系统里，磷酸化的响应调节蛋白就是转录因子 ⭐ Martínková response regulator

In bacteria, a two-component system ends in transcription: the phosphorylated response regulator, for example OmpR, binds DNA and switches genes on or off.

通往 Martínková。

⑬ 书外：RNA 世界假说——最早的生命可能只用 RNA RNA world

Because RNA can both store information and catalyse reactions, as ribozymes and the ribosome show, early life may have used RNA alone, with DNA and proteins added later. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Why does rifampicin not harm us?" — Our RNA polymerases are different. · 聚合酶不同。
  • "Is most cellular RNA mRNA?" — No, most is ribosomal RNA. · 大部分是 rRNA。
  • "Why use cDNA for expression in E. coli?" — Bacteria cannot splice out introns（= intron = intervening sequence）. · 细菌不会剪接。

定义

  • general transcription factors — proteins such as TFIID that polymerase II needs at every promoter · 通用转录因子
  • TATA box — a promoter sequence about 25 bases upstream of the start in many eukaryotic genes · TATA 框（= TATA box = Goldberg–Hogness box）
  • enhancer — a DNA site, often far from the gene, that increases transcription · 增强子
  • histone acetyltransferase — an enzyme that adds acetyl groups to histone lysines · 组蛋白乙酰转移酶
  • DNase footprinting — finding a protein's binding site as the DNA stretch it protects from DNase · DNase 足迹法
  • archaea — single-celled organisms without a nucleus, distinct from bacteria · 古菌
  • cDNA — DNA copied from mRNA, without introns · 互补 DNA
  • RT-qPCR（= qPCR = real-time PCR (≠ RT-PCR)） — reverse transcription followed by quantitative PCR · 逆转录定量 PCR
  • AhR — aryl hydrocarbon receptor, a transcription factor activated by aromatic pollutants · 芳烃受体
  • ribozyme — an RNA molecule with catalytic activity · 核酶

Translation

横向卡 x-trans · 每个残基烧四个高能键，贵得有道理

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有 mRNA 就跑 遗传密码的四个性质：简并性（一个氨基酸可以有好几个密码子，让很多突变不致命）· 通用性（从病毒到人共用一套）· 摆动性 · 无标点、不重叠。 起始 AUG（甲硫氨酸），终止 UAA · UAG · UGA（不编码任何氨基酸）。
  2. 活化 — 先把氨基酸装到 tRNA 上 amino acid ＋ tRNA ＋ ATP →[aminoacyl-tRNA synthetase] aminoacyl-tRNA ＋ AMP ＋ PPᵢ ⚠ ATP 断成 AMP，所以这一步就花掉 2 个高能键。 ⚠ 这个酶是密码正确性的真正把关人：它必须同时认对氨基酸和tRNA， 认错了后面核糖体发现不了。
  3. 起始 — 小亚基先找到起点 原核靠 mRNA 上的 Shine-Dalgarno 序列跟 16S rRNA 配对定位； 真核靠 5′ 帽，小亚基从帽子开始扫描到第一个 AUG。 然后大亚基合上，起始 tRNA 坐进 P 位。
  4. 延伸 — 三个动作循环 进位：EF-Tu 护送氨酰-tRNA 进 A 位，花 1 GTP → 成肽：肽酰转移酶把 P 位的肽链接到 A 位的氨基酸上（⚠ 这个活性在 rRNA 上，核糖体是核酶，不花 GTP）→ 转位：EF-G 推着核糖体沿 mRNA 走一个密码子，再花 1 GTP。
  5. 摆动 — 为什么 tRNA 不用 61 种 tRNA 反密码子的第 1 位跟密码子的第 3 位配对时可以松一点： C 只配 G · A 只配 U · U 配 A 或 G · G 配 C 或 U · 次黄嘌呤 I 能配 A、C、U。 ⚠ 所以一个 tRNA 能认好几个密码子，细胞不需要 61 种。
  6. 终止 — 没有 tRNA 认终止密码子（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon） 释放因子（蛋白质，不是 tRNA）进 A 位，让肽酰转移酶把肽链水解下来。
  7. 能量账 — 每个残基 4 个高能键 活化 2（ATP→AMP）＋ 进位 1 GTP ＋ 转位 1 GTP ＝ 4。 ⚠ 为什么这么贵：翻译是全细胞最耗能的过程之一，代价买的是精确 —— 一个错的氨基酸可能毁掉整条蛋白。

Say this

Translation starts at AUG and stops at UAA, UAG or UGA, and the code is degenerate, universal and read with wobble. Wobble means the pairing at the third position of the codon is loose: the first base of the anticodon can pair with more than one base — G pairs with C or U, and inosine pairs with A, C or U — so one tRNA reads several codons, and the cell needs far fewer than 61 tRNAs. The amino acid is first attached to its tRNA by an aminoacyl-tRNA synthetase, and because the ATP goes to AMP that costs two high-energy bonds. Elongation is then three steps repeated: EF-Tu (also called eEF1A in eukaryotes) brings the tRNA into the A site using one GTP, peptidyl transferase makes the bond — and that activity is in the rRNA, so the ribosome is a ribozyme — and EF-G (also called eEF2 in eukaryotes) translocates using another GTP. So each residue costs four high-energy phosphate bonds, and what that buys is accuracy. Termination is done by release factors, which are proteins, not tRNAs.

约 60 秒 · 「4 个高能键」和「核糖体是核酶」是两个必说；摆动那两句是 09-29 补的

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Lipovová、Šulc

① 细菌核糖体是 70S，人的是 80S，所以抗生素能只抑制细菌 70S vs 80S

Bacterial ribosomes are 70S and ours are 80S, and this difference lets many antibiotics block bacterial translation without blocking ours.

选择性毒性。

② 链霉素和四环素作用于 30S 小亚基，氯霉素和红霉素作用于 50S 大亚基 streptomycin · tetracycline · chloramphenicol · erythromycin

Streptomycin causes misreading and tetracycline blocks tRNA entry on the 30S subunit; chloramphenicol blocks peptidyl transferase and erythromycin blocks the exit tunnel on the 50S subunit.

两个小亚基，两个大亚基。

③ 线粒体核糖体像细菌的，所以氯霉素抑制骨髓，氨基糖苷类损伤听力 mitochondrial ribosomes

Mitochondria still have bacteria-like ribosomes from their bacterial origin, which explains side effects such as bone-marrow suppression by chloramphenicol and hearing loss from aminoglycosides.

内共生的证据。

④ 白喉毒素使 eEF2 失活，蓖麻毒素破坏 28S rRNA diphtheria toxin · ricin

Diphtheria toxin and ricin target our translation instead: diphtheria toxin inactivates the translocation factor eEF2, and ricin, from castor beans, removes an adenine from the 28S rRNA of the large subunit.

反过来打真核。

⑤ 嘌呤霉素结构像氨酰 tRNA 的末端，使肽链提前释放；实验室用它筛选哺乳动物细胞 puromycin

Puromycin resembles the end of an aminoacyl-tRNA, so it is added to the growing chain and releases it early; in the lab the puromycin resistance gene is a selection marker for mammalian cells.

接「筛选标记」。

⑥ 细菌以甲酰甲硫氨酸起始，人的免疫系统据此识别细菌 fMet

Another bacterial difference: translation starts with N-formylmethionine, and our neutrophils recognise formylated peptides as a sign of bacteria.

又一处原核真核差别。

⑦ 一条 mRNA 上同时有很多核糖体，分子伴侣帮助新生肽链折叠 polysome · chaperones

Many ribosomes read one mRNA at the same time as a polysome, and chaperones (also called heat shock protein (many of them)) such as DnaK and GroEL in bacteria help the new chain fold.

接「蛋白折叠」。

⑧ 有信号肽的蛋白由 SRP 带到内质网上翻译 signal peptide · SRP

A signal peptide (also called signal sequence), a hydrophobic stretch at the N-terminus, decides where translation happens: SRP binds it and brings the ribosome to the ER membrane, so secreted proteins are made there.

接「翻译后修饰」。

⑨ Nirenberg 1961 年用 poly-U 得到只含苯丙氨酸的肽，UUU 编码苯丙氨酸 poly-U · Nirenberg

The code was first cracked by Nirenberg and Matthaei in 1961: synthetic poly-U RNA added to a cell-free (also called cell-free protein synthesis, CFPS, in vitro transcription–translation) E. coli extract gave polyphenylalanine, so UUU codes for phenylalanine.

无细胞（= cell-free protein synthesis = CFPS = in vitro transcription–translation）体系，今天也用来表达蛋白。

⑩ 大肠杆菌缺少某些稀有密码子的 tRNA，用 Rosetta 菌株或密码子优化 ⭐ Lipovová codon bias · Rosetta

Organisms prefer different codons, so a human gene in E. coli may stall at rare codons such as AGG and AGA for arginine; I use Rosetta strains, which supply those tRNAs, or a codon-optimised gene.

接表达菌株。

⑪ 把琥珀终止密码子 UAG 分配给改造的 tRNA，就能插入非天然氨基酸 ⭐ Šulc amber suppression · ncAA

The amber stop codon (also called termination codon, nonsense codon) can be reassigned: an engineered tRNA and synthetase pair reads the amber codon UAG and inserts a non-canonical amino acid, such as a photo-cross-linker for mass spectrometry.

通往 Šulc 的交联质谱。

⑫ 书外：mRNA 疫苗用 N1-甲基假尿苷代替尿苷 N1-methylpseudouridine

mRNA vaccines replace uridine with N1-methylpseudouridine, which lowers the innate immune response and increases translation. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Why do these antibiotics spare us?" — Our cytosolic ribosomes are 80S. · 核糖体不同。
  • "Then why side effects?" — Mitochondrial ribosomes resemble bacterial ones. · 线粒体核糖体像细菌。
  • "Is peptidyl transferase a protein?" — No, it is the rRNA. · 是 rRNA。

定义

  • S (Svedberg unit) — a measure of how fast a particle sediments in a centrifuge · 沉降系数
  • aminoglycoside — the antibiotic class that includes streptomycin and kanamycin · 氨基糖苷类
  • eEF2 — the eukaryotic elongation factor that moves the ribosome along the mRNA · 真核延伸因子 2
  • polysome — one mRNA with many ribosomes · 多聚核糖体
  • chaperone — a protein that helps other proteins fold · 分子伴侣（= chaperone = heat shock protein (many of them)）
  • SRP — signal recognition particle, which brings ribosomes to the ER · 信号识别颗粒
  • codon bias — an organism's preference among synonymous codons · 密码子偏好
  • non-canonical amino acid — an amino acid outside the standard twenty · 非天然氨基酸

The lac operon

卡 12

Must say

Two switches, and both must be right. The repressor is the negative one, cAMP–CAP (also called CRP, catabolite activator protein, cAMP receptor protein) is the positive one.

Numbers

Three states: no lactose → off; lactose and glucose → barely on, because glucose keeps cAMP low; lactose and no glucose → fully on.

Hook

Glucose lowers cAMP, so the cell will not bother with lactose while there is glucose. That is catabolite repression.

Say this

The lac operon has two switches, and both have to be right. The repressor is the negative one: without lactose it sits on the operator and blocks the polymerase. cAMP with CAP is the positive one: it recruits the polymerase, but only when cAMP is high. So there are three states. With no lactose, the repressor is bound and the operon is off. With lactose and glucose, the repressor comes off, but glucose keeps cAMP low, so there is only weak basal transcription. With lactose and no glucose, the repressor is off and cAMP is high, so the operon is fully on. ⚠ That last effect of glucose is called catabolite repression — the cell will not bother with lactose while there is glucose to eat.

约 40 秒 · 「两个开关都要对」是这题的形状

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Martínková、Lipovová

① 分解代谢的 lac 操纵子由底物诱导，合成代谢的 trp 操纵子由产物阻遏 inducible vs repressible

The lac operon is inducible: its substrate switches it on; the trp operon, which makes tryptophan, is repressible: its product switches it off.

lac 对 trp。

② trp 阻遏蛋白只有结合色氨酸后才能结合操纵基因 corepressor

The trp repressor binds the operator only with tryptophan bound, so tryptophan acts as a corepressor and a high level stops its own synthesis.

色氨酸 ＝ 辅阻遏物。

③ trp 操纵子还有衰减作用：核糖体翻译前导序列的速度决定转录是否继续 attenuation

The trp operon has a second control, attenuation: when tryptophan is plentiful, ribosomes translate the leader peptide quickly and a terminator hairpin forms, which is possible only because bacteria translate while they transcribe.

接「转录」和「翻译」。

④ 核糖开关是 mRNA 里直接结合代谢物（比如 S-腺苷甲硫氨酸）的区域，不需要蛋白 riboswitch

A riboswitch, a region of an mRNA, regulates its own gene without any protein: when it binds a metabolite such as S-adenosylmethionine, it changes structure and stops transcription or hides the ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence).

不需要蛋白的调控。

⑤ 书外 · general：Monod 先看到二次生长——葡萄糖加乳糖时细菌分两段生长 diauxic growth · Monod

Jacques Monod first saw diauxic growth: on glucose plus lactose, bacteria use the glucose first, pause, and only then switch to lactose. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

两段生长 ＝ 分解代谢物阻遏。

⑥ 书外 · general：Jacob 和 Monod 1961 年用组成型突变株和部分二倍体——只在顺式起作用的是 DNA 位点（操纵基因），能反式起作用的是可扩散的蛋白（LacI 阻遏蛋白）；1965 年诺贝尔奖 Jacob · Monod · cis vs trans

That observation led to the operon model of Jacob and Monod in 1961. They used constitutive mutants, which made the enzymes all the time, and partial diploids carrying a second lac region.

A mutation acting only in cis marked a DNA site, the operator; one acting in trans marked a diffusible protein, the LacI repressor. They shared the 1965 Nobel Prize with Lwoff. That is also general knowledge.

顺式 ＝ 只管同一条 DNA 上的基因；反式 ＝ 能跑到另一条 DNA 上去管。

⑦ 细菌用二组分系统感受外界：感受激酶把磷酸传给响应调节蛋白 ⭐ Martínková two-component systems

Operons also respond to outside signals through two-component systems: a membrane sensor kinase such as EnvZ passes a phosphate to a response regulator such as OmpR, which controls genes.

Martínková 的血红素组氨酸激酶也属于这一类。

⑧ 真核没有操纵子，每个基因由转录因子、增强子和染色质状态共同控制 eukaryotic regulation

Eukaryotes mostly lack operons: each gene has its own promoter, controlled by combinations of transcription factors, enhancers, and histone acetylation and DNA methylation.

组合控制。

⑨ BL21(DE3) 里 T7 RNA 聚合酶基因受 lac 控制，所以用 IPTG 诱导 ⭐ Lipovová IPTG · DE3

I use the lac switch every week: in BL21(DE3) the T7 RNA polymerase gene is under a lac-controlled promoter, so IPTG, which binds LacI but is not metabolised, induces expression.

接我自己的表达。

⑩ 加葡萄糖降低本底表达；自诱导培养基先用完葡萄糖再由乳糖诱导 catabolite repression in the lab

Catabolite repression is used in the lab too: glucose in the medium lowers leaky expression, and auto-induction media contain glucose and lactose, so expression starts by itself when the glucose is used up.

书上的原理，台上的用法。

⑪ 插入片段破坏 lacZ，菌落在 X-gal 上是白色 blue–white screening

The lacZ gene also gives blue–white screening: an insert in the cloning site disrupts lacZ, so the colony cannot cut X-gal and stays white instead of blue.

克隆筛选。

⑫ 书外：用 lac 阻遏蛋白和其他阻遏蛋白构建人工基因开关 genetic toggle switch

In synthetic biology the lac repressor and other repressors are combined into artificial circuits such as the genetic toggle switch. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Does lactose itself bind the repressor?" — No, allolactose made from it does. · 是别乳糖。
  • "Is CAP（= CAP = CRP = catabolite activator protein = cAMP receptor protein） an activator or a repressor?" — An activator. · 激活物。
  • "Why is IPTG better than lactose?" — It is not metabolised. · 不被代谢。

定义

  • corepressor — a small molecule that must bind a repressor to activate it · 辅阻遏物
  • attenuation — early termination of transcription controlled by translation of a leader · 衰减作用
  • riboswitch — an mRNA region that binds a metabolite and controls its own gene · 核糖开关
  • cis-acting — affecting only genes on the same DNA molecule, as a DNA site does · 顺式作用
  • trans-acting — affecting genes on any DNA molecule, as a diffusible protein does · 反式作用
  • partial diploid — a bacterium carrying a second copy of a region, usually on a plasmid · 部分二倍体
  • diauxic growth — two growth phases when bacteria use one sugar after another · 二次生长
  • sensor kinase — a membrane histidine kinase that detects a signal · 感受激酶
  • leaky expression — expression without inducer · 本底表达
  • X-gal — a colourless compound that turns blue when cut by β-galactosidase · X-gal

Cloning, PCR & sequencing

卡 pe-clone · taq · 你自己天天做的那一套

  1. 为什么要克隆 — 要拿到很多份同一个基因 把目的基因放进一个能在细菌里自主复制的载体，细菌一分裂就替你复制一遍。
  2. 质粒三件套 Ori（复制起点，能自主复制）· 选择标记（抗性基因，在平板上毒死没转进去的）· MCS（多克隆位点，一小段集中了很多独特酶切位点）。
  3. 把基因放进去 — 两条路 传统：限制性内切酶切出黏末端，连接酶接上。 无切法：restriction-free cloning / Gibson —— 用带同源臂的引物把插入片段变成一条大引物， ⚠ 不需要合适的酶切位点，这是它最大的好处。
  4. 送进细胞 ＋ 挑出来 转化（化学感受态 ＋ 热激）或电穿孔。 铺抗生素平板 → 只有带质粒的长得出来 → 挑单克隆 → 测序确认。
  5. PCR — 三步一轮，指数放大 变性 → 退火 → 延伸。⚠ Taq 没有 3′→5′ 校对，Kapa 和 Pfu 有 —— 要克隆、要表达的，必须用有校对的那种，不然错配会被一路放大。
  6. Sanger 测序 — 靠一个缺掉的羟基 反应里掺一点 ddNTP：它的糖环 3′ 位是 −H 而不是 −OH。 一旦被掺进去，下一个磷酸二酯键再也建不起来，链当场停住。 不同长度的片段按大小分开，就读出了序列。

Say this

A cloning vector needs three things: an origin of replication so it copies itself in the host, a selectable marker so that only the cells that took it up survive, and a multiple cloning site. The insert can be put in with restriction enzymes and ligase, or by restriction-free cloning, where the insert itself is used as a large primer, which avoids needing suitable restriction sites at all. PCR is denaturation, annealing and extension, and Taq has no 3′→5′ proofreading while Kapa and Pfu do, so anything that will be cloned should be amplified with a proofreading polymerase. Sanger sequencing works by chain termination with ddNTPs: the 3′ position is a hydrogen instead of a hydroxyl, so no further phosphodiester bond can form.

约 45 秒 · 这一段是你的主场，说得比别人细是加分

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hudeček、Lipovová、Hodek

① 质粒是细菌染色体以外的小环状 DNA，能自己复制 plasmid

A plasmid is a small circular DNA molecule in a bacterium, separate from the chromosome; it replicates on its own and often carries genes such as antibiotic resistance.

plasmid ＝ 染色体外的小环 DNA。

② 书外 · general：Lederberg 1952 年起了 plasmid 这个名字；Cohen、Chang、Boyer、Helling 1973 年把外源 DNA 接进 pSC101 discovery

Joshua Lederberg coined the word plasmid in 1952. R factors, plasmids carrying several antibiotic resistances, were known to move between bacteria by conjugation. In 1973 Cohen, Chang, Boyer and Helling cut pSC101 with EcoRI, joined foreign DNA into it, transformed E. coli, and the inserted gene was expressed. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

⚠ 这是 Herbert Boyer，不是 ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase）那个 Paul Boyer。

③ 克隆载体用来复制和保存 DNA：高拷贝复制起点、选择标记、多克隆位点，不需要强启动子 cloning vector · pUC19

A cloning vector is a plasmid made to copy and store DNA; it needs a usually high-copy origin of replication, a selection marker such as ampicillin resistance, and a multiple cloning site. pUC19 is the standard example, and its lacZα gene gives blue–white screening.

目的：多拷贝、存起来。不需要表达。

④ 表达载体用来生产蛋白：在克隆载体基础上加强启动子、核糖体结合位点、起始密码子、标签和终止子 expression vector · pET

An expression vector is a plasmid made to produce the protein. On top of the cloning elements it adds a strong, usually inducible promoter, a ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence), a start codon, often a His-tag, and a transcription terminator. pET vectors are the usual example, and they are lower copy than cloning vectors.

克隆载体是仓库，表达载体是工厂。

⑤ 启动子是 RNA 聚合酶结合、开始转录的 DNA 序列；强度和紧密性是两个不同性质 promoter · strength vs tightness

A promoter is the DNA sequence where RNA polymerase binds and starts transcription. Strength is how much it expresses after induction, and tightness is how little it leaks before induction; the two usually conflict.

强 ≠ 紧。毒性蛋白要紧的。

⑥ lac 启动子用 IPTG 诱导，强度中等，漏得最多；tac 和 trc 是 trp 与 lac 的杂合，更强，也用 IPTG lac · tac · trc

The first promoters to choose from: the lac promoter is induced by IPTG, has moderate strength and is the leakiest. tac and trc, hybrids of the trp and lac promoters, are stronger than lac and are also induced by IPTG.

lac 中等最漏；tac/trc 比 lac 强。

⑦ T7 启动子最强，但只有 T7 RNA 聚合酶认它，所以宿主要用 BL21(DE3) T7 · BL21(DE3)

The T7 promoter, from phage T7, is the strongest, but only T7 RNA polymerase reads it. So the host must be a strain such as BL21(DE3), which carries the T7 polymerase gene under an IPTG-inducible lacUV5 promoter.

两级：IPTG → 造 T7 聚合酶 → 转录我的基因。

⑧ araBAD 启动子用阿拉伯糖诱导，最紧，还能按剂量调；组成型启动子一直开 araBAD · constitutive

The opposite choice is the araBAD promoter: it is induced by arabinose, it is the tightest, and the level can be tuned by the arabinose dose. A constitutive promoter, by contrast, is always on and cannot be switched.

T7 最强，araBAD 最紧，lac 最漏。

⑨ 真核宿主要真核启动子：哺乳动物细胞用 CMV，酵母用 GAL1（半乳糖），毕赤酵母用 AOX1（甲醇） CMV · GAL1 · AOX1

Other hosts need their own promoters. CMV, from cytomegalovirus, gives strong constitutive expression in mammalian cells. GAL1 is induced by galactose in yeast, and AOX1 by methanol in Pichia pastoris.

GAL1 就是我酵母展示里用的开关。

⑩ 元件只是一段序列，意义来自认它的分子：启动子由 RNA 聚合酶认，操纵基因由阻遏蛋白认 genetic elements · promoter · operator

A genetic element is just a stretch of sequence; its meaning comes from what recognises it. The promoter is recognised by RNA polymerase, through the σ factor in bacteria and through TBP and other transcription factors in eukaryotes. The operator is recognised by a repressor, such as LacI.

问「这个元件是什么」，就答「谁认它」。

⑪ 增强子由激活蛋白认，可以离基因很远，正反方向都起作用 enhancer

An enhancer is recognised by activator proteins. Unlike a promoter, it works at a distance from the gene and in either orientation.

远、方向无所谓。

⑫ 两个例外在 RNA 上起作用：终止子是 RNA 发夹加一串 U；SD 序列和 16S rRNA 配对 terminator · Shine–Dalgarno

Two elements are the exceptions, because they act as RNA, not as DNA. The terminator works as an RNA hairpin followed by a run of U; rho-dependent terminators use the rho protein. The ribosome binding site, or Shine–Dalgarno sequence, pairs with 16S rRNA.

先转录出来，才起作用。

⑬ 复制起点由 DnaA 认，多克隆位点由限制酶认，抗性基因是一整个基因，比如编码 β-内酰胺酶 origin · MCS · resistance gene

The origin of the E. coli chromosome, oriC, is recognised by the initiator protein DnaA. The multiple cloning site is recognised by restriction enzymes. The resistance gene is not a site but a whole gene, for example one encoding β-lactamase, which destroys ampicillin.

抗性基因要被表达成蛋白才有用。

⑭ 书外 · general：「转化」这个词来自 Griffith 1928 年的肺炎球菌实验；书上年表 1944 年 Avery 证明是 DNA transformation · Griffith · Avery

The finished vector enters bacteria by transformation. The word comes from Griffith's 1928 experiment: live harmless R pneumococci plus heat-killed virulent S pneumococci killed mice, so something from the dead cells had transformed R. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge. My textbook's timeline on p009 records Avery, 1944: genetic information is transferred by DNA.

S 有荚膜，致死；R 没有。

⑮ 吸光度和浓度成正比：A ＝ ε·c·l ⭐ Hudeček Beer–Lambert law

To check the DNA and protein I measure absorbance, which rests on the Beer–Lambert (also called Beer–Lambert law, Beer's law) law, A = ε·c·l: absorbance equals the extinction coefficient times concentration times path length. It fails at high concentration.

Hudeček：说方法先说测的是什么物理量。

⑯ NanoDrop 测 260 和 280 nm：A260/A280 约 1.8 是干净 DNA，约 2.0 是干净 RNA；A260/A230 低说明盐或苯酚残留 A260 · A280 · purity ratios

NanoDrop, a small-volume spectrophotometer, reads 260 nm, where the aromatic rings of the bases absorb, and 280 nm, where tryptophan and tyrosine absorb. A260/A280 near 1.8 means clean DNA and near 2.0 clean RNA. A low A260/A230 means guanidine salts or phenol were carried over.

吸光度分不清 DNA 和 RNA，所以要报比值。

⑰ OD600 测的是细胞散射光，也就是浊度，不是吸收；数值随仪器不同 OD600 · turbidity

OD600, used to follow the culture before induction, is not absorbance in the Beer–Lambert sense. Cells scatter light out of the beam, so it measures turbidity. 600 nm is chosen because medium and cells absorb little there, and the value depends on the instrument.

散射，不是吸收。

⑱ His 标签蛋白用 Ni-NTA 纯化，咪唑竞争把它洗脱下来 ⭐ Lipovová His-tag · IMAC · Ni-NTA

IMAC, immobilised metal affinity chromatography, purifies His-tagged proteins: the histidine side chains coordinate nickel held by Ni-NTA. Free imidazole competes for the nickel and elutes the protein. Binding does not need a folded protein, so it also works on inclusion bodies dissolved in urea.

咪唑就是组氨酸的侧链本身。

⑲ DNA 靠琼脂糖凝胶按大小分开；蛋白要加 SDS 才能按大小分，还原剂打开二硫键 agarose gel · SDS-PAGE

In an agarose gel DNA separates by size, because every nucleotide carries one phosphate. Proteins need SDS-PAGE: SDS unfolds the chain and gives a uniform negative charge per mass, so they separate by size. A reducing agent such as DTT breaks disulfide bonds.

DNA 天生电荷均匀，蛋白要 SDS 给。

⑳ Western blot：SDS-PAGE 后转到膜上，封闭，再用一抗和带标记的二抗检测 Western blot

A Western blot (also called immunoblot) starts from SDS-PAGE: the bands are transferred to a nitrocellulose or PVDF membrane, blocked, and probed with a primary antibody and a labelled secondary antibody. The transfer is needed because antibodies cannot enter the gel pores.

凝胶管分开，抗体管认出。

㉑ ELISA 在板上用抗体测抗原：夹心法用两个抗体认两个表位，酶把底物变色 ⭐ Hodek ELISA · IgY

ELISA, enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay, measures an antigen on a plate. In a sandwich ELISA a capture and a detection antibody bind two different epitopes, and an enzyme on the detection antibody turns a substrate into colour. Hodek uses chicken IgY from egg yolk as such probes.

抗体管特异性，酶管信号。

㉒ 酵母展示：蛋白通过 Aga2 融合挂在酵母表面，用 FACS 分选，直接在细胞上测亲和力 yeast display · FACS

Yeast display, my own method, shows the protein on the yeast surface as a fusion with Aga2, switched on by GAL1. Cells are sorted by FACS, and affinity is measured on the cells by titrating labelled antigen.

一个细胞 ＝ 一个变体。

陷阱问法

  • "Does a T7 promoter work in any E. coli?" — No, only in a host that makes T7 RNA polymerase, such as BL21(DE3). · 要有 T7 聚合酶。
  • "Is OD600 an absorbance?" — No, it is light scattered by cells. · 是散射。
  • "Can A260 tell DNA from RNA?" — No, both absorb there; the ratios help, a dye assay is specific. · 分不清。
  • "Is a terminator recognised as DNA?" — No, it acts as RNA, like the Shine–Dalgarno（= Shine–Dalgarno sequence = ribosome binding site） sequence. · 在 RNA 上起作用。

定义

  • IPTG — a lactose analogue that binds LacI but is not metabolised · 异丙基硫代半乳糖苷
  • σ factor — the bacterial RNA polymerase subunit that recognises the promoter · σ 因子
  • operator — the DNA site bound by a repressor · 操纵基因
  • enhancer — a DNA site bound by activators, working at a distance and in either orientation · 增强子
  • Shine–Dalgarno sequence — the ribosome binding site that pairs with 16S rRNA · SD 序列
  • DnaA — the bacterial protein that starts replication at oriC · 起始蛋白
  • β-lactamase — the enzyme that destroys ampicillin · β-内酰胺酶
  • extinction coefficient — ε, how strongly a substance absorbs at one wavelength · 消光系数
  • turbidity — cloudiness caused by light scattering · 浊度
  • Ni-NTA — nickel held by nitrilotriacetic acid on a resin · 镍柱
  • epitope — the part of an antigen an antibody binds · 表位
  • FACS — fluorescence-activated cell sorting · 荧光激活细胞分选

From the main app

卡 14 · mb-int · mb-ko · mb-post · x-ph · 主 app 里有、这一页原来没有的五块

  1. pH — 一个事实，四种读法 pH = −log₁₀[H⁺]，一个单位就是十倍，刻度反着走 —— 越酸，pH 越小。 中性是 7，是因为 25 °C 时 K_w = [H⁺][OH⁻] = 10⁻¹⁴，两个离子成对生出来，各是 10⁻⁷ M。不是谁挑的中点。 真正干活的那个数是 pKa：pH 等于 pKa 时，那个基团正好半解离。 滴定曲线、缓冲液、等电点、酶的 pH 最适 —— 这四样是同一个事实的四种读法，剩下的只是代数。 缓冲液 ＝ 一个弱酸加它自己的共轭碱，管用范围 pKa ± 1；⚠ 范围由 pKa 定，容量由浓度定，这是两件事。 ⚠ 书从头到尾没有定义过 pH，也没有任何一节讲缓冲 —— 这两块被问出处就直说「通用知识」。 书真正印出来的是甘氨酸曲线那三个数 2,4 / 9,8 / 6,1（p023）、pI = (pK₁ + pK₂)/2（p024）、pH 最适那一节和那张表（p058 p116）。
  2. 送进去 — 四个词，两个问题 transformation · electroporation · transduction · transfection 说的是同一件事：把外源核酸送进一个活细胞。 差别只在两个问题上：进的是哪种细胞、谁把它带过膜。 transformation ＝ 细菌自己从溶液里吸裸 DNA，前提是它处在 competent 状态； transduction ＝ 病毒当车，细菌那边是噬菌体； transfection ＝ 送进真核细胞，定义就这么多，不暗示任何机制。 ⚠ electroporation 不在这张清单上 —— 它回答的是「你怎么做的」。所以电转进大肠杆菌仍然是一次 transformation。 落锤那两句：The method did not change. The destination did. ⚠ 而且命名有一半是约定：酵母是真核，按定义该叫转染，全世界却都说 yeast transformation。 ⚠ 最后一个陷阱：癌症生物学里 transformation 指正常细胞变成恶性的，而书里 transformation 唯一的生物学用法正是这个（p182）。
  3. 证明接触 — 八种方法，八种读出 两个分子有没有接触，显微镜分辨不出来。所以每一种方法都在接触的下游造一个信号，再拿那个信号当答案 —— 一个长出来的菌落、一条胶上的带、一个反射光的角度、一个新的发射波长。 三个问题就能安放任何一种方法：问的是哪一对 · 在哪儿做 · 读出是什么。 酵母单杂交问「哪个蛋白结合这段 DNA」，ChIP 正好反过来问「这个蛋白结合哪些 DNA」； 酵母双杂交把已知的那半挂在 BD 上当诱饵、把文库挂在 AD 上当猎物； SPR 在芯片上读折射率，CoIP 和 GST pull-down 读沉淀和胶，FRET 读 1–10 nm 的距离，RNAi 根本不是检测、是扰动。 ⚠ 每一种都有它显示不出来的东西，而这个盲点决定了一个阳性结果值多少钱。 考场上只给三样 ——方法、读出、盲点 —— 说完就停，别背清单。
  4. 弄坏一个基因 — 五个难处，五种技术 反向遗传学：手上有序列、不知道功能，就故意弄坏，从出的问题反推它原本在干什么。 后面每一项技术，都在解决上一项带来的一个难处 —— ① 同源重组太罕见（动物 10⁻²–10⁻⁵）而且看不出来 → 正-负双向选择：neo 放在靶基因里面，HSV-tk 放在同源臂外面，重组只拷两臂之间，于是带进 neo、丢下 tk； ② 皿里的细胞不是一只动物 → 做在 ES 细胞上（能进生殖系）； ③ 完全敲除会杀死胚胎 → Cre/LoxP 条件敲除，两个 LoxP 放哪里就删掉哪一段； ④ 植物没有 ES 细胞 → 借农杆菌的 T-DNA； ⑤ 以上全都在等细胞自己重组 → CRISPR/Cas9 不等了，直接切。 ⚠ 两句要背死：切是工具，修复才是机制；筛选只做富集，从不做证明（活下来的仍要 PCR、Southern 或测序验）。
  5. 转录之外 — 每多一道手续，就多一个可调的位置 基因自己的结构，决定了它必须经过哪些手续；每一道手续都是一个调控可以坐下来的位置。 真核基因是断裂的 → 必须剪接：内含子 5′ 起始 GT、3′ 末尾 AG，这就是 GT–AG 法则 （⚠ 那是写在 DNA 上的写法，到了 mRNA 前体上读作 GU–AG）。 必须剪接，剪接就可以变着花样来 ——选择性剪接让同一个基因做出不止一个蛋白。 转录出来的大部分根本不编码蛋白：< 200 nt 的是 siRNA 和 miRNA，> 200 nt 的是 lncRNA； Dicer 把长双链 RNA 切成 21–23 nt，RISC（一个 Argonaute 握着一条单链引导 RNA）去切靶 mRNA。 mRNA 做出来还要被读：扫描模式 —— 40S 在 5′ 端上车、往 3′ 滑、见 AUG 就和 60S 合成 80S，而停在哪由 AUG 周围的 Kozak 序列决定。 ⚠ 落点一句：转录只是其中一层。

Say this

Five things sit under all of this, and pH comes first. pH is the negative logarithm of the hydrogen ion concentration, and a group is half dissociated when the pH equals its pKa. The titration curve, the buffer, the isoelectric point and the pH optimum of an enzyme are four readings of that one fact. The rest is molecular biology. Transformation, transduction and transfection each say which cell took up foreign nucleic acid and what carried it across the membrane, while electroporation says only how it was done. A knockout then asks what a gene does by breaking it, and CRISPR changed the economics: twenty new bases instead of a new protein. An interaction method asks whether two molecules touch, and each one reports a signal downstream of the contact rather than the contact itself. And transcription is only one layer: splicing, non-coding RNA and the control of translation initiation all sit after it.

约 45 秒 · 先把 pH 立起来，再顺着「送进去 → 弄坏 → 证明接触 → 转录之后」走一遍

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Lipovová、Hudeček、Hodek

① pH 就是氢离子浓度换了个写法 pH

pH is the negative logarithm, to base ten, of the hydrogen ion concentration in moles per litre. One pH unit is a factor of ten, and the scale runs backwards, so more acid means a smaller pH. My textbook never defines pH, so I give that as general knowledge.

读的是位数，不是量。

② 中性是七，是水自己算出来的 ion product of water

Water dissociates into a hydrogen ion and a hydroxide, and at twenty-five degrees the product of those two concentrations is ten to the minus fourteen. In pure water both are ten to the minus seven molar, which gives pH seven. That is general knowledge rather than my textbook.

中性 ＝ 两者相等，不是「等于 7」。

③ pKa 是那个基团正好半解离时的 pH pKa

The pKa of a group is the pH at which that group is exactly half dissociated, and numerically it is the negative logarithm of its acid dissociation constant. Below its pKa the group keeps its proton, above it the group has let it go. A smaller pKa means a stronger acid.

pH 是溶液的性质，pKa 是一个基团的性质。

④ 一条式子读完整条滴定曲线 Henderson–Hasselbalch

The Henderson–Hasselbalch relation says pH equals the pKa plus the logarithm of the ratio of dissociated to undissociated form. The curve is flat near the pKa, because both forms are present and take up what is added, and steep away from it. My textbook does not print that relation, so I give it as general knowledge.

平的那一段就是缓冲本身。

⑤ 缓冲范围由 pKa 定，缓冲容量由浓度定 buffer · buffering range

A buffer is a weak acid together with its own conjugate base in comparable amounts, and it works over roughly one pH unit either side of its pKa. The range is set by the pKa; the capacity, meaning how much acid it can absorb, is set by the concentration. My textbook has no section on buffers, so that is general knowledge.

选哪一种 vs 配多浓，两个选择。

⑥ 书外 · general · 校准一台 pH 计 calibrating a pH meter
