A pH meter is calibrated against two or three standard buffers that bracket the sample, usually pH four, seven and ten, and the electrode is rinsed between them. Temperature matters, because both the electrode response and the standard's own pH change with it. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

两点定一条线：零点和斜率。

⑦ 书印的甘氨酸滴定曲线，三个数互相自洽 titration curve · isoelectric point

The textbook prints the titration curve of glycine with pKa one at two point four, pKa two at nine point eight, and the isoelectric point at six point one, and it gives pI as the average of the two flanking pKa values. It adds that a protein is least soluble at its isoelectric point, because no net charge leaves no repulsion between neighbours.

2,4 ＋ 9,8 除以二正好 6,1。

⑧ 酶的 pH 最适不一定等于它工作的那个 pH ⭐ Lipovová pH optimum

The textbook says enzyme activity against pH is a curve with a maximum, called the pH optimum, and puts the cause in the acid–base character of the active site. Its own table gives pepsin one point five to two point five and trypsin seven point five to eight point five, while papain has an optimum of eight and comes from a papaya.

用书的表打书的话。

⑨ 小幅改 pH 只换电荷，大幅改 pH 才是变性 charge change vs denaturation

A moderate change of pH only changes which groups carry a charge, and that is reversible. A large change denatures the protein, and the textbook lists strong acids and strong bases among its chemical denaturants, next to organic solvents, detergents, urea and guanidine.

先丢盐桥 → 展开 → 聚集。

⑩ 四个词说的是同一件事，分歧只在两个问题 transformation · transduction · transfection

All four name ways of getting foreign nucleic acid into a living cell, and the difference is two questions: which cell, and what carries the nucleic acid across the membrane. Electroporation is the one word that names a method rather than a destination. This is general molecular biology rather than my textbook.

三个去处，一个做法。

⑪ 两边都是负电，所以先加钙再热激 chemical competence

The DNA backbone and the bacterial surface are both negative and repel each other, so cells are held on ice in calcium chloride, whose divalent cation screens that repulsion. A brief heat shock, around forty-two degrees in ordinary practice, is thought to drive the DNA through. The detailed mechanism is still not settled.

「thought to」这三个字要说出口。

⑫ 普遍性转导错在装，局限性转导错在切 generalised vs specialised transduction

In generalised transduction a phage head takes a piece of host DNA by mistake while packaging, so any part of the genome can travel. In specialised transduction an integrated phage cuts off centre when it leaves, so only genes next to its integration site move; phage lambda carrying gal or bio is the standard case.

一个错在装头，一个错在切出来。

⑬ 接触本身看不见，所以每种方法都报告一个下游信号 interaction methods

Two molecules touching is not something a microscope resolves, so every one of these methods builds a signal downstream of the contact and reports that instead — a colony, a band, an angle, a wavelength. Each method also has a blind spot, and the blind spot decides what a positive result is worth.

主动说盲点，比被挖出来好。

⑭ 酵母双杂交把一个转录因子拆成两半，再让互作把它们拼回去 yeast two-hybrid · bait and prey

A eukaryotic activator needs a DNA-binding domain and an activation domain, and they need not be on the same chain. The known protein fused to the binding domain is the bait; each library insert fused to the activation domain is a prey. A hit means two proteins bind when placed together in a yeast nucleus, and nothing more.

诱饵 ＝ 已知的、固定的那一半。

⑮ SPR 读的是折射率，给的是一个量而不是一个「有」 ⭐ Hudeček surface plasmon resonance

Surface plasmon resonance immobilises the bait on a dextran layer over a metal film of nanometre thickness and flows a protein mixture past it. Anything that binds raises the refractive index at the surface and shifts the resonance angle, the angle at which reflected light loses intensity. It needs no label and no dye, and it returns a quantity rather than a yes.

先说物理量，再说结论。

⑯ 共沉淀和 ChIP 都是抗体在干活 ⭐ Hodek co-immunoprecipitation · ChIP

Co-immunoprecipitation couples an antibody against the target onto a solid matrix and spins everything hanging off it down, so presence in the pellet is the entire readout. Chromatin immunoprecipitation fixes complexes in living cells, cuts the chromatin, and uses an antibody to enrich the DNA fragments bound by one protein.

两个方法，一个工具。

⑰ 正-负双向选择：一个基因放里面，一个放外面 positive–negative selection

Homologous recombination is far too rare to find by looking, so selection is rebuilt around it. neo, giving resistance to G418, sits inside the target's critical exon, and HSV thymidine kinase, which turns ganciclovir into a poison, sits outside the matching arms. Recombination copies in only what lies between the arms.

neo 既是破坏，又是标记。

⑱ Cre 只认 LoxP，两个 LoxP 放哪里就删掉哪一段 Cre/LoxP · conditional knockout

Cre, a recombinase from a bacteriophage, recognises a thirty-four base pair site called LoxP and deletes everything between two LoxP sites pointing the same way. Putting the Cre gene under a tissue-specific or drug-inducible promoter decides when and where the target gene dies, so a gene whose complete knockout is lethal can still be studied.

同向才是删除，反向是倒位。

⑲ CRISPR 换靶只要改二十个核苷酸 sgRNA · PAM

An sgRNA is the crRNA and tracrRNA pair fused into one hairpin, carrying a twenty-nucleotide guide complementary to the target, and Cas9 cuts only where a PAM, usually NGG, follows the target. Changing those twenty bases is the whole act of retargeting, and the Cas9 protein is never redesigned.

改的是经济账，不是生物学。

⑳ 干扰毁的是信使，敲除拿走的是基因 Dicer · RISC · knockdown vs knockout

Dicer, an RNase III enzyme, cuts long double-stranded RNA into short interfering RNAs of twenty-one to twenty-three nucleotides, and RISC, an Argonaute protein holding one guide strand, cleaves the matching message. The gene stays in the genome, so interference gives a knockdown rather than a knockout.

敲低是「少一点」，敲除是「没有」。

陷阱问法

  • "What is pH?" — The negative logarithm to base ten of the hydrogen ion concentration; one unit is a factor of ten. My textbook never defines it. · 主动说通用知识。
  • "What is a buffer?" — A weak acid with its own conjugate base, working about one pH unit either side of its pKa. · 别硬编书上的缓冲系统。
  • "Is the buffering range the same as the buffer capacity?" — No: the range is set by the pKa, the capacity by the concentration. · 位置 vs 容量。
  • "Is putting a plasmid into yeast a transfection?" — Strictly yes, but the field calls it yeast transformation; the naming is partly convention. · 承认分类的边界。
  • "Does a co-immunoprecipitation show that the two proteins touch?" — No, only that they are in the same pelleted complex. · 说 same complex，别说 directly。
  • "Is RNA interference a knockout?" — No, a knockdown: the message is destroyed and the gene stays in the genome. · 两个词说反是实测级别的错。
  • "Does no FRET signal mean no binding?" — No: it also fits two proteins that bind in the wrong orientation. · 阴性结果要会解释。

定义

  • pH — the negative logarithm, to base ten, of the hydrogen ion concentration in moles per litre · 氢离子浓度的负对数
  • pKa — the pH at which one particular group is exactly half dissociated · 半解离时的 pH
  • buffer — a weak acid together with its own conjugate base, resisting a change in pH · 缓冲液
  • isoelectric point — the pH at which a molecule carries no net charge and does not move in an electric field · 等电点
  • competence — the state in which a bacterium can take up DNA from outside · 感受态
  • transfection — the introduction of nucleic acid into a eukaryotic cell · 转染
  • transient vs stable transfection — the single difference is integration into a host chromosome · 瞬时与稳定转染
  • selection marker — a gene carried in with the foreign DNA whose product lets that cell survive a condition killing the rest · 选择标记
  • bait and prey — the known fixed half of a two-hybrid, and each library insert tested against it · 诱饵与猎物
  • reverse genetics — breaking a known sequence on purpose and reading the function off what then goes wrong · 反向遗传学
  • sgRNA — single guide RNA, one hairpin carrying a twenty-nucleotide guide · 单向导 RNA
  • PAM — protospacer-adjacent motif, usually NGG, required next to the target before Cas9 cuts · PAM
  • GT–AG rule — almost every intron（= intron = intervening sequence） starts with GT and ends with AG in the gene, read GU–AG in the transcript · GT–AG 法则
  • alternative splicing — one primary transcript spliced in more than one way, giving different proteins · 选择性剪接
  • Kozak sequence — the sequence around the start codon, A or G, two bases, AUG, then G, that stops the scanning ribosome · Kozak 序列

PESB · protein engineering and synthetic biology

卡 pe-clone · pe-expr · pe-pur · pe-sys · pe-evo · pe-cfps · 你自己的主场，⭐ Lipovová 整块在这里

  1. 克隆 — 每一种方法都在回答同一个问题 把一段基因和一个载体接成一个能在细胞里复制的环。七种方法听上去是七件事，其实都在答同一句： 怎么让两段 DNA 的末端互相找得到。末端的来处只有四个 —— 切在位点里面（经典酶切）· 切在位点外面（Golden Gate 的 type IIS）· 外切酶啃出单链（LIC 与 Gibson）· 根本不切（Gateway 的位点特异性重组）。 ⚠ 基因从哪来：查文献 → Addgene → RT-PCR → 全基因合成，越往后越贵。 ⚠ 真核基因组 DNA 里有内含子，原核没有剪接体 —— 直接从基因组扩出来放进大肠杆菌，表达出来是废的，所以走 mRNA 的 RT-PCR 或直接合成。
  2. 表达 — 几乎每一个麻烦都是同一个麻烦 从载体里的一个基因到管子里的蛋白。整张卡的脊梁只有一句： 蛋白被造出来的速度，快过细胞折叠它的速度。 折叠是分子内的，一条链自己塌下去；聚集是分子间的，要几条链碰上 —— 所以浓度一高，聚集的速率涨得比折叠快得多。 启动子有两个独立的轴：强度和紧密性，通常互相冲突（清单在 t-tools，别重讲）。 出事之后菌株表要倒着背：从症状问起，不从菌株名问起。 ⚠ 包涵体不是失败 —— 它是纯化上的好消息、产量上的坏消息。
  3. 纯化 — 五种模式，同样的四步 从澄清裂解液，到一管纯的、量得出来的蛋白。 所有模式都跑平衡 → 结合 → 洗涤 → 洗脱，模式之间只差一样：树脂抓的是哪一个性质。 遇到没见过的模式就问两句：它靠什么抓住？改什么它才放开？ 顺序是被逼出来的，不是挑出来的：捕获 → 中度纯化 → 精修， 因为体积从大到小，而要的分辨率从低到高。 ⚠ 亲和层析的判据是二元的 —— 它问「有没有那个标签」，不问「有多少电荷」， 所以它能一步做完别的模式要三步做的事。
  4. 选宿主 — 那张表是一条曲线，不是六个选项 是蛋白需要什么在选宿主，不是你在选宿主。 第一问就分出两类问题：有阶梯的和没有阶梯的 —— 不溶有一整条阶梯可以爬，二硫键也有；而缺糖基化机器连第一级都没有， 因为大肠杆菌没有糖基转移酶、也没有内质网，那是能力不存在，不是没调好。 第二问：糖必须像人的吗。必须像人 → 哺乳动物；只是要折叠帮助 → 昆虫或酵母，便宜得多。 剩下三问都在阵营内部问：二硫键 · 毒性 · 量级。 ⚠ 「它是真核生物所以会糖基化」是标准错答：酵母、昆虫、哺乳动物的糖不是同一种糖。
  5. 定向进化 — 造一堆，再把好的那个找出来 两个动作反复跑：造多样性 → 把好的找出来 → 再造一次。 「反复」和「每轮从上一轮的赢家出发」这两个词是定义里最重的两个。 脊梁一句：蛋白和编码它的基因必须物理连在一起 —— 因为蛋白既不能扩增，也不能测序，一千亿个分子里挑出那一个，没有基因你既读不出它、也做不出第二份。 所有展示方法只差「靠什么连」。 第二条轴是筛选还是选择，它决定了你的库能有多大。 ⚠ 你选什么就得到什么 —— 拿「谁抓得最紧」去选酶，挑出来的是产物抑制最严重的那些。
  6. 无细胞（= cell-free protein synthesis = CFPS = in vitro transcription–translation）与改写密码 — 先搬出来，才能改规矩 上半场：把翻译搬出细胞，在管子里跑。下半场：搬出来之后改它的规矩。 第二件事需要第一件事，这就是两讲合在一起教的原因。 铰链只有一句：重编程要的是减法 —— 某个天然合成酶必须不在场， 否则它一直把自己的氨基酸装到自己的 tRNA 上，靠数量取胜； 而一锅粗裂解物里你拿不掉任何一样东西，重构体系里你只要不加它就行了。 无细胞的所有优点其实是同一条优点：没有活细胞 —— 说出这一句，那张清单可以当场推出来。

Say this

This subject is really one project seen from six angles. First I join my gene to a vector, and every cloning method answers one question: how the two ends are made to find each other. Then I make the protein, usually in E. coli, where almost every problem is the same problem — the protein is made faster than the cell can fold it. Then I purify it, and every mode of chromatography runs the same four steps, differing only in which property the resin binds. If the protein needs modifications E. coli cannot make, I move up the scale of hosts — yeast, insect cells, mammalian cells — and I pay for each step with time and money. Finally, if the protein works but is not good enough, I stop optimising the conditions and change the protein itself: I make a library and either screen or select it. And if it cannot be made in a living cell at all, I go cell-free (also called cell-free protein synthesis, CFPS, in vitro transcription–translation).

约 50 秒 · 一条线：装上载体 → 做出来 → 纯出来 → 不够好就进化它

广度 · 六张卡压成一条线 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Lipovová、Hudeček、Martínek

① 所有克隆方法都在回答同一个问题：怎么让两段 DNA 的末端互相找得到 joining two ends

Cloning joins a gene to a vector to make a circle that replicates in a cell. Every method answers one question: what makes the two ends match. There are only four answers — cut inside the site, cut outside it, chew back one strand, or do not cut at all. In classical restriction cloning the sites are written on the primer tails and the enzyme cuts inside its own site, so the overhang sequence is dictated by the enzyme. The vector is dephosphorylated so that it cannot close on itself, which is the largest source of background colonies.

七种方法，四个来处；磷酸酶是记账，不是化学。

② 基因从哪来，以及内含子那道经典题 source of the gene · introns

The gene comes from the literature, from Addgene, from RT-PCR, or from whole-gene synthesis, in rising order of cost. ⚠ Eukaryotic genomic DNA contains introns (also called intervening sequence) and prokaryotes have no spliceosome, so I start from mRNA or synthesise the gene with codon optimisation.

直接从基因组扩，表达出来是废的。

③ Golden Gate：type IIS 酶切在识别位点外面，所以无疤痕，而且末端由你定 ⭐ Lipovová Golden Gate · BsaI

BsaI, BbsI and BsmBI cut outside their recognition sequence. Two things follow: the four-base overhang has a sequence I choose, so several fragments assemble directionally in one tube; and the site sits on the discarded piece, so the product is scarless.

一件事推出两个结果。

④ Golden Gate 的入场费叫 domestication：先用沉默突变删掉部件内部的位点 domestication

Before assembly I must remove every internal BsaI, BbsI or BsmBI site from my parts, or they are cut to pieces. I do that with silent mutations, which works only because the genetic code is degenerate. It is a one-off cost, and the parts are then reusable for ever.

付一次，永久可重用。

⑤ Gibson 与 LIC：把一条链啃掉，露出长到退火本身就够稳的单链 Gibson assembly · LIC

Gibson assembly uses T5 exonuclease with overlaps of twenty to forty base pairs, in one tube at fifty degrees. LIC uses T4 DNA polymerase as an exonuclease and gives about fifteen bases, which anneal so stably that no ligase is needed.

末端够长，就不需要连接酶。

⑥ 最小质粒只要复制起点加一个选择标记，而复制起点决定拷贝数 origin of replication · incompatibility

The minimal plasmid is an origin of replication plus a selection marker; the rest is added for a purpose. The origin sets the copy number, and ⚠ two plasmids with the same origin cannot coexist in one cell, which matters whenever I co-express two proteins.

共表达绝不能共转同一个复制起点。

⑦ 表达的脊梁：蛋白被造出来的速度快过细胞折叠它的速度 folding versus aggregation

Almost every problem in bacterial expression is one problem in a different place. Folding is intramolecular, so one chain collapses on itself. Aggregation is intermolecular, so several chains must meet. Aggregation therefore rises with concentration far more steeply than folding does.

所以降温、降诱导剂全是同一招。

⑧ 启动子有两个独立的性质：诱导后有多强，和诱导前漏多少 strength versus tightness

Strength is how much I get after induction, and tightness is how little leaks before it. The two usually conflict, and which one matters is decided by the protein, not the promoter — a toxic protein needs tightness before it needs strength. The promoters themselves I have listed already.

清单在 t-tools，这里只说两个轴。

⑨ 菌株表要倒着背：他从症状问起，不从菌株名问起 expression strains

Degraded product sends me to BL21, which lacks the Lon and OmpT proteases. A truncated eukaryotic product is codon bias, so Rosetta, which supplies rare-codon tRNAs. Cytoplasmic disulfides need Origami or SHuffle. All-or-none induction needs Tuner, which lacks lacY.

症状 → 菌株 → 它改了什么。

⑩ 包涵体不是失败，是纯化上的好消息、产量上的坏消息 ⭐ Lipovová inclusion bodies

Inclusion bodies are aggregates of misfolded recombinant protein in the cytoplasm. They are high purity and stable, they let me make products that would otherwise kill the host, and they can be purified under denaturing conditions. ⚠ But refolding yields are typically only one to five per cent.

好处全在「弄出来」，没有一条解决「折回去」。

⑪ 不溶的对策是五个手段两族：减少供给，或提高处理能力 ⭐ Lipovová solubility tags · chaperones

I lower the temperature, lower the IPTG, add a solubility fusion such as SUMO, MBP or thioredoxin, co-express chaperones (also called heat shock protein (many of them)) — DnaK with DnaJ and GrpE, or GroEL with GroES — and only as a last resort change the host. The lecture's own words: adjust the conditions, do not abandon the construct.

融合伴侣放 N 端是机制，不是习惯 —— 它最先出核糖体、最先折好。

⑫ 复性的三条路其实是同一件事：把有效浓度压低，让折叠赢过聚集 refolding

I solubilise in six molar guanidinium chloride or eight molar urea, then refold by dialysis, by rapid dilution, or on the column. A glutathione redox pair lets wrong disulfides reshuffle, and arginine suppresses aggregation intermediates. On-column refolding is the best, because it makes aggregation physically impossible.

三条路，一个目标。

⑬ 二硫键有两条路绕开还原性的胞质：敲掉还原系统，或者换个房间 disulfide bonds · periplasm

The E. coli cytoplasm is reducing, kept so by thioredoxin and glutathione, so disulfides do not form there. Either I remove the obstacle with an Origami or SHuffle strain, mutated in trxB and gor, or I avoid the compartment and send the protein to the periplasm with a signal peptide (also called signal sequence) such as pelB or OmpA.

周质天然是氧化性的，还自带 Dsb 校对系统。

⑭ 层析的顺序是被逼出来的：体积从大到小，分辨率从低到高 capture · intermediate · polishing

Capture takes the target out of crude lysate, usually by affinity, and is chosen for capacity. Intermediate purification is ion exchange or hydrophobic interaction. Polishing is size exclusion (also called size-exclusion chromatography, gel filtration), which must come last, because its load volume cannot exceed five per cent of the column volume.

那 5% 是硬的物理上限，不是习惯做法。

⑮ 镍柱上有两个杀手，它们不是洗脱，是把镍整个抢走 EDTA · DTT strip the nickel

Beyond the imidazole elution I have already described, two reagents destroy the column. EDTA is a chelator and strips the nickel off the resin; ⚠ it is a routine lysis-buffer component, added against metal-dependent proteases. DTT does the same by reducing the nickel. The column turns white and is finished.

咪唑洗脱在 t-tools；这里说的是把柱子弄报废的两样。

⑯ 「中和标签」和「移除标签」是两个问题，答错就丢分 ⭐ Lipovová tag removal · TEV · SUMO protease

Neutralising means getting the protein off the resin, and the answer is imidazole. Removing means cutting the tag off with a protease, afterwards, in a tube. TEV recognises ENLYFQ and leaves an extra glycine on my protein for ever; SUMO protease reads the fold rather than a sequence, so it leaves a native N-terminus.

一个在柱上，一个在柱后。

⑰ 收尾两个数：纯度看 SDS-PAGE，浓度用 A280 ⭐ Hudeček purity and quantity

SDS does three jobs at once — it unfolds the chain, it gives every protein the same charge-to-mass ratio, and it makes everything rod-shaped — so only molecular weight is left to vary. A280 needs no standard curve, because the extinction coefficient is calculated from the sequence, mostly from tryptophan and tyrosine.

这是它跟 Bradford 的根本差别。

⑱ 书外 · general：纯化表上真正的数字是比活 specific activity

A purification table gives one row per step with total protein, total activity and specific activity, which is units of activity per milligram. Yield and purification fold follow from it. A gel alone cannot show this, because it says nothing about whether the target is still active. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

跑胶只说杂带没了，不说活性还在。

⑲ 选宿主的第一问，是分出「有阶梯的问题」和「没有阶梯的问题」 choosing a host

Insoluble protein has a ladder — lower the temperature, change the strain, add a fusion, refold. Unformed disulfides also have a ladder, and it stays inside E. coli. ⚠ Missing glycosylation machinery has no ladder at all: E. coli has no glycosyltransferases and no endoplasmic reticulum, so the ability is absent, not badly tuned.

最常见的错答是把「有二硫键」当成「必须用真核系统」。

⑳ 「它会糖基化」不是一个答案，因为那不是同一种糖 glycosylation is not one thing

Yeast builds high-mannose structures that are immunogenic in humans and vary between molecules in one batch. Insect cells modify, but not with human glycans either. Only mammalian cells give the human complex type. So the question is not whether a host glycosylates, but whether it installs the glycan my protein needs.

两个后果分开说：免疫原性，和异质性。

㉑ E. coli 是曲线的左端，而且没有哪一条优点特别突出 E. coli

It is still the default after forty years because the advantages add up: it doubles in about twenty minutes, the media are cheap, scale-up is predictable, and the toolbox is enormous. It is also the only practical route to isotope labelling, which needs a minimal medium with one defined nitrogen and one defined carbon source.

「累加起来的」这句话本身就是答案的一部分。

㉒ 酵母便宜、会大部分修饰，但两个缺点都要能说 yeast · Pichia pastoris

Yeast is a eukaryote, so it performs most modifications and is still cheap to culture. Its two real weaknesses are high-mannose glycans and a cell wall that is very hard to break. That is why the standard strategy in Pichia pastoris is secretion, which avoids lysis altogether.

缺点推出策略。

㉓ 昆虫细胞坐在酵母和哺乳动物之间：折叠和修饰都有，代价中等 insect cells

Insect cells fold complex proteins and carry out many modifications, including disulfide bonds, at moderate cost. They sit between yeast and mammalian cells on the scale. I choose them when the protein needs eukaryotic folding help but the glycans do not have to be human-like.

中间那一档的存在理由，一句话。

㉔ 昆虫系统的载体是一个病毒，蛋白是在一次感染里被做出来的，用的是 polyhedrin 启动子 baculovirus · polyhedrin promoter

The vector is a baculovirus, a circular double-stranded DNA virus of about one hundred and twenty kilobase pairs. It infects over six hundred species, mostly larvae, and ⚠ it does not replicate in vertebrates, which is the safety argument. The protein is made during the infection, not by a plasmid sitting quietly in the cell. My gene goes under the polyhedrin promoter, one of the strongest known: in the wild the virus must pack itself into polyhedrin crystals to survive outside a host, so that very late promoter has been pushed to be extremely strong. In culture the virus needs none of that, so polyhedrin can be replaced by my gene at no cost to replication.

强度和「删得起」是同一个原因。

㉕ 代价是要先做出一支病毒：所以昆虫系统比大肠杆菌慢得多才出第一个样品 Bac-to-Bac · virus stock

In Bac-to-Bac the gene is cloned between Tn7 sites, transformed into DH10Bac, and transposed into a bacmid, a viral genome maintained in E. coli. Blue-white screening takes four days, the large bacmid DNA is handled gently, transfected into Sf9, and P1 virus is harvested after four to seven days, then amplified to P2 and P3.

第一个样品之前，先花掉一两周做病毒。

㉖ 感染时要定的是 MOI，而细胞系有三种，性格各不相同 MOI · Sf9 · Sf21 · Hi5 · S2

The multiplicity of infection is optimised between one to one and ten to one. Sf9 and Sf21, from the fall armyworm, are the robust standard at about six times ten to the sixth per millilitre; Hi5 often gives higher secreted yields; S2, from Drosophila, reaches the highest density. They grow at twenty-four to twenty-seven degrees.

半贴壁，很容易驯化成悬浮 —— 所以能放大。

㉗ 代次效应：滴度很高而表达在掉 ⭐ Lipovová passage effect

⚠ Some baculoviruses lose the gene of interest, especially after P3. My gene is pure burden to the virus — extra bases to replicate, no benefit — so viruses that drop the insert replicate faster and take over. The symptom is high titre with falling expression. I express from P1 or P2 and freeze a low-passage seed stock.

不知道这一条，现场会一头雾水。

㉘ 昆虫细胞往两个方向各比一次，答案才完整 insect cells compared both ways

Against E. coli: better folding, disulfides and modifications, but slower and dearer, because a virus stock must be made and amplified first. Against mammalian cells: cheaper and higher yielding, but ⚠ the glycans are not human, which matters if the protein is a therapeutic. S2 offers a third way — a stable line built in about three weeks, so no virus is made again.

两个方向都比，才不是背了一个位置。

㉙ 哺乳动物细胞是唯一给完整人源产物的，而瞬时和稳定回答的是两个问题 CHO · HEK293 · transient versus stable

Only mammalian cells give correct folding, correct disulfide pairing and complex glycosylation. HEK293 is the research line and CHO the production line. ⚠ Transient gives material in under a week but varies between batches; stable takes weeks to build and then varies hardly at all. So the deciding question is how many times I will make this protein.

不是「这一次要多少」。

㉚ 定向进化的脊梁：蛋白和编码它的基因必须物理连在一起 ⭐ Hudeček genotype–phenotype linkage

A protein cannot be amplified and cannot be sequenced, so the winner is lost without its gene. Phage display links them in a particle: ⚠ pIII at three to five copies allows monovalent display, which is required for true affinity, while pVIII at about 2700 copies selects weak binders that win by numbers. Ribosome display omits the stop codon (also called termination codon, nonsense codon); mRNA display uses puromycin. Yeast display I have already described.

多价会把亲和力测虚 —— Kd 要在单价上读。

㉛ 筛选还是选择，决定了你的库能有多大 screening versus selection · library size

In a screen I test every variant; in a selection I test nothing, and the pressure does the work. Synthesis gives about ten to the eighteen molecules, but getting into cells drops it to ten to the six or eight; in vitro display returns to ten to the thirteen. ⚠ I must screen three times the library size for coverage, because sampling is with replacement.

中间那个悬崖，整个是「细胞」两个字。

㉜ 怎么建库，取决于你已经知道多少 ⭐ Lipovová ⭐ Martínek error-prone PCR · DNA shuffling

If I know which residues, that is site-directed mutagenesis; if I know the positions but not the amino acids, saturation mutagenesis; if I want mutations but not where, error-prone PCR, which needs Taq and never a proofreading polymerase, biased with manganese and unbalanced dNTPs to give one to five mutations per gene. ⚠ The library is never unbiased. DNA shuffling instead recombines homologous genes with DNase I and a primerless PCR. De novo design starts from nothing at all.

太少全是野生型，太多全是死的。

㉝ 无细胞合成的所有优点其实是同一条：没有活细胞 cell-free protein synthesis

Translation runs in a tube, from a crude extract (top-down) or a reconstituted PURE system (bottom-up). Because no cell is alive, it makes toxic products, it accepts a linear PCR product directly as template, and it is the fastest of all, about one day. The costs are low yield, high price and unsolved scale-up.

三件套：核糖体 · 氨基酸连同 tRNA 和合成酶 · 能量再生。

㉞ 改写密码：先说自己在答哪一个问题 orthogonal pair · amber suppression · flexizyme

There are two independent questions: how the monomer gets onto the tRNA, and which codon points to it. The heavy route evolves an orthogonal synthetase and tRNA pair for every new monomer; the shortcut is a flexizyme, a ribozyme that charges tRNA by reading only an activating group and the 3-prime CCA, so the synthetase question never arises. Amber suppression answers the second question, using TAG, the least-used codon in E. coli.

2026-09-01 丢掉的就是这一问 —— 他要的是 flexizyme。

陷阱问法

  • "How do you neutralise a His-tag?" — Imidazole, which competes for the nickel. A protease removes the tag, which is a different question. · 洗脱层答咪唑，移除层才答蛋白酶。
  • "It is a eukaryote, so glycosylation is solved?" — No. Yeast, insect and mammalian glycans are different glycans, and only mammalian cells give the human complex type. · 「会糖基化」不是答案。
  • "My protein has disulfide bonds, so I need a eukaryotic host?" — No. I stay in E. coli and use Origami or SHuffle, or the periplasm. · 二硫键有阶梯。
  • "You added IPTG to a pET plasmid and got nothing." — The strain has no DE3 cassette, so the host never makes T7 RNA polymerase. · 宿主里没有那个盒子。
  • "Why are the white colonies still wrong?" — Blue-white screening only shows that lacZ-alpha was interrupted; it says nothing about orientation or sequence. · 白色只说明插进去了。
  • "Your baculovirus titre is high but expression is falling." — That is the passage effect: viruses that have lost the insert replicate faster and take over. · 用 P1 或 P2 表达。
  • "You selected for binding and got a poor enzyme." — You get what you select for; display selects binding, so it enriches product inhibition. Catalysts need compartmentalisation in droplets. · 选什么得什么。

定义

  • recombinant — DNA joined together outside the organism · 重组
  • heterologous expression — expressing a gene in a species it does not come from · 异源表达
  • type IIS enzyme — a restriction enzyme that cuts outside its own recognition site, such as BsaI · IIS 型限制酶
  • domestication — removing internal recognition sites from a part by silent mutation · 驯化
  • inclusion body — an aggregate of misfolded recombinant protein in the cytoplasm · 包涵体
  • solubility tag — a highly soluble fusion partner such as SUMO or MBP that keeps the product soluble · 可溶性标签
  • IMAC — immobilised metal affinity chromatography, the mode a His-tag binds on Ni-NTA · 固定化金属亲和层析
  • specific activity — units of enzyme activity per milligram of total protein · 比活
  • baculovirus — the insect-cell expression vector; a large circular double-stranded DNA virus that does not replicate in vertebrates · 杆状病毒
  • bacmid — a baculovirus genome maintained as a plasmid in E. coli · 杆粒
  • multiplicity of infection — the ratio of infectious virus particles to cells at infection · 感染复数
  • passage effect — loss of the gene of interest from a baculovirus stock over successive amplifications · 代次效应
  • directed evolution — rounds of screening or selection separated by further diversification · 定向进化
  • screen versus selection — in a screen every variant is tested; in a selection only survivors remain · 筛选与选择
  • error-prone PCR — amplification under conditions that lower fidelity, giving one to five mutations per gene · 易错 PCR
  • DNA shuffling — fragmenting homologous genes with DNase I and reassembling them into chimaeras by primerless PCR · 基因重排
  • orthogonal pair — a synthetase and tRNA that work only with each other and not with the host's · 正交对
  • amber suppression — reading the stop codon（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon） TAG as an amino acid instead of a stop · 琥珀抑制
  • flexizyme — an artificial ribozyme that charges any tRNA with almost any activated monomer · 柔性酶

Lab techniques

实验技术 34 题 · 答案都是你自己写过的，这里压成一句一条

  1. 配一管溶液 — 一条算术，两个坑 质量 ＝ 摩尔浓度 × 体积 × 分子量，稀释是 C₁V₁ ＝ C₂V₂。整门课的算术就这两条。 ⚠ 摩尔浓度是「每升溶液」，不是「每升溶剂」 —— 这五个字就是为什么要定容： 先用少于终体积的溶剂溶开，再补到刻度。溶质自己占体积，先量一升水再把粉倒进去，配出来一定偏稀。 分子量照瓶子上印的取，带结晶水的盐每摩尔更重。 标准溶液的定义是「浓度被准确知道」，所以定量转移（称量舟要冲洗进瓶）、定容、贴标签，每一步都是在保这个「准确」。 ⚠ 标准品的误差是系统误差：它原封不动传给每一个照它测出来的样品 —— 标准液坏了不是打折扣，是整批作废。 两个试剂各有各的坑：1 M EDTA 的坑是溶解度（游离酸要用氢氧化钠把 pH 提到约 8 才溶得开）； 6 M 盐酸胍的坑是体积（固体本身占掉很大空间，先小体积溶、再定容），而且它溶解强烈吸热，会又冷又慢。
  2. pH 与缓冲 — 你挑的从来不是酸或碱，是一个 pKa pH ＝ 氢离子活度的负对数，差一个单位就是十倍。 缓冲液 ＝ 一个弱酸 ＋ 它自己的共轭碱：加进来的酸被碱型吃掉，加进来的碱被酸型吃掉。 pH = pK_a + log([共轭碱]/[弱酸]) —— 要用的那一层读法只有一句： 两种形式相等时 log 项为零，pH ＝ pKa，所以挑一个 pKa 贴着目标 pH 的缓冲液，工作区间是 pKa ± 1。 ⚠ 「酸性缓冲液 vs 碱性缓冲液」是个假对立：机制一模一样，差别只是那个 pKa 落在 7 的哪一边 （乙酸盐 ≈ 4.8 在下，Tris ≈ 8.1 在上）。 两个要盯死：Tris 的 pKa 随温度强烈移动，室温调好的 Tris 进冷室就是另一个 pH；磷酸盐会和钙镁生成沉淀。 pH 计那一题接得上：它测的是电压再换算成 pH，斜率和截距都会漂，所以要用两个把目标夹在中间的标准液 （先 7 定截距，再 4 或 10 定斜率），同温校准，用新鲜标准液，电极吸干不能擦。
  3. 量出一个数 — 每台仪器都只量一个物理量 吸光度 ＝ ε × c × l，这条线性关系就是分光光度法能定量的全部理由。 仪器四件：光源 · 单色器 · 固定光程的比色皿 · 检测器；先读空白，把缓冲液和比色皿自己的吸收减掉。 读数超出线性范围就稀释后重测，绝不外推 —— 那里检测器正好饱和。 标准曲线的要害是：它校准的是整个流程，不只是仪器 —— 所以标准品必须和样品同缓冲液、同温育、同一批次测。 ⚠ OD600 是这一族里的假成员：细胞在 600 nm 不吸收，它们把光散射出光路，所以它没有 ε，也分不出死活。 核酸定量三层递进：A260（快，但凡带碱基的都吸）→ 必须连比值一起报（A260/A280 ≈ 1.8 是 DNA，≈ 2.0 是 RNA）→ 只对双链发光的荧光染料法才特异 → 要看完整性就跑胶。 而这一切的下游是移液器：准确度（离真值多近）靠校准和选量程，精密度（重复之间多一致）靠手法 —— ⚠ 一支移液器可以非常精密，同时稳定地错着。
  4. 把混合物分开 — 力、分配、筛孔，三种分法 离心靠一个远大于重力的力，沉降速率由大小、形状、密度决定。 差速离心（低速沉大的、留上清、再提速沉下一档）把裂解液变成澄清裂解液； 密度梯度离心分的不是大小而是浮力密度，样品走到自身密度等于介质那一点就停。 ⚠ rcf 正比于「半径 × 转速²」，所以同一个 rpm 换个转子就是另一个力 —— 方法里写 × g，不写 rpm。 层析只有一个想法：混合物在固定相和流动相之间分配，作用强的走得慢；每一种模式都跑同样四步 —— 平衡 · 结合 · 洗 · 洗脱。模式之间变的只有一样：固定相被选来利用哪一种物理性质 （电荷 → 离子交换 · 疏水性 → HIC 与反相 · 大小 → 凝胶过滤 · 特异结合 → 亲和）。 ⚠ 一个诊断习惯：读跑柱先看流穿液 —— 待在那里的蛋白根本没结合过，问题在结合条件，不在洗脱。 电泳的前提只有一句：单位质量电荷必须一致，否则你同时在量电荷和大小，两个都读不出来。 核酸天生满足（每个核苷酸一个磷酸），蛋白不满足 —— 所以要加 SDS 人为造出来； 而两块胶是为了让所有蛋白从同一条起跑线出发：浓缩胶只浓缩，分离胶才筛分。
  5. 认出一个分子 — 抗体认形状，质谱认质量 免疫测定的分工是固定的：抗体提供特异性，标记提供信号。 夹心 ELISA 三次加样（捕获抗体 → 样品 → 针对另一个表位的检测抗体）， ⚠ 要两支抗体认两个表位，这正是它特异的原因；定量还是靠标准曲线。 Western blot 是同一个想法换个摆法：分离 → 转印 → 探测， 而转印是必需的不是图方便 —— 让分离成立的那些胶孔太细，笨重的抗体钻不进去。 ⚠ 上样对照验的是 Western blot 自己验不了的那个假设（每道起点总蛋白相同）， 挑它的条件不是「丰度高」，而是已被独立证明不随处理而变；用法是做比值，不是看有没有。 质谱换一条路：它不问形状，它测质荷比。三个部件（离子源 · 质量分析器 · 检测器）、 两种软电离（电喷雾 · MALDI）、一条 bottom-up 流程（胰蛋白酶酶解 → 液相色谱 → 选一条肽碎裂 → 从质量差读序列）。 落点一句：它给的是翻译后修饰，那正是测序告诉不了你的东西。
  6. 做出一个分子，再别弄伤自己 — 表达、PCR、无菌、安全 细菌表达五步一条线：质粒 → 转化 → 长到对数中期诱导 → 收菌破碎 → 亲和层析 ＋ 跑胶验。 ⚠ 质粒上的元件第二句就主动报完，别等他问：T7 启动子 · 核糖体结合位点 · 复制起点 · 抗性标记 · 亲和标签。 菌株的选择对应三个不同的难处：转录（BL21(DE3) 带 T7 聚合酶）· 翻译（Rosetta 补稀有密码子 tRNA）· 折叠（Origami／SHuffle 的胞质还原性较弱，二硫键能形成），蛋白酶保护垫在这三样下面。 IPTG 替代的是别乳糖，而且 ——诱导是把一个阻挡拿掉，不是把一个基因打开。 给表面受体加标签，整道题的轴是拓扑：先定要检测哪一侧，再避开会被切掉的信号肽。 PCR 六样里镁最该讲道理：它两头都有代价，少了酶慢，多了错配引发； 而 Taq 没有校正活性，所以要克隆、表达、测序的一律换高保真酶。 最后两件事的判据都极干净：灭菌的标准永远是杀死芽孢（121 ℃ 就是这么定出来的，0.22 µm 过滤是除菌不是灭菌）； 危险化学品按控制层级答，而 PPE 排在最后是因为它只保护一个人，失效的时候没有声音。

Say this

Almost every technique here measures one physical quantity, and the honest answer to "what is the principle" is to name that quantity first. A spectrophotometer measures absorbance, and the Lambert-Beer (also called Beer–Lambert law, Beer's law) law makes it proportional to concentration. OD600 is the exception that proves it: cells do not absorb at that wavelength, they scatter light out of the beam. A centrifuge applies a force, and that force depends on the rotor radius, so I report times g rather than rpm. Chromatography measures partitioning between a stationary and a mobile phase. Electrophoresis measures size, but only once the charge per unit mass is uniform, which is why proteins need SDS. An immunoassay measures antibody binding, and mass spectrometry measures the mass to charge ratio. Once I have named the quantity, the control and the working range follow from it.

约 45 秒 · 先报物理量，再报它的对照和线性范围 —— 这个顺序本身就是答案

广度 · 34 题，一题一两句 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 4 位考官：Hudeček、Hodek、Šulc、Lipovová

① 摩尔浓度是「每升溶液」，所以要定容而不是加一升溶剂 molarity

Molarity is moles of solute per litre of solution, so mass equals molarity times volume times molecular weight and dilutions follow C1V1 equals C2V2. I dissolve in less than the final volume and make up to the mark, and I take the molecular weight from the bottle, because a hydrated salt weighs more per mole.

溶质自己占体积。

② 标准溶液的每一步都在保护那个「准确已知」 standard solution

A standard solution is one whose concentration is known accurately, so I transfer quantitatively, rinsing the weighing boat into the volumetric flask, make up to the mark, mix by inversion and label. An error in the standard is systematic: it passes into every unknown measured against it.

留在称量舟里的都是误差。

③ EDTA 的坑是溶解度，6 M 盐酸胍的坑是体积 EDTA · guanidine hydrochloride

The free acid of EDTA will not dissolve until sodium hydroxide raises the pH to about eight, and I set the pH before making up to volume. Six molar guanidine hydrochloride goes into a smaller volume first, because the solid takes up so much space, and dissolving it is strongly endothermic.

一个卡在 pH，一个卡在体积。

④ pH 是氢离子活度的负对数，缓冲液是弱酸加它自己的共轭碱 pH · buffer · Henderson-Hasselbalch

pH is the negative logarithm of the hydrogen ion activity, and a buffer is a weak acid with its conjugate base: added acid is absorbed by the base form, added base by the acid form. Henderson-Hasselbalch says pH equals the pKa plus the log of that ratio, so equal amounts give pH equal to pKa.

所以挑 pKa 贴着目标 pH 的那一个，范围 ±1。

⑤ 酸性和碱性缓冲液机制完全一样，差别只是挑了哪个 pKa acidic versus basic buffers

The mechanism is identical and only the chosen pKa differs: acetate, near four point eight, buffers below pH seven, and Tris, near eight point one, above it. Tris has a strongly temperature-dependent pKa, and phosphate precipitates with calcium and magnesium.

室温调好的 Tris 进冷室就是另一个 pH。

⑥ pH 计测的是电压，斜率和截距都会漂，所以要两点校准 calibrating a pH meter

The meter converts a voltage into a pH, and a glass electrode drifts in both slope and offset, so I use two standard buffers that bracket my target — conventionally pH seven for the offset, then pH four or ten for the slope. I calibrate at the temperature I will measure at, use fresh standards, and blot the electrode rather than wipe it.

单点校准正好掩盖坏掉的那一项。

⑦ 吸光度等于消光系数乘浓度乘光程，这个线性关系才让它能定量 ⭐ Hudeček Lambert-Beer law

Absorbance equals the molar extinction coefficient times concentration times path length, so absorbance is linearly proportional to concentration — which is what makes spectrophotometry quantitative. I use it for protein at 280 nanometres, nucleic acid at 260 and NADH at 340, and it fails at high concentration and assumes no scattering.

280 是色氨酸和酪氨酸在吸。

⑧ 分光光度计四件套，控制项是空白 ⭐ Hudeček spectrophotometry

The instrument is a light source, a monochromator, a cuvette of fixed path length and a detector that compares transmitted with incident light, and absorbance is the logarithm of that ratio, so it has no units. The control is the blank, everything except the analyte, read first so buffer and cuvette are subtracted.

280 nm 以下要用石英比色皿。

⑨ 标准曲线校准的是整个流程，不只是仪器 standard curve

I read the unknown off a line built from standards measured in the same run, so the method never measures concentration directly. Two constraints carry every failure: the unknown must fall inside the range of the standards, and the standards must be treated exactly like the samples.

超出范围就稀释重测，绝不外推。

⑩ OD600 测的是散射不是吸收 ⭐ Hudeček OD600

OD600 is optical density at six hundred nanometres, a turbidity reading standing in for cell density — and it is not absorbance in the Lambert-Beer sense, because cells scatter light out of the beam. It counts living and dead cells alike and is linear only to about 0.6 to 0.8.

要真正的活菌数就涂板数 CFU。

⑪ A260 给浓度，两个比值给纯度 quantifying DNA and RNA

Absorbance at 260 nanometres is quickest, and an A260 of one is about 50 micrograms per millilitre of double-stranded DNA, about 40 for RNA. It cannot tell DNA from RNA, so I report ratios too: A260 over A280 near 1.8 for DNA and 2.0 for RNA, while a fluorescent dye is the specific option.

A260/A230 偏低 ＝ 带进了胍盐或苯酚。

⑫ 准确度是离真值多近，精密度是重复之间多一致 accuracy and precision of pipetting

A pipette can be very precise and consistently wrong. Accuracy comes from calibration, checked gravimetrically with distilled water where one milligram is one microlitre, and from using the pipette in the upper part of its range; precision comes from technique — first stop to aspirate, second only to expel, vertical, slow, and pre-wet the tip.

不预湿，第一次排液就偏少。

⑬ 空气置换对水溶液准，粘稠和易挥发的液体要正向置换 types of micropipettes

Air displacement puts a cushion of air between piston and liquid and is unreliable for volatile, viscous or dense liquids, because that cushion expands and contracts. Positive displacement has the piston touching the liquid in a disposable capillary, and it is also what I use in PCR work, since there is no air path for an aerosol.

单道 · 多道 · 连续分液 · 电动，是形式不是机制。

⑭ 离心机施加远大于重力的力，沉降由大小、形状、密度决定 role of a centrifuge

A centrifuge separates by a force far greater than gravity, and particles sediment at rates set by size, shape and density. Differential centrifugation pellets the largest things first and spins the supernatant harder, while a density gradient separates by buoyant density, each species stopping where its density matches the medium.

对侧管一定要配平。

⑮ rcf 还取决于转子半径，所以方法里写 × g 不写 rpm rpm versus rcf

rpm is how fast the rotor turns; rcf is the force the sample feels, as multiples of gravity, and it is proportional to the radius times the square of the speed. So the same rpm in a different rotor gives a different force, and a protocol quoting rpm alone cannot be reproduced.

换算表只对某一个转子半径成立。

⑯ 所有层析都是在两相之间分配，四步不变 principles of chromatography

The mixture is partitioned between a stationary and a mobile phase, and what interacts more with the stationary phase moves more slowly; every mode runs equilibrate, bind, wash and elute, isocratically or on a gradient. I read a run starting from the flow-through, because protein sitting there never bound at all.

上样前先换到结合缓冲液。

⑰ 五种层析模式，按它利用的那个物理性质命名 types of chromatography

Ion exchange uses net charge and elutes on a salt gradient; hydrophobic interaction binds in high salt and elutes in low; size exclusion (also called size-exclusion chromatography, gel filtration) binds nothing, so it is gentle but dilutes; affinity, usually a histidine tag on a nickel column, is the highest-resolution single step. Reverse phase uses hydrophobicity under organic solvent, so it denatures and is analytical.

HIC 与反相同一原理，区别是蛋白活不活得下来。

⑱ 电泳按大小分的前提是单位质量电荷一致 principle of gel electrophoresis

Charged molecules move through a porous gel and are sieved by size, but only if the charge per unit mass is the same for everything — otherwise I measure charge and size at once and can read neither. Nucleic acid meets that for free, one phosphate per nucleotide; a protein does not, so SDS imposes a uniform negative charge.

DNA 用嵌入染料，蛋白用考马斯或银染。

⑲ 浓缩胶只浓缩，分离胶才筛分 stacking and resolving gel

Separation needs every protein to start from the same line, but a loaded well is millimetres deep. The stacking gel, large pores at pH 6.8, compresses the sample into a thin band by isotachophoresis without separating anything; the resolving gel, small pores at pH 8.8, then sieves by size.

做堆积的是缓冲液里的甘氨酸，不是胶。

⑳ 细菌 DNA 提取四步，质粒改用碱裂解 DNA extraction from bacteria

Four steps: lyse with SDS after lysozyme opens the peptidoglycan, remove protein with proteinase K and RNA with RNase A, separate with phenol-chloroform or a silica column, then precipitate with salt and cold ethanol. For plasmid I use alkaline lysis: on neutralisation the small closed circle renatures and stays soluble while chromosomal DNA precipitates.

留着 RNA 会把 A260 抬高。

㉑ 免疫测定：抗体给特异性，标记给信号 ⭐ Hodek immunoassays · sandwich ELISA

An immunoassay uses the specific binding of an antibody to its antigen in a complex mixture: the antibody supplies specificity, the label supplies signal. In a sandwich ELISA a capture antibody coats the plate and a detection antibody against a second epitope carries the enzyme — and needing two epitopes is what makes it specific.

空白孔 ＋ 不加检测抗体的孔，两个对照。

㉒ Western blot 三步，转印是必需的不是图方便 ⭐ Hodek Western blot

Three separable steps: separate by SDS-PAGE, transfer to nitrocellulose or PVDF, then probe with a primary and a labelled secondary antibody. The transfer is necessary, because the gel pores that made separation possible are too fine for a bulky antibody and the soft gel would not survive repeated washing.

Southern 是 DNA，Northern 是 RNA，Western 是蛋白。

㉓ 上样对照验的是 Western blot 自己验不了的那个假设 loading control

It checks that every lane started with the same total protein, an assumption the blot depends on and cannot otherwise verify, since uneven pipetting, uneven transfer or partial degradation all look like a real change in my protein. The condition for choosing it is that the protein is known not to respond to my treatment, and I use it as a ratio within the lane.

肌动蛋白、微管蛋白、GAPDH 各有已发表的例外。

㉔ 质谱测的是质荷比，它给的是测序告诉不了你的翻译后修饰 ⭐ Šulc mass spectrometry

Mass spectrometry measures the mass to charge ratio of ions, with an ion source, a mass analyser and a detector, and the sample must be ionised without being destroyed — hence electrospray and MALDI. The bottom-up workflow digests with trypsin, separates the peptides, then fragments one and reads the sequence from mass differences.

磷酸化与未磷酸化：同一个基因，不同的分子。

㉕ 细菌表达五步：克隆、诱导、破碎、亲和纯化、跑胶 ⭐ Lipovová protein expression workflow

I clone the gene into a plasmid carrying a T7 promoter, a ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence), an origin of replication, an antibiotic marker and an affinity tag, and transform BL21(DE3). I grow to mid exponential phase, induce with IPTG at a lower temperature for solubility, sonicate on ice, then purify by affinity chromatography and check every fraction on a gel.

元件那一串别等他问，第二句就报完。

㉖ 三个表达菌株对应三个难处：转录、翻译、折叠 ⭐ Lipovová BL21(DE3) · Rosetta · Origami

DE3 means the strain carries the T7 RNA polymerase gene, so it works with pET vectors, and BL21 also lacks the Lon and OmpT proteases. Rosetta adds tRNAs for codons rare in E. coli; Origami or SHuffle have a less reducing cytoplasm so disulfide bonds can form.

没有 (DE3)，pET 质粒什么都不表达。

㉗ IPTG 替代别乳糖，诱导是拿掉一个阻挡 IPTG

IPTG, a synthetic inducer of the lac operon, replaces allolactose: both bind the LacI repressor and make it release the operator, so induction removes a block rather than switching a gene on. IPTG is not metabolised, so the level stays where I set it, while lactose is degraded by the very operon it turns on.

50 µM～1 mM，对数中期加。

㉘ 给表面受体加标签，整道题的轴是拓扑 ⭐ Lipovová tagging a surface receptor

A surface receptor sits in three compartments, so I first decide which side to detect: an extracellular tag can be labelled on intact living cells, a cytoplasmic one needs permeabilisation. The N-terminal signal peptide (also called signal sequence) is cleaved during translocation, so I place the tag after it or on the C terminus, on a flexible linker and away from the ligand site.

破坏转运的标签，回答的是另一个问题。

㉙ PCR 六样，镁两头都有代价 components of a PCR mixture

Template, two primers, a thermostable polymerase, the four dNTPs, magnesium and buffer. The primers flank the target, so they fix both ends and set the annealing temperature; magnesium is the cofactor and also stabilises primer-template pairing, so too little makes the enzyme slow and too much gives mispriming and primer dimers.

出非特异条带，先把镁降下来。

㉚ 校正活性是长在同一个聚合酶身上的 3′→5′ 外切酶 proofreading

A mismatch pairs badly and stalls extension, so the primer terminus moves into the three prime to five prime exonuclease site, the wrong base is excised and synthesis resumes — a correction inside the enzyme, not a separate repair pathway. Taq lacks it, while Pfu and Kapa have it, so anything cloned, expressed or sequenced needs a proofreading enzyme.

早期循环的一个错会被扩增成一大部分。

㉛ 卡那霉素耐热，氨苄会降解，卫星菌落因此只长在氨苄板上 ampicillin versus kanamycin

Ampicillin has a hydrolysable beta-lactam ring, destroyed by heat and faster by the beta-lactamase the resistant cells secrete, which clears a halo where non-resistant cells then grow. Kanamycin is an aminoglycoside with no such ring and gives no satellites, and neither is autoclaved — both are filter sterilised into cooled medium.

卡那抗性在胞内修饰药物，培养基仍有选择性。

㉜ 灭菌的标准永远是杀死芽孢 sterilising lab equipment

The default is autoclaving with saturated steam at 121 degrees for fifteen to twenty minutes, a temperature set by bacterial endospores rather than vegetative cells, with dry heat for metal and glass that must stay dry. Heat-labile things go through a 0.22 micrometre filter, which removes bacteria rather than killing them.

胶带只证明它热过，要用芽孢条验证。

㉝ 危险化学品按控制层级答，PPE 排在最后 hierarchy of controls

I answer in order: substitution first, a SYBR-type stain instead of ethidium bromide; then engineering controls, a fume hood or shielding, which protect everyone without depending on memory; then administrative controls, reading the safety data sheet and segregating waste. PPE comes last, because it protects one person and fails silently.

洒了先提醒周围的人，再确认是什么。

陷阱问法

  • "Your reading is above the highest standard — what now?" — Dilute and read again; never extrapolate, because the detector saturates there. · 稀释重测，绝不外推。
  • "Is OD600 absorbance?" — No. Cells scatter light out of the beam, so there is no extinction coefficient. · 散射不是吸收。
  • "Can I just quote rpm?" — Not reproducibly; rcf depends on the rotor radius, so a protocol must say times g. · 写 × g。
  • "Which component actually creates the stacking effect?" — Glycine in the running buffer, not the gel: slow at pH 6.8, fast at pH 8.8. · 是甘氨酸。
  • "Why do satellite colonies appear only on ampicillin?" — Beta-lactamase is secreted and clears the drug around the colony. · 卡那是胞内修饰。
  • "Why does a T7 system use IPTG at all?" — The T7 polymerase gene itself sits under lac control in DE3, so the control is one layer removed. · 隔了一层。
  • "What can mass spectrometry tell you that sequencing cannot?" — Post-translational modifications, and whether the protein was actually made. · 翻译后修饰。
  • "Two antibodies against one epitope?" — They compete, so the signal falls instead of rising. · 必须认不同表位。
  • "Why is PPE last?" — It protects one person, depends on being done right every time, and fails silently. · 失效时没有提示。

定义

  • molarity — moles of solute per litre of solution · 摩尔浓度
  • buffer — a weak acid together with its conjugate base, resisting change in pH · 缓冲液
  • pKa — the pH at which a group is exactly half dissociated · 半解离时的 pH
  • absorbance — the logarithm of incident over transmitted light, and dimensionless · 吸光度
  • extinction coefficient — the absorbance of a one molar solution over a one centimetre path · 消光系数
  • accuracy — closeness to the true value · 准确度
  • precision — reproducibility between replicates · 精密度
  • rcf — the force on the sample as multiples of gravity, set by radius times speed squared · 相对离心力
  • isotachophoresis — concentrating a sample between a fast and a slow ion front · 等速电泳
  • chaotrope — a salt such as guanidine hydrochloride that disrupts hydrogen bonding and unfolds proteins · 离液剂
  • epitope — the part of an antigen that an antibody actually binds · 表位
  • loading control — a second, unchanging protein used to normalise lanes on a blot · 上样对照
  • endospore — the dormant bacterial form that sets the sterilisation standard · 细菌芽孢

Microbiology

微生物 32 题 · 答案都是你自己写过的，这里压成一句一条

  1. 细菌长什么样 — 一切从壁开始 肽聚糖＝N-乙酰葡糖胺和 N-乙酰胞壁酸交替成链，再由短肽交联；做交联的是转肽酶。 细菌相对环境是高渗的，水一直往里灌，壁是唯一挡住裂解的那一层。 革兰阳性：单层膜外一层厚肽聚糖，贯穿磷壁酸。革兰阴性：薄肽聚糖躲在周质里，外面还有一层脂多糖外膜。 ⚠ 别说革兰阴性「没有肽聚糖」——它有，只是薄。外膜那句后面接的是 lipid A ＝ 内毒素，他很容易追过去。 壁外面还有两样：荚膜（多糖，抗吞噬）和饿出来的芽孢（不是繁殖）。
  2. 怎么养 · 怎么繁殖 · 怎么算 繁殖只有一个词：二分裂。所以一个菌落 ＝ 一个细胞的克隆后代，划线分离才算得上纯化手段。 生长曲线是细胞数取对数对时间作图，四个期：延滞 · 对数 · 稳定 · 衰亡。 ⚠ 只有对数期里「生长速率」才是一个有意义的数，表达蛋白也在对数中期诱导。 量它靠 OD₆₀₀——但那是光散射，死活一起算，要区分就另做活菌计数。 代谢分类两条独立的轴交叉：碳源（自养／异养）× 能源（光养／化能养，化能养再分无机／有机），第三条轴是末端电子受体。
  3. 怎么认出是谁 — 表型一路走到序列 老路是生化试验组合：过氧化氢酶 · 氧化酶 · 凝固酶加糖发酵，读的是 pH 指示剂的颜色。 ⚠ 定菌的是一整组答案的模式，不是任何单独一个试验；而且前提是纯培养，混样会给你一个很自信的错答案。 新路是16S rRNA 基因测序：保守区让一对通用引物扒得住，可变区携带种的信号，不用培养。 ⚠ 测的是 16S 的基因（DNA），不是 rRNA 本身。 Carl Woese 当年就是拿这条分子比出了古菌和三域——今天鉴定还用 16S，用的就是他那套方法。
  4. 基因怎么在细菌之间传 — 进化不是一棵树 水平基因转移三条路：转化（感受态细胞吃裸 DNA）· 转导（噬菌体捎带宿主 DNA）· 接合（质粒经性菌毛直接过去）。 ⚠ 后果是一个基因可以一步跳进一个很远的谱系，所以基因组是镶嵌的：核心基因组 ＋ 附属基因组。 质粒是这件事的主要载具，它的定义性质是自带复制起点、独立复制。 噬菌体两条程序：裂解和溶原——溶原那条正是 DE3 的原理。
  5. 实验室里怎么用细菌 — 克隆 · 菌株 · 开关 克隆按「接口是怎么做出来的」分组，不是背一串名字：酶切–连接 · Golden Gate · Gibson，外加 TA／TOPO、Gateway、RF。 判据一句话：克隆株是 recA⁻ endA⁻（DH5α），表达株带 DE3 且缺蛋白酶（BL21(DE3)）。 ⚠ 菌株名里没有 (DE3)，pET 质粒什么都不会产——口试上这句比机制本身更得分。 细菌那一侧还有两个特有的零件：多顺反子 mRNA（每个基因自带 RBS，一个开关管一整套）和紧凑的启动子（−10／−35，σ 因子直接认）。 真核是扣帽扫描＋一整套通用转录因子，所以表达载体是分宿主的，两边不通用。
  6. 抗生素 · 耐药 · 群体行为 按靶点分五组，统一原理是选择性毒性：壁 · 核糖体 · 核酸 · 叶酸 · 膜。 她天天用的三个正好打三个靶：氨苄（转肽酶）· 卡那（30S）· 环丙沙星（DNA 旋转酶）。 ⚠ 抗生素不制造耐药，它筛选群体里本来就有的变异体。四种机制：毁药 · 修饰药 · 改靶点 · 挡在外面（外排泵）。 群体那一层：群体感应靠自诱导物＋阈值＋正反馈，所以是骤然开关；生物膜里的耐受是生理性的 tolerance，不是遗传性的 resistance。

Say this

Microbiology begins at the wall. Peptidoglycan, cross-linked by transpeptidases, holds the cell against turgor, and the Gram stain simply reads its thickness. A thick wall traps the crystal violet iodine complex through alcohol decolourisation and stays purple. A thin wall under a lipopolysaccharide outer membrane loses that complex and takes up safranin. The wall is also what ampicillin attacks; the other classes hit the ribosome, DNA gyrase (also called DNA gyrase, topoisomerase II (bacteria)) or folate synthesis, always by selective toxicity. Bacteria reproduce only by binary fission, passing through lag, exponential, stationary and death phases. Everything else — transformation, transduction, conjugation — is horizontal gene transfer, and that is why resistance spreads faster than mutation alone could explain. To name an organism I sequence the 16S ribosomal RNA gene, the molecule Carl Woese used to discover the Archaea.

约 45 秒 · 一根线：壁 → 染色 → 抗生素 → 怎么长 → 怎么传 → 怎么认

广度 · 32 题，一题一两句 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① 革兰阳性与阴性的差别就是壁，染色和临床都从它推出来 Gram-positive vs Gram-negative

A Gram-positive cell has thick peptidoglycan outside one membrane, with teichoic acids running through it. A Gram-negative cell has thin peptidoglycan in the periplasm, between an inner membrane and an outer membrane of lipopolysaccharide, which carries lipid A, the endotoxin.

先说壁，其余都是它的后果。

② 细胞壁是肽聚糖，它挡住的是膨压 peptidoglycan · murein

Peptidoglycan, also called murein, is chains of alternating N-acetylglucosamine and N-acetylmuramic acid, cross-linked by short peptides. Transpeptidases make those cross-links. A bacterium is hypertonic to its surroundings, so the wall is the only thing stopping lysis.

交联酶正是 β-内酰胺的靶。

③ 革兰染色四步，第三步才是真正干活的那一步 Gram stain

I flood a heat-fixed smear with crystal violet, add iodine as a mordant, decolourise briefly with alcohol, then counterstain with safranin. Decolourisation is the timed and critical step: the thick wall dehydrates and traps the complex, the thin wall cannot hold it.

脱色过头或培养物太老，阳性读成阴性。

④ 荚膜的意义主要是毒力：抗吞噬，而且把下面的抗原藏起来 ⭐ Hodek capsule

A capsule is an organised polysaccharide layer outside the wall, firmly attached unlike a slime layer. It resists phagocytosis and hides the surface antigens from antibodies. Conjugate vaccines link capsular polysaccharide to a carrier protein, turning a weak response into a lasting one.

Hodek 做抗体；纯多糖疫苗无记忆，所以要做成 conjugate。

⑤ 芽孢形成是对饥饿的存活反应，不是繁殖 endospore formation · sporulation

The cell divides asymmetrically into a forespore and a mother cell, which engulfs the forespore. A cortex and protein coat are laid down, the core dehydrates and accumulates calcium dipicolinate, and small acid-soluble proteins protect the DNA.

抗性来自结构，所以高压灭菌要 121 °C。

⑥ 生长曲线是细胞数的对数对时间作图，四个期 bacterial growth curve

In lag phase the cells make the enzymes and ribosomes the new medium needs. In exponential phase the doubling time is constant. In stationary phase division and death balance. Then the viable count falls in death phase.

表达蛋白在对数中期诱导。

⑦ 做生长曲线实验：定时取样、测 OD₆₀₀、对数作图 growth-curve experiment · OD600

I inoculate from a fresh overnight culture, incubate with shaking, and sample at fixed intervals, measuring optical density at 600 nanometres against sterile medium. Optical density measures light scattering, so a viable count by dilution and plating is added when live and dead must be separated.

出线性范围先稀释；对数期点要密，才拟合得出倍增时间。

⑧ 分离培养的目标是纯培养，而划线就是用接种环做的稀释 streak plating · pure culture

A colony is the clonal progeny of one colony-forming unit, so a single well-separated colony is the working definition of pure. I flame the loop between sectors, incubate, then re-streak one colony to confirm purity.

选择性培养基压住别的，鉴别性培养基让它们长得不一样。

⑨ 无菌操作同时朝两个方向保护，而污染在站稳之前看不见 aseptic technique

It keeps contaminants out of my culture and keeps my organism out of me. A faster-growing contaminant takes over, and every result afterwards concerns the wrong species. I work near a flame, flame the loop and the bottle neck, and incubate an uninoculated control plate.

对照板长了，实验作废，不是打折。

⑩ 繁殖只有二分裂，水平基因转移和产孢都不算 binary fission

The chromosome replicates from a single origin, the copies move to opposite poles, and a septum forms at mid-cell to give two identical daughters. A few species bud and filamentous bacteria fragment. Transformation, transduction, conjugation and sporulation do not raise the cell number.

「什么不是繁殖」那一句才是考官在等的。

⑪ 代谢按碳源和能源两条独立的轴分类，交叉出名字 types of bacterial metabolism

Autotrophs fix carbon dioxide, heterotrophs use organic carbon; phototrophs use light, chemotrophs chemical oxidation, splitting into lithotrophs and organotrophs. Cyanobacteria are photoautotrophs, nitrifying bacteria chemolithoautotrophs, and Escherichia coli a chemoorganoheterotroph.

第三条轴是末端电子受体：氧／硝酸盐、硫酸盐／发酵。

⑫ 用测序鉴定细菌：测 16S rRNA 基因，不用培养 16S ribosomal RNA gene

Every bacterium has the 16S ribosomal RNA gene, it is essential, and its conserved regions sit next to variable regions. One universal primer pair therefore amplifies it from an unknown organism, and the variable regions identify the species against a reference database.

优势是免培养，局限是常常只到属。

⑬ Woese 发现了古菌和三域，而他用的方法是比序列 Carl Woese · Archaea

He compared 16S ribosomal RNA sequences rather than morphology, and found that organisms then called bacteria fell into two deeply separate groups. Life was reorganised into Bacteria, Archaea and Eukarya, and molecular sequence became the basis of phylogeny.

方法那一半直接接回 ⑫。

⑭ 生化试验靠一整组答案的模式定菌，不是任何单独一个试验 biochemical tests

Each test asks whether one enzyme is present. Catalase separates staphylococci from streptococci, oxidase separates Pseudomonas from the Enterobacteriaceae, and coagulase separates Staphylococcus aureus. Sugar fermentation is read as a colour change of a pH indicator.

前提是纯培养；分辨率不如 16S。

⑮ PCR 是三个温度反复循环，引物划定产物两端 PCR

Denaturation near ninety-five degrees separates the strands, annealing lets two primers bind facing each other, and extension near seventy-two degrees lets a thermostable polymerase build the new strands. In microbiology the uses are diagnostic: 16S amplification, and quantitative PCR for viral load.

终点 PCR 只能说「有」，要说「多」得用 qPCR（= qPCR = real-time PCR (≠ RT-PCR)）。

⑯ 定 SARS-CoV-2 的谱系是一条测序流程，因为谱系由基因组序列定义 SARS-CoV-2 lineage

The genome is RNA, so I reverse transcribe it to cDNA, amplify the whole genome by tiled multiplex PCR, sequence the amplicons and assemble against the reference. The consensus sequence is then assigned a lineage from its mutations, using Pango nomenclature.

诊断用的 RT-qPCR 给不出 lineage。

⑰ 水平基因转移有三条路，后果是进化不是一棵树 horizontal gene transfer

Transformation is uptake of free DNA by a competent cell, transduction is transfer by a bacteriophage, and conjugation is direct plasmid transfer through a pilus. A gene can appear in a distant lineage in one step, so genomes are mosaic: a core genome plus an accessory genome.

最清楚的例子是耐药基因跨种。

⑱ 转化是细菌摄取裸 DNA，天然过程和台上操作要分开说 bacterial transformation

Naturally, a competent cell binds extracellular DNA, degrades one strand and imports the other, which homologous recombination integrates. At the bench I wash cells in ice-cold calcium chloride, heat-shock at forty-two degrees, recover in non-selective medium, then plate on selection.

电穿孔效率高两到三个数量级。

⑲ 转化效率 ＝ 每微克质粒 DNA 得到的菌落形成单位 transformation efficiency · CFU/µg

I transform a small known mass of a supercoiled control plasmid, because too much DNA saturates the cells and reads falsely low. I plate a known fraction of a known dilution, count, then divide by the fraction plated and by the micrograms of DNA.

两个对照：不加 DNA 的板 ＋ 已知对照质粒。

⑳ 质粒的定义性质是自带复制起点、独立于染色体复制 plasmid

It carries accessory genes not needed for growth: antibiotic resistance, virulence factors, extra metabolic capabilities, and the transfer functions of a conjugative plasmid. As a vector it needs an origin setting copy number, a selectable marker and a multiple cloning site.

同一个复制起点的两个质粒不相容。

㉑ 噬菌体是感染细菌的病毒，吸附的受体决定宿主范围 bacteriophage

The genome is injected while the capsid stays outside, which is the observation behind the Hershey and Chase experiment. In the lytic cycle the cell lyses and releases progeny; in the lysogenic cycle the genome integrates as a prophage until stress induces lysis.

溶原正是 DE3 的原理。

㉒ 克隆按「接口是怎么做出来的」分组，不是按名字 ⭐ Lipovová types of cloning

Restriction–ligation cuts compatible ends and seals them with a ligase. Golden Gate uses type IIS enzymes cutting outside their recognition site, so I choose the overhang. Gibson assembly needs no restriction enzyme: homologous overlapping ends anneal in one isothermal reaction.

再顶三个：TA／TOPO · Gateway · RF cloning。

㉓ 克隆菌株挑的是 DNA 稳定性和转化效率，不是表达 ⭐ Lipovová DH5α · XL10-Gold

DH5α is the workhorse: recA1 removes homologous recombination so repeats are not rearranged, and endA1 removes an endonuclease that would degrade plasmid during a miniprep. XL10-Gold or TOP10 strains are very highly competent and tolerate difficult DNA.

通则：克隆株 recA⁻ endA⁻，表达株带 DE3 且缺蛋白酶。

㉔ DE3 是染色体上的一个 λ 原噬菌体，带着 T7 RNA 聚合酶的基因 ⭐ Lipovová DE3 cassette

That gene sits under a lacUV5 promoter. My gene sits under a T7 promoter, which the host polymerase cannot read at all. IPTG relieves LacI repression, the cell makes T7 RNA polymerase, and only then is my gene transcribed.

菌株名里没有 (DE3)，pET 什么都不产。

㉕ 营养缺陷型不能自己合成某个必需化合物，所以培养基必须补 auxotrophy

The opposite is a prototroph, which grows on minimal medium. In the laboratory auxotrophy is a selectable marker needing no antibiotic: a strain deleted for LEU2 or URA3 grows only if a plasmid restores the gene. It is also the readout of the yeast two-hybrid assay.

酵母工作完全靠它。

㉖ 多顺反子 mRNA 是一条 mRNA 编码不止一个蛋白，也是操纵子的物理基础 polycistronic mRNA

Several genes with related functions share one promoter and one transcript, and each has its own ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence), so ribosomes initiate independently. One switch controls the whole set, as the lac operon does. Eukaryotic mRNA is monocistronic, because initiation works by cap binding and scanning.

所以细菌操纵子搬进真核细胞表达不出来。

㉗ 细菌启动子紧凑、被直接认出；真核启动子模块化、被间接认出 bacterial vs eukaryotic promoters

Two conserved elements near minus ten and minus thirty-five are read by the sigma factor of the holoenzyme, and swapping sigma factors switches whole gene sets. A eukaryotic core promoter such as a TATA box (also called Goldberg–Hogness box) needs general transcription factors to assemble a pre-initiation complex.

后果：表达载体分宿主，两边不通用。

㉘ 氨苄、卡那、环丙沙星打三个不同的靶，抗性每次都跟着靶点走 ampicillin · kanamycin · ciprofloxacin

Ampicillin, a beta-lactam, inhibits the transpeptidases cross-linking peptidoglycan, and beta-lactamase hydrolyses its ring. Kanamycin, an aminoglycoside, binds the 30S subunit and causes misreading; resistance phosphorylates the drug. Ciprofloxacin, a fluoroquinolone, inhibits DNA gyrase; resistance is a point mutation.

壁 · 核糖体 · DNA，三个都是杀菌性的。

㉙ 抗生素按靶点分五组，统一原理是选择性毒性 classes of antibiotics · selective toxicity

Cell wall synthesis is hit by beta-lactams and by vancomycin; protein synthesis on the 30S by aminoglycosides and tetracyclines, on the 50S by macrolides and chloramphenicol. Nucleic acid synthesis is hit by fluoroquinolones and rifampicin, folate synthesis by sulfonamides and trimethoprim, and the outer membrane by polymyxins.

每一类打的都是细菌有、我们没有的东西。

㉚ 抗生素不制造耐药，它筛选群体里本来就有的变异体 antibiotic resistance

Resistance is growth at a concentration that inhibits other members of the species. Four mechanisms: destroy the drug, modify it, alter the target, or keep it out by low permeability and efflux pumps. It arises by mutation or by horizontal gene transfer, and transfer dominates clinically.

外排泵常常一次覆盖好几种不相关的药。

㉛ 群体感应是依赖细胞密度的基因调控，靠阈值加正反馈 quorum sensing · autoinducer

Every cell constantly releases an autoinducer, whose outside concentration rises with the population. Above a threshold it binds a regulator and switches on a gene set, and the regulator also raises autoinducer synthesis, so the switch turns on sharply. Gram-negative bacteria signal with acyl-homoserine lactones, Gram-positive with peptides.

经典例子：费氏弧菌发光 · 毒力因子 · 生物膜 · 感受态。

㉜ 生物膜是附着在表面、包埋在自产基质里的群落，行为跟摇瓶里不一样 biofilm

The matrix is extracellular polymeric substances: polysaccharide, protein and extracellular DNA. Formation runs from reversible to irreversible attachment, then maturation and dispersal. The matrix limits penetration, deep cells grow slowly and escape drugs acting on growing cells, and some are dormant persisters.

导管、植入物、心脏瓣膜之所以难清。

陷阱问法

  • "Do Gram-negative bacteria have peptidoglycan?" — Yes, a thin layer in the periplasm; only the thickness differs. · 有，只是薄。
  • "Is sporulation a form of reproduction?" — No. One cell gives one spore, so the cell number does not rise. · 不是繁殖。
  • "Does OD600 count living cells?" — No, it measures light scattering, so live and dead cells are counted together. · 死活一起算。
  • "Do you sequence the 16S rRNA?" — I sequence the gene, which is DNA, not the ribosomal RNA itself. · 测的是基因。
  • "Does the antibiotic create the resistance?" — No, it selects variants already present in the population. · 只是筛选。
  • "Is ampicillin active on cells that are not dividing?" — No, it blocks building of new wall, it does not destroy existing wall. · 只杀正在长的。
  • "Is anaerobic respiration the same as fermentation?" — No, anaerobic respiration keeps an electron transport chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）; fermentation has none. · 两回事。
  • "Is biofilm resistance the same as antibiotic resistance?" — No, biofilm tolerance is physiological and reversible; resistance is genetic. · tolerance ≠ resistance。
  • "Which strain for a pET plasmid?" — One carrying DE3; without it there is no T7 RNA polymerase and nothing is made. · 没 DE3 不表达。

定义

  • peptidoglycan (murein) — the bacterial wall polymer, two alternating sugars cross-linked by short peptides · 肽聚糖
  • lipid A — the endotoxin part of Gram-negative lipopolysaccharide · 内毒素
  • colony-forming unit — one cell able to give rise to one colony · 菌落形成单位
  • transformation efficiency — colony-forming units obtained per microgram of plasmid DNA · 转化效率
  • competent cell — a cell in a state able to take up free DNA · 感受态细胞
  • auxotroph — an organism unable to make a compound it needs, so the medium must supply it · 营养缺陷型
  • prototroph — an organism that grows on minimal medium · 原养型
  • polycistronic mRNA — one messenger RNA encoding more than one protein · 多顺反子 mRNA
  • prophage — a phage genome integrated into the host chromosome · 前噬菌体
  • conjugative plasmid — a plasmid carrying the genes for its own transfer through a pilus · 接合型质粒
  • selective medium — medium that suppresses everything except the target organism · 选择性培养基
  • differential medium — medium on which several organisms grow but look different · 鉴别性培养基
  • autoinducer — the small diffusible signal molecule of quorum sensing · 自诱导物
  • persister — a dormant cell that survives a drug without being genetically resistant · 休眠存活细胞
  • selective toxicity — hitting a target bacteria have and human cells do not · 选择性毒性

⚠ 这一页只帮你想起「有哪几件事」。「为什么」在卡上—— 它替代的是「卡在第三个想不起第四个」那种空白，不是理解。
⚠ 临床那几个病名都是 general，你的教材不是医学教材，多半不问； 放这儿只因为成本低，万一问到就是白拿。
